


CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[SP_NEW_REPORT]
@Status varchar(500)='',
@Store_Id int =0,
@WH_ID INT='',
@User_Type NVARCHAR(50)='',
@User_ID int=0,
@CI_STATUS Nvarchar(10)='', ---0-UNPROCESSED_HU;  1-PROCESSED_HU;
@SearchTerm VARCHAR(100) = '',
@fromdate DATE ='',
@todate DATE ='',
@Reciving_Plant NVARCHAR(20)='',
@HUCOUNT INT=0 OUTPUT,
@MATERIALCOUNT  INT=0 OUTPUT,
@ACTUALQTY INT=0 OUTPUT,
@SCANQTY INT=0 OUTPUT,
@TAGQTY INT=0 OUTPUT,
@PageIndex INT =1,
@PageSize INT = 10,
@HU_NO NVARCHAR(50)='',
@RecordCount INT=0 OUTPUT,
@ref_No nvarchar (50)='',
@Store_code NVARCHAR(20)='',
@Vendor_Code NVARCHAR(50)='',
@Material NVARCHAR(50)='',
@TotalCount INT=0 OUTPUT,
@EAN NVARCHAR(20)='',
@COUNTER_NO NVARCHAR (50)='',
@BILL_DATE DATE='',
@QTY INT=0 OUTPUT,
@Ttl_Act_QTY INT=0 OUTPUT,
@Sum_Scanned_Qty INT=0 OUTPUT,
@Excess_Qty INT=0 OUTPUT,
@ENCQTY INT=0 OUTPUT,
@DIFFQTY INT=0 OUTPUT,
@GRC_STATUS CHAR='' ,---0-FAIL;  1-PASS;
@SortColumn NVARCHAR(100)='',
@SortDirection NVARCHAR(10)='',
@SortType NVARCHAR(20) = 'string',
@SQL NVARCHAR(MAX)='',
@VEHICLE_NO NVARCHAR(50)='',
@8TO9 INT = 0 OUTPUT,@9TO10 INT = 0 OUTPUT,@10TO11 INT = 0 OUTPUT,@11TO12 INT = 0 OUTPUT,@12TO13 INT = 0 OUTPUT,@13TO14 INT = 0 OUTPUT,@14TO15 INT = 0 OUTPUT,
@15TO16 INT = 0 OUTPUT,@16TO17 INT = 0 OUTPUT, @17TO18 INT = 0 OUTPUT,@18TO19 INT = 0 OUTPUT,@19TO20 INT = 0 OUTPUT,
@8TO9_ERR INT = 0 OUTPUT,@9TO10_ERR INT = 0 OUTPUT,@10TO11_ERR INT = 0 OUTPUT,@11TO12_ERR INT = 0 OUTPUT,@12TO13_ERR INT = 0 OUTPUT,@13TO14_ERR INT = 0 OUTPUT,@14TO15_ERR INT = 0 OUTPUT,
@15TO16_ERR INT = 0 OUTPUT,@16TO17_ERR INT = 0 OUTPUT, @17TO18_ERR INT = 0 OUTPUT,@18TO19_ERR INT = 0 OUTPUT,@19TO20_ERR INT = 0 OUTPUT,
@MRGQTY INT= 0 OUTPUT,@EVNQTY INT= 0 OUTPUT,@T_ENC_QTY INT= 0 OUTPUT,@T_ENC_USERS INT= 0 OUTPUT,
@LOCATION NVARCHAR(50)='',@AVGQTY INT= 0 OUTPUT,@ERRQTY INT= 0 OUTPUT,@ERROR_MSG NVARCHAR(50)='',@STORECOUNT INT=0 OUTPUT,@WHCOUNT INT =0 OUTPUT
--,@CYCLE_COUNT INT=0 OUTPUT,@GRC_DONE INT=0 OUTPUT,@GRC_INCOMPLETE INT=0 OUTPUT,@HU_VALIDATION INT=0 OUTPUT,@POS_CHECKOUT INT=0 OUTPUT,@STOCK_TAKE INT=0 OUTPUT,
--@STORE_ENCODEING INT=0 OUTPUT,@WAREHOUSE_ENCODEING INT=0 OUTPUT,@WAREHOUSE_INWARD INT=0 OUTPUT
AS
BEGIN

	DECLARE @StartRow INT = (@PageIndex - 1) * @PageSize + 1;
	DECLARE @EndRow   INT =  @PageIndex * @PageSize;
	DECLARE @OrderBy NVARCHAR(200);

    SELECT @Store_ID = dbo.User_Registration.Store_ID,@WH_ID = dbo.User_Registration.WH_ID,@User_Type=dbo.User_Registration.User_Type 
    FROM dbo.User_Registration 
    LEFT JOIN dbo.tbl_Store_Master  ON dbo.User_Registration.Store_ID = dbo.tbl_Store_Master.Store_ID
    LEFT JOIN dbo.tbl_Warehouse_Mst ON dbo.User_Registration.WH_ID = dbo.tbl_Warehouse_Mst.WH_ID
    WHERE dbo.User_Registration.User_ID = @User_ID;

	----------------HU(HANDLE UNIT REPORT)----------------------------------
	IF(@Status='BIND_STORE_FOR_ALL')
	BEGIN
		SELECT DISTINCT S.Store_Code as 'STORE_CODE',S.Store_Name AS 'STORE_NAME' FROM dbo.tbl_Store_Master S JOIN dbo.User_Registration U ON S.Store_ID = U.Store_ID
		WHERE U.User_ID = @USER_ID  OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM dbo.User_Registration WHERE User_ID = @USER_ID AND User_Type IN ('Super Admin','Warehouse Admin'))
		ORDER BY S.Store_Code DESC;
	END
    ELSE IF(@status='BIND_HU_NUMBER')
    BEGIN  
		SELECT DISTINCT TOP 10 dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].HU_No AS 'HU_NUMBER' FROM dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl] 
		WHERE dbo.tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl.Is_Complete_Status_Flg=@CI_STATUS
		AND dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].Ref_No IS NOT NULL
        AND dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].Ref_No <>''
		AND dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].Reciving_Plant=COALESCE(NULLIF(@Reciving_Plant, ''),dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].Reciving_Plant)
		AND CONVERT(DATE,dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].Sending_HU_Scan_Dt) between  
		CONVERT(date, COALESCE(NULLIF(@fromdate, ''),dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].Sending_HU_Scan_Dt)) 
		AND CONVERT(date, COALESCE(NULLIF(@todate, ''),dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].Sending_HU_Scan_Dt)) 
		GROUP BY dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].HU_No ORDER BY dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].HU_No
	END

    ELSE If(@status='SEARCH_BIND_HU_NUMBER')
    BEGIN  
		SELECT DISTINCT HU_No AS 'HU_NUMBER' FROM dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl] 
		WHERE dbo.tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl.Is_Complete_Status_Flg=@CI_STATUS
		AND dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].Ref_No IS NOT NULL
        AND dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].Ref_No <>''
		AND dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].Reciving_Plant=COALESCE(NULLIF(@Reciving_Plant, ''),dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].Reciving_Plant)
		AND CONVERT(DATE,dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].Sending_HU_Scan_Dt) between  
			CONVERT(date, COALESCE(NULLIF(@fromdate, ''),dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].Sending_HU_Scan_Dt)) 
			AND CONVERT(date, COALESCE(NULLIF(@todate, ''),dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].Sending_HU_Scan_Dt)) 
		AND (@SearchTerm = '' OR dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].HU_No LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%')
		GROUP BY dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].HU_No ORDER BY dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].HU_No;
	END

	ELSE If(@status='HU_REPORT')
    BEGIN
	IF(@CI_STATUS='1')
		BEGIN
			SELECT --ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].HU_No desc) AS RowNumber,
			dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].Sending_Plant AS 'Sending_Plant', 
	        dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].Reciving_Plant AS 'Receiving_Plant', 
			dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].HU_No AS 'HU_Number',
			dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].Ref_No AS 'Ref_No',
			COUNT(distinct dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].Main_Article) AS 'MATERIAL_COUNT',
			CONVERT(DATE,dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].Sending_HU_Scan_Dt) AS 'Scan_Date',
	        CAST(SUM(dbo.tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl.Act_Qty) AS INT) as 'Act_Qty',
			CAST (SUM(dbo.tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl.Scan_Qty)AS INT) as 'Scan_Qty',
            0 as Invalid_Tag,'PASS' as 'Status',
			ISNULL(dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].HU_Created_By,'NA') AS 'HU_Created_By',
			ISNULL(dbo.[User_Registration].User_Name,'NA') AS 'HU_Validation_By'
			INTO #PROCESSEDHU
			FROM dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl]
			LEFT JOIN dbo.[User_Registration] ON dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].HU_Validation_By=dbo.[User_Registration].User_ID
			wHERE tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl.Is_Complete_Status_Flg='1'
		    AND dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].Ref_No IS NOT NULL
            AND dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].Ref_No <>''
			AND dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].Reciving_Plant=COALESCE(NULLIF(@Reciving_Plant, ''),dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].Reciving_Plant)
			AND CONVERT(DATE,dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].Sending_HU_Scan_Dt) between  
			CONVERT(date, COALESCE(NULLIF(@fromdate, ''),dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].Sending_HU_Scan_Dt)) 
			AND CONVERT(date, COALESCE(NULLIF(@todate, ''),dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].Sending_HU_Scan_Dt))
			AND dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].HU_No = COALESCE(NULLIF(@HU_NO, ''),dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].HU_No)
			AND (dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].Sending_Plant LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%'
				OR dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].Reciving_Plant LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%'
				OR dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].HU_No LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%'
				OR dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].Ref_No LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%')
		   Group by dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].Sending_Plant,dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].Reciving_Plant,dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].HU_No,
		   CONVERT(DATE,dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].Sending_HU_Scan_Dt),dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].Is_Complete_Status_Flg,dbo.tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl.Ref_No,
		   dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].HU_Created_By,dbo.[User_Registration].User_Name;


		   	SET @SortDirection =
				CASE 
					WHEN LOWER(@SortDirection) = 'desc' THEN 'DESC'
					ELSE 'ASC'
				END;

			SET @OrderBy =	
				CASE @SortColumn
					WHEN 'Sending_Plant' THEN 'R.Sending_Plant' 
					WHEN 'Receiving_Plant' THEN 'R.Receiving_Plant'
					--WHEN 'HU_Number' THEN 'R.HU_Number' 
					WHEN 'Ref_No' THEN 'R.Ref_No'
					WHEN 'MATERIAL_COUNT' THEN 'R.MATERIAL_COUNT'
					WHEN 'Scan_Date' THEN 'R.Scan_Date'
					WHEN 'Act_Qty' THEN 'R.Act_Qty'
					WHEN 'Scan_Qty' THEN 'R.Scan_Qty'
					WHEN 'Status' THEN 'R.Status'
					WHEN 'HU_Created_By' THEN 'R.HU_Created_By'
					WHEN 'HU_Validation_By' THEN 'R.HU_Validation_By'
					ELSE 'R.HU_Number'
				END
				+ ' ' + @SortDirection
				+ ', R.HU_Number ASC';
			
           	SET @SQL = N'SELECT *FROM (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY ' + @OrderBy + N') AS RowNumber,* FROM #PROCESSEDHU R ) T
			WHERE RowNumber BETWEEN @StartRow AND @EndRow
			ORDER BY RowNumber;';

			EXEC sp_executesql
			@SQL,N'@StartRow INT, @EndRow INT',
			@StartRow = @StartRow,	@EndRow   = @EndRow;
	
			-- Get the total record count
			SELECT @HUCOUNT = COUNT(DISTINCT HU_Number) FROM #PROCESSEDHU;
			SELECT @MATERIALCOUNT = SUM(MATERIAL_COUNT) FROM #PROCESSEDHU;
			SELECT @ACTUALQTY = SUM(Act_Qty) FROM #PROCESSEDHU;
			SELECT @SCANQTY = SUM(Scan_Qty) FROM #PROCESSEDHU;
			SELECT @TAGQTY = SUM(Invalid_Tag) FROM #PROCESSEDHU;
			SELECT @RecordCount = COUNT(*) FROM #PROCESSEDHU;
	

			-- --Retrieve the paginated results
			--SELECT * FROM #PROCESSEDHU WHERE RowNumber BETWEEN (@PageIndex - 1) * @PageSize + 1 AND @PageIndex * @PageSize
			--ORDER BY #PROCESSEDHU.HU_Number,#PROCESSEDHU.Status desc
			 
			 
			-- Clean up the temporary table
			DROP TABLE #PROCESSEDHU;
		END
		ELSE
		BEGIN
			SELECT --ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].HU_No desc) AS RowNumber,
			dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].Sending_Plant AS 'Sending_Plant', 
	        dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].Reciving_Plant AS 'Receiving_Plant', dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].HU_No AS 'HU_Number',dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].Ref_No AS 'Ref_No',
			COUNT(distinct dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].Main_Article) AS 'MATERIAL_COUNT',CONVERT(DATE,dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].Sending_HU_Scan_Dt) AS 'Scan_Date',
	        CAST(SUM(dbo.tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl.Act_Qty) AS INT) as 'Act_Qty',CAST (SUM(dbo.tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl.Scan_Qty)AS INT) as 'Scan_Qty',
            (Select Count(*) from tbl_DC_Outward_EPC_Dtl where HUNO=HU_No and Is_Status='0' ) as 'Invalid_Tag','FAIL' as 'Status',
			ISNULL(dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].HU_Created_By,'NA') AS 'HU_Created_By',
			ISNULL(dbo.[User_Registration].User_Name,'NA') AS 'HU_Validation_By'
			INTO #UNPROCESSEDHU
			FROM dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl] 
			LEFT JOIN dbo.[User_Registration] ON dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].HU_Validation_By=dbo.[User_Registration].User_ID
			wHERE tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl.Is_Complete_Status_Flg='0'
			AND dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].Ref_No IS NOT NULL
            AND dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].Ref_No <>''
			AND dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].Reciving_Plant=COALESCE(NULLIF(@Reciving_Plant, ''),dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].Reciving_Plant)
			AND CONVERT(DATE,dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].Sending_HU_Scan_Dt) between  
			CONVERT(date, COALESCE(NULLIF(@fromdate, ''),dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].Sending_HU_Scan_Dt)) 
			AND CONVERT(date, COALESCE(NULLIF(@todate, ''),dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].Sending_HU_Scan_Dt))
			AND dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].HU_No = COALESCE(NULLIF(@HU_NO, ''),dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].HU_No)
			AND (dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].Sending_Plant LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%'
				OR dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].Reciving_Plant LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%'
				OR dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].HU_No LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%'
				OR dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].Ref_No LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%')
		   Group by dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].Sending_Plant,dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].Reciving_Plant,dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].HU_No,
		   CONVERT(DATE,dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].Sending_HU_Scan_Dt),dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].Is_Complete_Status_Flg,dbo.tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl.Ref_No,
		   dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].HU_Created_By,dbo.[User_Registration].User_Name;

			-- Get the total record count
			SELECT @HUCOUNT = COUNT(DISTINCT HU_Number) FROM #UNPROCESSEDHU;
			SELECT @MATERIALCOUNT = SUM(MATERIAL_COUNT) FROM #UNPROCESSEDHU;
			SELECT @ACTUALQTY = SUM(Act_Qty) FROM #UNPROCESSEDHU;
			SELECT @SCANQTY = SUM(Scan_Qty) FROM #UNPROCESSEDHU;
			SELECT @TAGQTY = SUM(Invalid_Tag) FROM #UNPROCESSEDHU;
			SELECT @RecordCount = COUNT(*) FROM #UNPROCESSEDHU;

			  	SET @SortDirection =
				CASE 
					WHEN LOWER(@SortDirection) = 'desc' THEN 'DESC'
					ELSE 'ASC'
				END;

			SET @OrderBy =	
				CASE @SortColumn
					WHEN 'Sending_Plant' THEN 'R.Sending_Plant' 
					WHEN 'Receiving_Plant' THEN 'R.Receiving_Plant'
					--WHEN 'HU_Number' THEN 'R.HU_Number' 
					WHEN 'Ref_No' THEN 'R.Ref_No'
					WHEN 'MATERIAL_COUNT' THEN 'R.MATERIAL_COUNT'
					WHEN 'Scan_Date' THEN 'R.Scan_Date'
					WHEN 'Status' THEN 'R.Status'
					WHEN 'HU_Created_By' THEN 'R.HU_Created_By'
					WHEN 'HU_Validation_By' THEN 'R.HU_Validation_By'
					WHEN 'Act_Qty' THEN 'R.Act_Qty'
					WHEN 'Scan_Qty' THEN 'R.Scan_Qty'
					ELSE 'R.HU_Number'
				END
				+ ' ' + @SortDirection
				+ ', R.HU_Number ASC';

			SET @SQL = N'SELECT *FROM (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY ' + @OrderBy + N') AS RowNumber,*
			FROM #UNPROCESSEDHU R ) T
			WHERE RowNumber BETWEEN @StartRow AND @EndRow
			ORDER BY RowNumber;';

			EXEC sp_executesql	@SQL,N'@StartRow INT, @EndRow INT',
			@StartRow = @StartRow,	@EndRow   = @EndRow;
	

			-- --Retrieve the paginated results
			--SELECT * FROM #UNPROCESSEDHU WHERE RowNumber BETWEEN (@PageIndex - 1) * @PageSize + 1 AND @PageIndex * @PageSize
			--ORDER BY #UNPROCESSEDHU.HU_Number,#UNPROCESSEDHU.Status desc
			 

			-- Clean up the temporary table
			DROP TABLE #UNPROCESSEDHU;
		END
	END

	ELSE If(@status='EXPORT_HU_REPORT')
    BEGIN
		IF(@CI_STATUS='1')
		BEGIN
			SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].HU_No desc) AS RowNumber,dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].Sending_Plant AS 'Sending_Plant', 
	        dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].Reciving_Plant AS 'Receiving_Plant', dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].HU_No AS 'HU_Number',dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].Ref_No AS 'Ref_No',
			COUNT(distinct dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].Main_Article) AS 'MATERIAL_COUNT',CONVERT(DATE,dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].Sending_HU_Scan_Dt) AS 'Scan_Date',
	        CAST(SUM(dbo.tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl.Act_Qty) AS INT) as 'Act_Qty',CAST (SUM(dbo.tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl.Scan_Qty)AS INT) as 'Scan_Qty',
            0 as Invalid_Tag,'PASS' as 'Status',
			ISNULL(dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].HU_Created_By,'NA') AS 'HU_Created_By',
			ISNULL(dbo.[User_Registration].User_Name,'NA') AS 'HU_Validation_By'
	        FROM dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl] 
			LEFT JOIN dbo.[User_Registration] ON dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].HU_Validation_By=dbo.[User_Registration].User_ID
			wHERE tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl.Is_Complete_Status_Flg='1'
			AND dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].Ref_No IS NOT NULL
            AND dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].Ref_No <>''
			AND dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].Reciving_Plant=COALESCE(NULLIF(@Reciving_Plant, ''),dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].Reciving_Plant)
			AND CONVERT(DATE,dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].Sending_HU_Scan_Dt) between  
			CONVERT(date, COALESCE(NULLIF(@fromdate, ''),dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].Sending_HU_Scan_Dt)) 
			AND CONVERT(date, COALESCE(NULLIF(@todate, ''),dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].Sending_HU_Scan_Dt))
			AND dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].HU_No = COALESCE(NULLIF(@HU_NO, ''),dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].HU_No)
			AND (dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].Sending_Plant LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%'
				OR dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].Reciving_Plant LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%'
				OR dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].HU_No LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%')
		   Group by dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].Sending_Plant,dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].Reciving_Plant,dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].HU_No,
		   CONVERT(DATE,dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].Sending_HU_Scan_Dt),dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].Is_Complete_Status_Flg,dbo.tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl.Ref_No,
		   dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].HU_Created_By,dbo.[User_Registration].User_Name
		END
		ELSE
		BEGIN
			SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].HU_No desc) AS RowNumber,dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].Sending_Plant AS 'Sending_Plant', 
	        dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].Reciving_Plant AS 'Receiving_Plant', dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].HU_No AS 'HU_Number',dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].Ref_No AS 'Ref_No',
			COUNT(distinct dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].Main_Article) AS 'MATERIAL_COUNT',CONVERT(DATE,dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].Sending_HU_Scan_Dt) AS 'Scan_Date',
	        CAST(SUM(dbo.tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl.Act_Qty) AS INT) as 'Act_Qty',CAST (SUM(dbo.tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl.Scan_Qty)AS INT) as 'Scan_Qty',
            (Select Count(*) from tbl_DC_Outward_EPC_Dtl where HUNO=HU_No and Is_Status='0' ) as 'Invalid_Tag','FAIL' as 'Status',
			ISNULL(dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].HU_Created_By,'NA') AS 'HU_Created_By',
			ISNULL(dbo.[User_Registration].User_Name,'NA') AS 'HU_Validation_By'
		    FROM dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl]
			LEFT JOIN dbo.[User_Registration] ON dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].HU_Validation_By=dbo.[User_Registration].User_ID
			wHERE tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl.Is_Complete_Status_Flg='0'
			AND dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].Ref_No IS NOT NULL
            AND dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].Ref_No <>''
			AND dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].Reciving_Plant=COALESCE(NULLIF(@Reciving_Plant, ''),dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].Reciving_Plant)
			AND CONVERT(DATE,dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].Sending_HU_Scan_Dt) between  
			CONVERT(date, COALESCE(NULLIF(@fromdate, ''),dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].Sending_HU_Scan_Dt)) 
			AND CONVERT(date, COALESCE(NULLIF(@todate, ''),dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].Sending_HU_Scan_Dt))
			AND dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].HU_No = COALESCE(NULLIF(@HU_NO, ''),dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].HU_No)
			AND (dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].Sending_Plant LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%'
				OR dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].Reciving_Plant LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%'
				OR dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].HU_No LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%')
		   Group by dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].Sending_Plant,dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].Reciving_Plant,dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].HU_No,
		   CONVERT(DATE,dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].Sending_HU_Scan_Dt),dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].Is_Complete_Status_Flg,dbo.tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl.Ref_No,
		   dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].HU_Created_By,dbo.[User_Registration].User_Name
		 END
     END

	 ELSE If(@status='HU_REPORT_VIEW')
	 BEGIN   
		SELECT  --ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY CONVERT (VARCHAR(19),dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].Sending_HU_Scan_Dt,120) DESC) AS RowNumber,
		dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].Sending_Plant AS 'Sending_Plant',
		dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].Reciving_Plant AS 'Receiving_Plant',
		dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].HU_No AS 'HU_Number',
		dbo.[tbl_DC_Outward_EPC_Dtl].Ref_No,
		dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].Main_Article AS 'Material',
		dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].Article_Code AS 'Material_Code',
		CAST (dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].Act_Qty AS DECIMAL(10,0)) AS 'Actual_Qty',
		(Select Count(EPC) from tbl_DC_Outward_EPC_Dtl Where DC_Outward_ID=dbo.tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl.DC_Outward_ID 
			and dbo.tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl.HU_No=@HU_NO and Ref_No=@ref_No) as 'Scan_Qty',
		CASE WHEN CAST (dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].Act_Qty AS DECIMAL(10,0)) =
		(Select Count(EPC) from tbl_DC_Outward_EPC_Dtl Where DC_Outward_ID=dbo.tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl.DC_Outward_ID and dbo.tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl.HU_No=@HU_NO and Ref_No=@ref_No)
		THEN 'COMPLETED' ELSE 'INCOMPLETED' END AS 'STATUS',
		CONVERT (VARCHAR(19),dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].Sending_HU_Scan_Dt,120) AS 'Scan_Date'
		INTO #HUREPORTVIEW
		FROM  dbo.tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl LEFT OUTER JOIN dbo.tbl_DC_Outward_EPC_Dtl ON dbo.tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl.HU_No = dbo.tbl_DC_Outward_EPC_Dtl.HUNO
		WHERE dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].HU_No = COALESCE(NULLIF(@HU_NO, ''),dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].HU_No)
		AND dbo.[tbl_DC_Outward_EPC_Dtl].Ref_No = COALESCE(NULLIF(@Ref_No, ''),dbo.[tbl_DC_Outward_EPC_Dtl].Ref_No)
		AND
			(dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].Sending_Plant LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%'
			OR dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].Reciving_Plant LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%'
			OR dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].HU_No LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%'
			OR dbo.[tbl_DC_Outward_EPC_Dtl].Ref_No LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%'
			OR dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].Main_Article LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%'
			OR dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].Article_Code LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%')

		Group by dbo.tbl_DC_Outward_EPC_Dtl.Ref_No,dbo.tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl.Sending_Plant, 
		dbo.tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl.Reciving_Plant, dbo.tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl.HU_No, 
		dbo.tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl.Main_Article, dbo.tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl.Article_Code, 
		dbo.tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl.Act_Qty,dbo.tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl.DC_Outward_ID,
		CONVERT (VARCHAR(19),dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].Sending_HU_Scan_Dt,120);

		-- Get the total record count
		SELECT @RecordCount = COUNT(*) FROM #HUREPORTVIEW;
		SELECT @ACTUALQTY = SUM(Actual_Qty) FROM #HUREPORTVIEW;
        SELECT @SCANQTY = SUM(Scan_Qty) FROM #HUREPORTVIEW;
			 
			 
		   	SET @SortDirection =
				CASE 
					WHEN LOWER(@SortDirection) = 'desc' THEN 'DESC'
					ELSE 'ASC'
				END;

			SET @OrderBy =	
				CASE @SortColumn
					WHEN 'Sending_Plant' THEN 'R.Sending_Plant' 
					WHEN 'Receiving_Plant' THEN 'R.Receiving_Plant'
					--WHEN 'HU_Number' THEN 'R.HU_Number' 
					WHEN 'Ref_No' THEN 'R.Ref_No'
					WHEN 'Material' THEN 'R.Material'
					WHEN 'Material_Code' THEN 'R.Material_Code'
					WHEN 'Scan_Date' THEN 'R.Scan_Date'
					WHEN 'Actual_Qty' THEN 'R.Actual_Qty'
					WHEN 'Scan_Qty' THEN 'R.Scan_Qty'
					WHEN 'STATUS' THEN 'R.STATUS'			
					ELSE 'R.HU_Number'
				END
				+ ' ' + @SortDirection
				+ ', R.HU_Number ASC';
			
           	SET @SQL = N'SELECT *FROM (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY ' + @OrderBy + N') AS RowNumber,* FROM #HUREPORTVIEW R ) T
			WHERE RowNumber BETWEEN @StartRow AND @EndRow
			ORDER BY RowNumber;';

			EXEC sp_executesql
			@SQL,N'@StartRow INT, @EndRow INT',
			@StartRow = @StartRow,	@EndRow   = @EndRow;
	
		---- Retrieve the paginated results
		--	SELECT * FROM #HUREPORTVIEW WHERE RowNumber BETWEEN (@PageIndex - 1) * @PageSize + 1 AND @PageIndex * @PageSize
		--	ORDER BY [#HUREPORTVIEW].[Scan_Date],STATUS DESC;

		-- Clean up the temporary table
		DROP TABLE #HUREPORTVIEW;
     END

     ELSE If(@status='EXPORT_HU_REPORT_VIEW')
     BEGIN   
		SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY (SELECT 1) ASC) AS RowNumber,
		dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].Sending_Plant AS 'Sending_Plant',
		dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].Reciving_Plant AS 'Receiving_Plant',
		dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].HU_No AS 'HU_Number',
		dbo.[tbl_DC_Outward_EPC_Dtl].Ref_No,
		dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].Main_Article AS 'Prepack_Article_No',
		dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].Article_Code AS 'Variant_Article_No',
		CAST (dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].Act_Qty AS DECIMAL(10,0)) AS 'Actual_Qty',
		(Select Count(EPC) from tbl_DC_Outward_EPC_Dtl Where DC_Outward_ID=dbo.tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl.DC_Outward_ID 
			and dbo.tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl.HU_No=@HU_NO and Ref_No=@ref_No) as 'Scan_Qty',
		CASE WHEN CAST (dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].Act_Qty AS DECIMAL(10,0)) =
		(Select Count(EPC) from tbl_DC_Outward_EPC_Dtl Where DC_Outward_ID=dbo.tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl.DC_Outward_ID and dbo.tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl.HU_No=@HU_NO and Ref_No=@ref_No)
		THEN 'COMPLETED' ELSE 'INCOMPLETED' END AS 'STATUS',
		CONVERT (VARCHAR(19),dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].Sending_HU_Scan_Dt,120) AS 'Scan_Date'
		FROM  dbo.tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl LEFT OUTER JOIN dbo.tbl_DC_Outward_EPC_Dtl ON dbo.tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl.HU_No = dbo.tbl_DC_Outward_EPC_Dtl.HUNO
		WHERE dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].HU_No = COALESCE(NULLIF(@HU_NO, ''),dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].HU_No)
		AND dbo.[tbl_DC_Outward_EPC_Dtl].Ref_No = COALESCE(NULLIF(@Ref_No, ''),dbo.[tbl_DC_Outward_EPC_Dtl].Ref_No)
		Group by dbo.tbl_DC_Outward_EPC_Dtl.Ref_No,dbo.tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl.Sending_Plant, 
		dbo.tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl.Reciving_Plant, dbo.tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl.HU_No, 
		dbo.tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl.Main_Article, dbo.tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl.Article_Code, 
		dbo.tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl.Act_Qty,dbo.tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl.DC_Outward_ID,
		CONVERT (VARCHAR(19),dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].Sending_HU_Scan_Dt,120)
		ORDER BY CONVERT (VARCHAR(19),dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl].Sending_HU_Scan_Dt,120) desc;
    END

	-------------------FOR ENCOADING STORE REPORT---ARTICLE QTY---------------------------------------

	ELSE If(@status='BIND_MATERIAL_FOR_ENCODING_STORE')
    BEGIN
		SELECT DISTINCT top 10 dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].Material AS ARTICLE FROM 
		dbo.tbl_DC_Outward_EPC_Dtl 
		JOIN dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl] ON dbo.tbl_DC_Outward_EPC_Dtl.EPC = dbo.tbl_Encoding_Dtl.Encode_EPC 
		LEFT JOIN dbo.[tbl_Ean_Mat_Mst] ON dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].ean = dbo.[tbl_Ean_Mat_Mst].EAN_UPC
		LEFT JOIN dbo.[tbl_Material_Master] ON dbo.[tbl_Ean_Mat_Mst].Material = dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].Material
		LEFT JOIN dbo.[tbl_Store_Master]  ON dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Store_ID = dbo.[tbl_Store_Master].Store_ID
		WHERE dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].WH_ID IS NOT NULL AND dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Store_ID IS NOT NULL
		AND dbo.[tbl_Store_Master].Store_Code =COALESCE(NULLIF(@Store_code, ''),dbo.[tbl_Store_Master].Store_Code)
		AND dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].Material is not null
		AND (dbo.tbl_DC_Outward_EPC_Dtl.Ref_No IN (SELECT DISTINCT Ref_No FROM dbo.tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl AS tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl 
		WHERE --(FORMAT(Sending_HU_Scan_Dt, 'yyyy-MM-dd') BETWEEN @fromdate AND @Todate)))
		(@fromdate ='' OR Sending_HU_Scan_Dt >= @fromdate) AND (@Todate ='' OR Sending_HU_Scan_Dt < DATEADD(DAY, 1,@todate))))
		AND(@SearchTerm = '' OR dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].Material LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%');
	END

	ELSE If(@status='SEARCH_BIND_MATERIAL_FOR_ENCODING_STORE')
	BEGIN
		SELECT DISTINCT dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].Material AS ARTICLE FROM 
		dbo.tbl_DC_Outward_EPC_Dtl 
		JOIN dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl] ON dbo.tbl_DC_Outward_EPC_Dtl.EPC = dbo.tbl_Encoding_Dtl.Encode_EPC 
		LEFT JOIN dbo.[tbl_Ean_Mat_Mst] ON dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].ean = dbo.[tbl_Ean_Mat_Mst].EAN_UPC
		LEFT JOIN dbo.[tbl_Material_Master] ON dbo.[tbl_Ean_Mat_Mst].Material = dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].Material
		LEFT JOIN dbo.[tbl_Store_Master]  ON dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Store_ID = dbo.[tbl_Store_Master].Store_ID
		WHERE dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].WH_ID IS NOT NULL AND dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Store_ID IS NOT NULL
		AND dbo.[tbl_Store_Master].Store_Code =COALESCE(NULLIF(@Store_code, ''),dbo.[tbl_Store_Master].Store_Code)
		AND dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].Material is not null
		AND (dbo.tbl_DC_Outward_EPC_Dtl.Ref_No IN (SELECT DISTINCT Ref_No FROM dbo.tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl AS tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl 
		WHERE--(FORMAT(Sending_HU_Scan_Dt, 'yyyy-MM-dd') BETWEEN @fromdate AND @Todate)))
		(@fromdate ='' OR Sending_HU_Scan_Dt >= @fromdate) AND (@Todate ='' OR Sending_HU_Scan_Dt < DATEADD(DAY, 1,@todate))))
		AND(@SearchTerm = '' OR dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].Material LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%');
	END

	ELSE If(@status='BIND_EAN_FOR_ENCODING_STORE')
    BEGIN
		select DISTINCT Top 10 dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].EAN from 
		dbo.tbl_DC_Outward_EPC_Dtl 
		JOIN dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl] ON dbo.tbl_DC_Outward_EPC_Dtl.EPC = dbo.tbl_Encoding_Dtl.Encode_EPC 
		LEFT JOIN dbo.[tbl_Ean_Mat_Mst] ON dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].ean = dbo.[tbl_Ean_Mat_Mst].EAN_UPC
		LEFT JOIN dbo.[tbl_Material_Master] ON dbo.[tbl_Ean_Mat_Mst].Material = dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].Material
		LEFT JOIN dbo.[tbl_Store_Master]  ON dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Store_ID = dbo.[tbl_Store_Master].Store_ID
		where dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].WH_ID IS NOT NULL AND dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Store_ID IS NOT NULL
		AND dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].Material = COALESCE(NULLIF(@Material, ''), dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].Material)
		AND dbo.[tbl_Store_Master].Store_Code =COALESCE(NULLIF(@Store_code, ''),dbo.[tbl_Store_Master].Store_Code)
		AND dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].Material is not null
		AND (dbo.tbl_DC_Outward_EPC_Dtl.Ref_No IN 
		(SELECT DISTINCT Ref_No FROM dbo.tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl AS tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl 
		WHERE  --(FORMAT(Sending_HU_Scan_Dt, 'yyyy-MM-dd') BETWEEN @fromdate AND @Todate)
		(@fromdate ='' OR Sending_HU_Scan_Dt >= @fromdate) AND (@Todate ='' OR Sending_HU_Scan_Dt < DATEADD(DAY, 1,@todate))))
		AND(@SearchTerm = '' OR dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].EAN LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%');
	END

	ELSE If(@status='SEARCH_BIND_EAN_FOR_ENCODING_STORE')
    BEGIN
		select DISTINCT dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].EAN from 
		dbo.tbl_DC_Outward_EPC_Dtl 
		JOIN dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl] ON dbo.tbl_DC_Outward_EPC_Dtl.EPC = dbo.tbl_Encoding_Dtl.Encode_EPC 
		LEFT JOIN dbo.[tbl_Ean_Mat_Mst] ON dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].ean = dbo.[tbl_Ean_Mat_Mst].EAN_UPC
		LEFT JOIN dbo.[tbl_Material_Master] ON dbo.[tbl_Ean_Mat_Mst].Material = dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].Material
		LEFT JOIN dbo.[tbl_Store_Master]  ON dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Store_ID = dbo.[tbl_Store_Master].Store_ID
		where dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].WH_ID IS NOT NULL AND dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Store_ID IS NOT NULL
		AND dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].Material = COALESCE(NULLIF(@Material, ''), dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].Material)
		AND dbo.[tbl_Store_Master].Store_Code =COALESCE(NULLIF(@Store_code, ''),dbo.[tbl_Store_Master].Store_Code)
		AND dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].Material is not null
		AND (dbo.tbl_DC_Outward_EPC_Dtl.Ref_No IN (SELECT DISTINCT Ref_No FROM dbo.tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl AS tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl
		WHERE --(FORMAT(Sending_HU_Scan_Dt, 'yyyy-MM-dd') BETWEEN @fromdate AND @Todate)
		(@fromdate ='' OR Sending_HU_Scan_Dt >= @fromdate) AND (@Todate ='' OR Sending_HU_Scan_Dt < DATEADD(DAY, 1,@todate))))
		AND(@SearchTerm = '' OR dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].EAN LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%');
    END

	ELSE If(@status='ENCODING_SHOW_DATA_FOR_STORE')
    BEGIN   
		SELECT -- ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY FORMAT(dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Encode_Date,'yyyy-MM-dd') DESC) AS RowNumber,
		dbo.[tbl_Store_Master].Store_Code AS 'LOCATION_NAME',
		dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].EAN, 
		COUNT(distinct dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Encode_EPC) AS 'ENCODING_Tags',
		ISNULL(dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].Material, 'NA') AS 'ARTICLE',
		ISNULL(dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].ART_DESC, 'NA') AS 'ARTICLE_DESC',
		FORMAT(dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Encode_Date,'yyyy-MM-dd') as 'Encode_Date'
		INTO #ENCODINGSTORE
		FROM dbo.tbl_DC_Outward_EPC_Dtl 
		JOIN dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl] ON dbo.tbl_DC_Outward_EPC_Dtl.EPC = dbo.tbl_Encoding_Dtl.Encode_EPC
		LEFT JOIN dbo.[tbl_Ean_Mat_Mst] ON dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].ean = dbo.[tbl_Ean_Mat_Mst].EAN_UPC
		LEFT JOIN dbo.[tbl_Material_Master] ON dbo.[tbl_Ean_Mat_Mst].Material = dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].Material
		LEFT JOIN dbo.[tbl_Store_Master]  ON dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Store_ID = dbo.[tbl_Store_Master].Store_ID
		WHERE dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].WH_ID IS NOT NULL AND dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Store_ID IS NOT NULL
		AND dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].EAN = COALESCE(NULLIF(@EAN, ''), dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].EAN)
		AND  ((@Material='' and (dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].Material IS NULL)) 
		     OR dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].Material = COALESCE(NULLIF(@material, ''), dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].Material))
		AND dbo.[tbl_Store_Master].Store_Code =COALESCE(NULLIF(@Store_code, ''),dbo.[tbl_Store_Master].Store_Code)
		AND (dbo.tbl_DC_Outward_EPC_Dtl.Ref_No IN (SELECT DISTINCT Ref_No FROM dbo.tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl AS tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl 
		WHERE--(FORMAT(Sending_HU_Scan_Dt, 'yyyy-MM-dd') BETWEEN @fromdate AND @Todate) 
		(@fromdate ='' OR Sending_HU_Scan_Dt >= @fromdate) AND (@Todate ='' OR Sending_HU_Scan_Dt < DATEADD(DAY, 1,@todate))
		AND Is_Complete_Status_Flg = '1'))
		AND(@SearchTerm = '' OR dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].Material LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%' 
		OR dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].ART_DESC LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%'
		OR dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].EAN LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%'
		OR FORMAT(dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Encode_Date,'yyyy-MM-dd') LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%')
		GROUP BY dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].EAN,dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].Material,dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].ART_DESC,FORMAT(dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Encode_Date,'yyyy-MM-dd'),
		dbo.[tbl_Store_Master].Store_Code;

	
		-- Get the total record count
		SELECT @RecordCount = COUNT(*) FROM #ENCODINGSTORE;
		SELECT @TotalCount = sum(ENCODING_Tags) FROM #ENCODINGSTORE;

		 	SET @SortDirection =
				CASE 
					WHEN LOWER(@SortDirection) = 'desc' THEN 'DESC'
					ELSE 'ASC'
				END;

			SET @OrderBy =	
				CASE @SortColumn
					WHEN 'ENCODING_Tags' THEN 'R.ENCODING_Tags' 
					WHEN 'LOCATION_NAME' THEN 'R.LOCATION_NAME' 
					WHEN 'ARTICLE_DESC' THEN 'R.ARTICLE_DESC'
					WHEN 'EAN' THEN 'R.EAN'
					WHEN 'Encode_Date' THEN 'R.Encode_Date'
					ELSE 'R.ARTICLE'
				END
				+ ' ' + @SortDirection
				+ ', R.ARTICLE ASC';
			
           	SET @SQL = N'SELECT *FROM (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY ' + @OrderBy + N') AS RowNumber,* FROM #ENCODINGSTORE R ) T
			WHERE RowNumber BETWEEN @StartRow AND @EndRow
			ORDER BY RowNumber;';

			EXEC sp_executesql	@SQL,N'@StartRow INT, @EndRow INT',
			@StartRow = @StartRow,	@EndRow   = @EndRow;
	

			 
	   -- Clean up the temporary table
		DROP TABLE #ENCODINGSTORE;
    END

    ELSE If(@status='EXPORT_ENCODING_SHOW_DATA_FOR_STORE')
    BEGIN   
	    SELECT  ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY FORMAT(dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Encode_Date,'yyyy-MM-dd') DESC) AS RowNumber,
		dbo.[tbl_Store_Master].Store_Code AS 'ASSIGNED_STORE_NAME',
		dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].EAN, 
		COUNT(distinct dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Encode_EPC) AS 'ENCODING_TAGS',
		ISNULL(dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].Material, 'NA') AS 'ARTICLE_NO',
		ISNULL(dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].ART_DESC, 'NA') AS 'ARTICLE_DESCRIPTION',
		FORMAT(dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Encode_Date,'yyyy-MM-dd') as 'ENCODE_DATE'
		FROM dbo.tbl_DC_Outward_EPC_Dtl 
		JOIN dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl] ON dbo.tbl_DC_Outward_EPC_Dtl.EPC = dbo.tbl_Encoding_Dtl.Encode_EPC
		LEFT JOIN dbo.[tbl_Ean_Mat_Mst] ON dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].ean = dbo.[tbl_Ean_Mat_Mst].EAN_UPC
		LEFT JOIN dbo.[tbl_Material_Master] ON dbo.[tbl_Ean_Mat_Mst].Material = dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].Material
		LEFT JOIN dbo.[tbl_Store_Master]  ON dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Store_ID = dbo.[tbl_Store_Master].Store_ID
		WHERE dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].WH_ID IS NOT NULL AND dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Store_ID IS NOT NULL
		AND dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].EAN = COALESCE(NULLIF(@EAN, ''), dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].EAN)
		AND  ((@Material='' and (dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].Material IS NULL)) 
		     OR dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].Material = COALESCE(NULLIF(@material, ''), dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].Material))
		AND dbo.[tbl_Store_Master].Store_Code =COALESCE(NULLIF(@Store_code, ''),dbo.[tbl_Store_Master].Store_Code)
		AND (dbo.tbl_DC_Outward_EPC_Dtl.Ref_No IN (SELECT DISTINCT Ref_No FROM dbo.tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl AS tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl 
		WHERE--(FORMAT(Sending_HU_Scan_Dt, 'yyyy-MM-dd') BETWEEN @fromdate AND @Todate) 
		(@fromdate ='' OR Sending_HU_Scan_Dt >= @fromdate) AND (@Todate ='' OR Sending_HU_Scan_Dt < DATEADD(DAY, 1,@todate))
		AND Is_Complete_Status_Flg = '1'))
		AND(@SearchTerm = '' OR dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].Material LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%' 
		OR dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].ART_DESC LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%'
		OR dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].EAN LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%'
		OR FORMAT(dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Encode_Date,'yyyy-MM-dd') LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%')
		GROUP BY dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].EAN,dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].Material,dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].ART_DESC,FORMAT(dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Encode_Date,'yyyy-MM-dd'),
		dbo.[tbl_Store_Master].Store_Code;
	END

	ELSE If(@status='VIEW_ENCODING_SHOW_DATA_FOR_STORE')
    BEGIN   
		SELECT -- ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY FORMAT(dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Encode_Date,'yyyy-MM-dd') DESC) AS RowNumber,
		dbo.[tbl_Store_Master].Store_Code AS 'LOCATION_NAME',
		dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].EAN, 
		dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Encode_EPC,
		ISNULL(dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].Material, 'NA') AS 'ARTICLE',
		ISNULL(dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].ART_DESC, 'NA') AS 'ARTICLE_DESC',
		FORMAT(dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Encode_Date,'yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss') as 'Encode_Date'
		INTO #VIEW_ENCODING_SHOW_DATA_FOR_STORE
		FROM dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl]--dbo.tbl_DC_Outward_EPC_Dtl 
		--INNER JOIN dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl] ON dbo.tbl_DC_Outward_EPC_Dtl.EPC = dbo.tbl_Encoding_Dtl.Encode_EPC 
		LEFT JOIN dbo.[tbl_Ean_Mat_Mst] ON dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].ean = dbo.[tbl_Ean_Mat_Mst].EAN_UPC
		LEFT JOIN dbo.[tbl_Material_Master] ON dbo.[tbl_Ean_Mat_Mst].Material = dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].Material
		LEFT JOIN dbo.[tbl_Store_Master]  ON dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Store_ID = dbo.[tbl_Store_Master].Store_ID
		WHERE dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].WH_ID IS NOT NULL AND dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Store_ID IS NOT NULL
		AND dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].EAN = COALESCE(NULLIF(@EAN, ''), dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].EAN)
		AND  ((@Material='' and (dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].Material IS NULL)) 
		OR dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].Material = COALESCE(NULLIF(@material, ''), dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].Material))
		AND dbo.[tbl_Store_Master].Store_Code =COALESCE(NULLIF(@Store_code, ''),dbo.[tbl_Store_Master].Store_Code)
		--AND (dbo.tbl_DC_Outward_EPC_Dtl.Ref_No IN (SELECT DISTINCT Ref_No FROM dbo.tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl AS tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl WHERE(FORMAT(Sending_HU_Scan_Dt, 'yyyy-MM-dd') BETWEEN @fromdate AND @Todate)))
		AND convert (date,dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Encode_Date,103) between 
			convert (date,COALESCE(NULLIF(@fromdate, ''),dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Encode_date),103)
			and convert (date,COALESCE(NULLIF(@Todate, ''),dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Encode_date),103);

			SELECT @RecordCount = COUNT(*) FROM #VIEW_ENCODING_SHOW_DATA_FOR_STORE;
			

			SET @SortDirection =
				CASE 
					WHEN LOWER(@SortDirection) = 'desc' THEN 'DESC'
					ELSE 'ASC'
				END;

			SET @OrderBy =	
				CASE @SortColumn
					WHEN 'ENCODING_Tags' THEN 'R.ENCODING_Tags' 
					WHEN 'LOCATION_NAME' THEN 'R.LOCATION_NAME' 
					WHEN 'ARTICLE_DESC' THEN 'R.ARTICLE_DESC'
					WHEN 'EAN' THEN 'R.EAN'
					WHEN 'Encode_Date' THEN 'R.Encode_Date'
					WHEN 'Encode_EPC' THEN 'R.Encode_EPC'
					ELSE 'R.ARTICLE'
				END
				+ ' ' + @SortDirection
				+ ', R.ARTICLE ASC';
			
           	SET @SQL = N'SELECT *FROM (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY ' + @OrderBy + N') AS RowNumber,* FROM #VIEW_ENCODING_SHOW_DATA_FOR_STORE R ) T
			WHERE RowNumber BETWEEN @StartRow AND @EndRow
			ORDER BY RowNumber;';

			EXEC sp_executesql	@SQL,N'@StartRow INT, @EndRow INT',
			@StartRow = @StartRow,	@EndRow   = @EndRow;

			DROP TABLE #VIEW_ENCODING_SHOW_DATA_FOR_STORE;
	
	END

	ELSE If(@status='EXPORT_VIEW_ENCODING_SHOW_DATA_FOR_STORE')
    BEGIN   
	    SELECT -- ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY FORMAT(dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Encode_Date,'yyyy-MM-dd') DESC) AS RowNumber,
		dbo.[tbl_Store_Master].Store_Code AS 'LOCATION_NAME',
		dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].EAN, 
		dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Encode_EPC,
		ISNULL(dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].Material, 'NA') AS 'ARTICLE',
		ISNULL(dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].ART_DESC, 'NA') AS 'ARTICLE_DESC',
		FORMAT(dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Encode_Date,'yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss') as 'Encode_Date'
		FROM dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl]--dbo.tbl_DC_Outward_EPC_Dtl 
		--INNER JOIN dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl] ON dbo.tbl_DC_Outward_EPC_Dtl.EPC = dbo.tbl_Encoding_Dtl.Encode_EPC 
		LEFT JOIN dbo.[tbl_Ean_Mat_Mst] ON dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].ean = dbo.[tbl_Ean_Mat_Mst].EAN_UPC
		LEFT JOIN dbo.[tbl_Material_Master] ON dbo.[tbl_Ean_Mat_Mst].Material = dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].Material
		LEFT JOIN dbo.[tbl_Store_Master]  ON dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Store_ID = dbo.[tbl_Store_Master].Store_ID
		WHERE dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].WH_ID IS NOT NULL AND dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Store_ID IS NOT NULL
		AND dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].EAN = COALESCE(NULLIF(@EAN, ''), dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].EAN)
		AND  ((@Material='' and (dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].Material IS NULL)) 
		OR dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].Material = COALESCE(NULLIF(@material, ''), dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].Material))
		AND dbo.[tbl_Store_Master].Store_Code =COALESCE(NULLIF(@Store_code, ''),dbo.[tbl_Store_Master].Store_Code)
		--AND (dbo.tbl_DC_Outward_EPC_Dtl.Ref_No IN (SELECT DISTINCT Ref_No FROM dbo.tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl AS tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl WHERE(FORMAT(Sending_HU_Scan_Dt, 'yyyy-MM-dd') BETWEEN @fromdate AND @Todate)))
		AND convert (date,dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Encode_Date,103) between 
			convert (date,COALESCE(NULLIF(@fromdate, ''),dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Encode_date),103)
			and convert (date,COALESCE(NULLIF(@Todate, ''),dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Encode_date),103);
	END

	-----------------------FOR RETURN QTY-------------------------------------------------------------
	ELSE If(@status='BIND_COUNTER_FOR_RETURN')
    BEGIN 
	   SELECT DISTINCT COUNTER_NO  FROM [dbo].[VPOS_SALE_RET_DTL] 
	   WHERE --[dbo].[VPOS_SALE_RET_DTL].BILL_DATE =@BILL_DATE
	   (@fromdate ='' OR [dbo].[VPOS_SALE_RET_DTL].BILL_DATE >= @fromdate) AND (@Todate ='' OR [dbo].[VPOS_SALE_RET_DTL].BILL_DATE < DATEADD(DAY, 1, @Todate))
	   AND [dbo].[VPOS_SALE_RET_DTL].COUNTER_NO IN ('RO','RE')  
	   AND SUBSTRING (dbo.[VPOS_SALE_RET_DTL].item_cd,1,3) in ('111','112','113') AND [dbo].[VPOS_SALE_RET_DTL].STORE_CODE = @Store_code;
	End

	ELSE If(@status='BIND_EAN_FOR_RETURN')
	BEGIN 
	   SELECT DISTINCT TOP 10 SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD AS 'EAN' FROM [dbo].[VPOS_SALE_RET_DTL] 
	   WHERE --[dbo].[VPOS_SALE_RET_DTL].BILL_DATE =@BILL_DATE
	   (@fromdate ='' OR [dbo].[VPOS_SALE_RET_DTL].BILL_DATE >= @fromdate) AND (@Todate ='' OR [dbo].[VPOS_SALE_RET_DTL].BILL_DATE < DATEADD(DAY, 1, @Todate))
	   AND [dbo].[VPOS_SALE_RET_DTL].COUNTER_NO IN ('RO','RE')  
	   AND SUBSTRING (dbo.[VPOS_SALE_RET_DTL].item_cd,1,3) in ('111','112','113') 
	   AND (@Store_code = '' OR [dbo].[VPOS_SALE_RET_DTL].STORE_CODE =@Store_code)
	   AND (@COUNTER_NO = '' OR [dbo].[VPOS_SALE_RET_DTL].COUNTER_NO = @COUNTER_NO)
	   AND(@SearchTerm = '' OR [dbo].[VPOS_SALE_RET_DTL].SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%');
	End

	ELSE If(@status='SEARCH_BIND_EAN_FOR_RETURN')
	BEGIN 
	   SELECT DISTINCT SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD AS 'EAN' FROM [dbo].[VPOS_SALE_RET_DTL]
	   WHERE --(@SearchTerm = '' OR [dbo].[VPOS_SALE_RET_DTL].SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD  LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%') AND
	    --[dbo].[VPOS_SALE_RET_DTL].BILL_DATE =@BILL_DATE
	   (@fromdate ='' OR [dbo].[VPOS_SALE_RET_DTL].BILL_DATE >= @fromdate) AND (@Todate ='' OR [dbo].[VPOS_SALE_RET_DTL].BILL_DATE < DATEADD(DAY, 1, @Todate))
	   AND [dbo].[VPOS_SALE_RET_DTL].COUNTER_NO IN ('RO','RE') AND SUBSTRING (dbo.[VPOS_SALE_RET_DTL].item_cd,1,3) in ('111','112','113') 
	   AND (@Store_code = '' OR [dbo].[VPOS_SALE_RET_DTL].STORE_CODE =@Store_code)
	   AND (@COUNTER_NO = '' OR [dbo].[VPOS_SALE_RET_DTL].COUNTER_NO = @COUNTER_NO)
	   AND(@SearchTerm = '' OR [dbo].[VPOS_SALE_RET_DTL].SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%');
	END

	ELSE If(@status='SHOW_SUMMARY_FOR_RETURN')
	BEGIN 
		--SELECT --ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY BB.STORE_CODE,BB.COUNTER_NO) AS RowNumber, 
		--BB.BILL_DATE,BB.STORE_CODE,BB.COUNTER_NO,SUM(BB.RET_QTY) AS 'RETURN_QTY', SUM(BB.ENC_QTY) AS 'ENCODE_QTY',SUM(BB.RET_QTY) - SUM(BB.ENC_QTY) AS 'DIFFERENCE_QTY',
		--CASE WHEN SUM(BB.RET_QTY) = SUM(BB.ENC_QTY) THEN 'COMPLETE' ELSE 'PENDING' END AS 'STATUS'
		--INTO #SUMMARYRETURN
		--FROM 
		--	(
		--	SELECT CAST ([dbo].[VPOS_SALE_RET_DTL].BILL_DATE AS date) AS 'BILL_DATE',[dbo].[VPOS_SALE_RET_DTL].STORE_CODE,COUNTER_NO,CAST(ISNULL(RET_QTY,0) AS int) AS 'RET_QTY',
		--	CASE WHEN ISNULL(CAST(RET_QTY AS INT), 0) < ISNULL(E.ENCODE_QTY, 0) THEN ISNULL(CAST(RET_QTY AS INT), 0) ELSE ISNULL(E.ENCODE_QTY, 0) END AS 'ENC_QTY'
		--	FROM [dbo].[VPOS_SALE_RET_DTL] 
		--	LEFT JOIN 
		--			(SELECT COUNT([dbo].[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Encode_Trans_ID) AS 'ENCODE_QTY',[dbo].[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].EAN,[dbo].[tbl_Store_Master].Store_Code
		--				FROM [dbo].[tbl_Encoding_Dtl]  LEFT JOIN [dbo].[tbl_Store_Master] ON [dbo].[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Store_ID =  [dbo].[tbl_Store_Master].Store_ID
		--				WHERE --[dbo].[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].encode_date >=@BILL_DATE
		--				(@fromdate ='' OR [dbo].[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].encode_date  >= @fromdate) AND (@Todate ='' OR [dbo].[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].encode_date  < DATEADD(DAY, 1, @Todate)) 
		--				AND [dbo].[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].STORE_FLOOR_ID IS NOT NULL AND [dbo].[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].STORE_ID IS NOT NULL AND [dbo].[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].WH_ID IS NULL
		--				GROUP BY[dbo].[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].EAN,[dbo].[tbl_Store_Master].Store_Code
		--				) AS E 
		--	ON [dbo].[VPOS_SALE_RET_DTL].SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD=E.EAN AND [dbo].[VPOS_SALE_RET_DTL].STORE_CODE=E.Store_Code
		--	WHERE --[dbo].[VPOS_SALE_RET_DTL].BILL_DATE= @BILL_DATE
		--	(@fromdate ='' OR [dbo].[VPOS_SALE_RET_DTL].BILL_DATE >= @fromdate) AND (@Todate ='' OR [dbo].[VPOS_SALE_RET_DTL].BILL_DATE < DATEADD(DAY, 1, @Todate))
		--	AND SUBSTRING (dbo.[VPOS_SALE_RET_DTL].item_cd,1,3) in ('111','112','113') AND [dbo].[VPOS_SALE_RET_DTL].COUNTER_NO IN ('RO','RE')
  --          AND (@Store_code = '' OR [dbo].[VPOS_SALE_RET_DTL].STORE_CODE =@Store_code)
		--	AND (@COUNTER_NO = '' OR [dbo].[VPOS_SALE_RET_DTL].COUNTER_NO = @COUNTER_NO)
		--	AND (@EAN = '' OR [dbo].[VPOS_SALE_RET_DTL].SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD = @EAN)
		--	AND(@SearchTerm = '' OR [dbo].[VPOS_SALE_RET_DTL].STORE_CODE LIKE @SearchTerm + '%'
		--	OR [dbo].[VPOS_SALE_RET_DTL].COUNTER_NO LIKE @SearchTerm + '%')
		--	) BB
		--GROUP BY CAST (BB.BILL_DATE AS date),BB.STORE_CODE,BB.COUNTER_NO;

		;WITH EncodingCTE AS (
		SELECT COUNT([dbo].[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Encode_Trans_ID) AS 'ENCODE_QTY',[dbo].[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].EAN,[dbo].[tbl_Store_Master].Store_Code,
		CAST([dbo].[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Encode_DATE AS DATE) AS 'ENCODE_DATE'
		FROM [dbo].[tbl_Encoding_Dtl] INNER JOIN [dbo].[tbl_Store_Master] ON [dbo].[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Store_ID =  [dbo].[tbl_Store_Master].Store_ID
		WHERE [dbo].[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].STORE_FLOOR_ID IS NOT NULL AND [dbo].[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].STORE_ID IS NOT NULL AND [dbo].[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].WH_ID IS NULL
		AND (@fromdate ='' OR [dbo].[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].encode_date  >= @fromdate) AND (@Todate ='' OR [dbo].[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].encode_date  < DATEADD(DAY, 1, @Todate)) 
		GROUP BY[dbo].[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].EAN,[dbo].[tbl_Store_Master].Store_Code,CAST([dbo].[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Encode_DATE AS DATE)),

		SalesCTE AS (
		 SELECT CAST ([dbo].[VPOS_SALE_RET_DTL].BILL_DATE AS DATE) AS 'BILL_DATE',[dbo].[VPOS_SALE_RET_DTL].STORE_CODE,COUNTER_NO,SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD,CAST(ISNULL(RET_QTY,0) AS int) AS 'RET_QTY'
		 FROM [dbo].[VPOS_SALE_RET_DTL]
		 WHERE (@fromdate ='' OR [dbo].[VPOS_SALE_RET_DTL].BILL_DATE >= @fromdate) AND (@Todate ='' OR [dbo].[VPOS_SALE_RET_DTL].BILL_DATE < DATEADD(DAY, 1, @Todate))
		 AND (dbo.[VPOS_SALE_RET_DTL].item_cd LIKE '111%' OR dbo.[VPOS_SALE_RET_DTL].item_cd LIKE '112%' OR dbo.[VPOS_SALE_RET_DTL].item_cd LIKE '113%') 
		 AND [dbo].[VPOS_SALE_RET_DTL].COUNTER_NO IN ('RO','RE')
		 AND (@Store_code = '' OR [dbo].[VPOS_SALE_RET_DTL].STORE_CODE =@Store_code)
		 AND (@COUNTER_NO = '' OR [dbo].[VPOS_SALE_RET_DTL].COUNTER_NO = @COUNTER_NO)
		 AND (@EAN = '' OR [dbo].[VPOS_SALE_RET_DTL].SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD = @EAN))

		SELECT S.BILL_DATE,S.STORE_CODE,S.COUNTER_NO,SUM(S.RET_QTY) AS 'RETURN_QTY',
		ISNULL(SUM(CASE WHEN S.RET_QTY < ISNULL(E.ENCODE_QTY, 0) THEN S.RET_QTY ELSE ISNULL(E.ENCODE_QTY, 0)END), 0) AS 'ENCODE_QTY',
		ISNULL(SUM(S.RET_QTY), 0) - ISNULL(SUM(CASE WHEN S.RET_QTY < ISNULL(E.ENCODE_QTY, 0) THEN S.RET_QTY ELSE ISNULL(E.ENCODE_QTY, 0) END), 0) AS 'DIFFERENCE_QTY',
		CASE WHEN ISNULL(SUM(S.RET_QTY), 0) = ISNULL(SUM(CASE WHEN S.RET_QTY < ISNULL(E.ENCODE_QTY, 0)THEN S.RET_QTY ELSE ISNULL(E.ENCODE_QTY, 0)END), 0) THEN 'COMPLETE' ELSE 'PENDING' END AS 'STATUS'
		INTO #SUMMARYRETURN FROM SalesCTE S LEFT JOIN EncodingCTE E ON S.SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD = E.EAN AND S.STORE_CODE = E.Store_Code AND S.BILL_DATE=E.ENCODE_DATE
		WHERE(@SearchTerm = '' OR S.STORE_CODE LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%' OR S.COUNTER_NO LIKE  '%' + @SearchTerm + '%')
		GROUP BY S.BILL_DATE,S.STORE_CODE,S.COUNTER_NO ORDER BY  S.BILL_DATE DESC;
   

		SELECT @RecordCount = COUNT(*) FROM #SUMMARYRETURN;
		SELECT @QTY = SUM(RETURN_QTY) FROM #SUMMARYRETURN;
		SELECT @ENCQTY = SUM(ENCODE_QTY) FROM #SUMMARYRETURN;
		SELECT @DIFFQTY = SUM(DIFFERENCE_QTY) FROM #SUMMARYRETURN;

			SET @SortDirection =
				CASE 
					WHEN LOWER(@SortDirection) = 'desc' THEN 'DESC'
					ELSE 'ASC'
				END;

			SET @OrderBy =	
				CASE @SortColumn
					WHEN 'COUNTER_NO' THEN 'R.COUNTER_NO' 
					WHEN 'STORE_CODE' THEN 'R.STORE_CODE' 
					WHEN 'ENCODE_QTY' THEN 'R.ENCODE_QTY'
					WHEN 'RETURN_QTY' THEN 'R.RETURN_QTY'
					WHEN 'DIFFERENCE_QTY' THEN 'R.DIFFERENCE_QTY'
					WHEN 'STATUS' THEN 'R.STATUS'
					ELSE 'R.BILL_DATE'
				END
				+ ' ' + @SortDirection
				+ ', R.BILL_DATE ASC';
			
           	SET @SQL = N'SELECT *FROM (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY ' + @OrderBy + N') AS RowNumber,* FROM #SUMMARYRETURN R ) T
			WHERE RowNumber BETWEEN @StartRow AND @EndRow
			ORDER BY RowNumber;';

			EXEC sp_executesql	@SQL,N'@StartRow INT, @EndRow INT',
			@StartRow = @StartRow,	@EndRow   = @EndRow;

		-- Clean up the temporary table
		DROP TABLE #SUMMARYRETURN;
	END

	
	ELSE If(@status='SHOW_SUMMARY_DATA_FOR_RETURN')
	BEGIN 
	  -- SELECT --ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY (SELECT 1) ASC) AS RowNumber,
	  -- CAST ([dbo].[VPOS_SALE_RET_DTL].BILL_DATE AS date) AS 'BILL_DATE',[dbo].[VPOS_SALE_RET_DTL].POS_TYPE,[dbo].[VPOS_SALE_RET_DTL].STORE_CODE,
	  -- COUNTER_NO,SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD AS 'EAN',ITEM_CD AS 'MATERIAL',
	  -- CAST(ISNULL(RET_QTY,0) AS int) AS 'RETURN_QTY',
	  -- CASE WHEN ISNULL(CAST(RET_QTY AS INT), 0) < ISNULL(E.ENCODE_QTY, 0) THEN ISNULL(CAST(RET_QTY AS INT), 0) ELSE ISNULL(E.ENCODE_QTY, 0) END AS 'ENCODE_QTY',
	  -- CAST(ISNULL(RET_QTY,0) AS int) - CASE WHEN ISNULL(CAST(RET_QTY AS INT), 0) < ISNULL(E.ENCODE_QTY, 0) THEN ISNULL(CAST(RET_QTY AS INT), 0) 
	  -- ELSE ISNULL(E.ENCODE_QTY, 0) END AS 'DIFFERENCE_QTY',
	  -- CASE WHEN CAST(ISNULL(RET_QTY,0) AS INT) = CASE WHEN ISNULL(CAST(RET_QTY AS INT), 0) < ISNULL(E.ENCODE_QTY, 0) THEN ISNULL(CAST(RET_QTY AS INT), 0) 
	  -- ELSE ISNULL(E.ENCODE_QTY, 0) END THEN 'COMPLETE' ELSE 'PENDING' END AS 'STATUS'
	  -- INTO #SUMMARYDATARETURN
	  -- FROM [dbo].[VPOS_SALE_RET_DTL] 
	  -- LEFT JOIN 
		 --  (SELECT COUNT([dbo].[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Encode_Trans_ID) AS 'ENCODE_QTY',[dbo].[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].EAN,[dbo].[tbl_Store_Master].Store_Code
			--FROM [dbo].[tbl_Encoding_Dtl]  LEFT JOIN [dbo].[tbl_Store_Master] ON [dbo].[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Store_ID =  [dbo].[tbl_Store_Master].Store_ID
			--WHERE CAST([dbo].[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].encode_date AS DATE) >=@BILL_DATE 
			--AND [dbo].[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].STORE_FLOOR_ID IS NOT NULL AND [dbo].[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].STORE_ID IS NOT NULL AND [dbo].[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].WH_ID IS NULL
			--GROUP BY[dbo].[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].EAN,[dbo].[tbl_Store_Master].Store_Code) AS E 

	  -- ON [dbo].[VPOS_SALE_RET_DTL].SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD=E.EAN AND [dbo].[VPOS_SALE_RET_DTL].STORE_CODE=E.Store_Code
	  -- WHERE [dbo].[VPOS_SALE_RET_DTL].BILL_DATE= @BILL_DATE  AND SUBSTRING (dbo.[VPOS_SALE_RET_DTL].item_cd,1,3) in ('111','112','113')
	  -- AND [dbo].[VPOS_SALE_RET_DTL].COUNTER_NO IN ('RO','RE')
	  -- AND (@Store_code = '' OR [dbo].[VPOS_SALE_RET_DTL].STORE_CODE =@Store_code)
	  -- AND (@COUNTER_NO = '' OR [dbo].[VPOS_SALE_RET_DTL].COUNTER_NO = @COUNTER_NO)
	  -- AND (@EAN = '' OR [dbo].[VPOS_SALE_RET_DTL].SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD = @EAN)
	  -- AND(@SearchTerm = '' OR [dbo].[VPOS_SALE_RET_DTL].SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD LIKE @SearchTerm + '%'
	  -- OR [dbo].[VPOS_SALE_RET_DTL].ITEM_CD LIKE @SearchTerm + '%'
	  -- OR [dbo].[VPOS_SALE_RET_DTL].STORE_CODE LIKE @SearchTerm + '%'
	  -- OR [dbo].[VPOS_SALE_RET_DTL].COUNTER_NO LIKE @SearchTerm + '%');


	  ;WITH EncodingCTE AS (
		SELECT COUNT([dbo].[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Encode_Trans_ID) AS 'ENCODE_QTY',[dbo].[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].EAN,[dbo].[tbl_Store_Master].Store_Code,
		CAST([dbo].[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Encode_DATE AS DATE) AS 'ENCODE_DATE'
		FROM [dbo].[tbl_Encoding_Dtl] INNER JOIN [dbo].[tbl_Store_Master] ON [dbo].[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Store_ID =  [dbo].[tbl_Store_Master].Store_ID
		WHERE [dbo].[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].STORE_FLOOR_ID IS NOT NULL AND [dbo].[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].STORE_ID IS NOT NULL AND [dbo].[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].WH_ID IS NULL
		AND (@BILL_DATE ='' OR [dbo].[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].encode_date  >= @BILL_DATE) AND (@BILL_DATE ='' OR [dbo].[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].encode_date  < DATEADD(DAY, 1, @BILL_DATE)) 
		GROUP BY[dbo].[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].EAN,[dbo].[tbl_Store_Master].Store_Code,CAST([dbo].[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Encode_DATE AS DATE)),

		SalesCTE AS (
		 SELECT CAST ([dbo].[VPOS_SALE_RET_DTL].BILL_DATE AS DATE) AS 'BILL_DATE',[dbo].[VPOS_SALE_RET_DTL].STORE_CODE,COUNTER_NO,SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD,ITEM_CD,POS_TYPE,
		 CAST(ISNULL(RET_QTY,0) AS int) AS 'RET_QTY'
		 FROM [dbo].[VPOS_SALE_RET_DTL]
		 WHERE (@BILL_DATE ='' OR [dbo].[VPOS_SALE_RET_DTL].BILL_DATE >= @BILL_DATE) AND (@BILL_DATE ='' OR [dbo].[VPOS_SALE_RET_DTL].BILL_DATE < DATEADD(DAY, 1, @BILL_DATE)) AND
		 (dbo.[VPOS_SALE_RET_DTL].item_cd LIKE '111%' OR dbo.[VPOS_SALE_RET_DTL].item_cd LIKE '112%' OR dbo.[VPOS_SALE_RET_DTL].item_cd LIKE '113%') 
		 AND [dbo].[VPOS_SALE_RET_DTL].COUNTER_NO IN ('RO','RE')
		 AND (@Store_code = '' OR [dbo].[VPOS_SALE_RET_DTL].STORE_CODE =@Store_code)
		 AND (@COUNTER_NO = '' OR [dbo].[VPOS_SALE_RET_DTL].COUNTER_NO = @COUNTER_NO)
		 AND (@EAN = '' OR [dbo].[VPOS_SALE_RET_DTL].SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD = @EAN))

		SELECT S.BILL_DATE,S.POS_TYPE,S.STORE_CODE,S.COUNTER_NO,SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD AS 'EAN',ITEM_CD AS 'MATERIAL',SUM(S.RET_QTY) AS 'RETURN_QTY',
		ISNULL(SUM(CASE WHEN S.RET_QTY < ISNULL(E.ENCODE_QTY, 0) THEN S.RET_QTY ELSE ISNULL(E.ENCODE_QTY, 0)END), 0) AS 'ENCODE_QTY',
		ISNULL(SUM(S.RET_QTY), 0) - ISNULL(SUM(CASE WHEN S.RET_QTY < ISNULL(E.ENCODE_QTY, 0) THEN S.RET_QTY ELSE ISNULL(E.ENCODE_QTY, 0) END), 0) AS 'DIFFERENCE_QTY',
		CASE WHEN ISNULL(SUM(S.RET_QTY), 0) = ISNULL(SUM(CASE WHEN S.RET_QTY < ISNULL(E.ENCODE_QTY, 0)THEN S.RET_QTY ELSE ISNULL(E.ENCODE_QTY, 0)END), 0) THEN 'COMPLETE' ELSE 'PENDING' END AS 'STATUS'
		INTO #SUMMARYDATARETURN FROM SalesCTE S LEFT JOIN EncodingCTE E ON S.SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD = E.EAN AND S.STORE_CODE = E.Store_Code AND S.BILL_DATE=E.ENCODE_DATE
		WHERE (@SearchTerm = '' OR S.SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%' OR S.ITEM_CD LIKE '%' +  @SearchTerm + '%' OR S.STORE_CODE LIKE '%' +  @SearchTerm + '%' 
		OR S.COUNTER_NO LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%') GROUP BY S.BILL_DATE,S.POS_TYPE,S.STORE_CODE,S.COUNTER_NO,SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD,S.ITEM_CD;
   

	   SELECT @RecordCount = COUNT(*) FROM #SUMMARYDATARETURN;
	   SELECT @QTY = SUM(RETURN_QTY) FROM #SUMMARYDATARETURN;
		SELECT @ENCQTY = SUM(ENCODE_QTY) FROM #SUMMARYDATARETURN;
		SELECT @DIFFQTY = SUM(DIFFERENCE_QTY) FROM #SUMMARYDATARETURN;
	
		SET @SortDirection =
				CASE 
					WHEN LOWER(@SortDirection) = 'desc' THEN 'DESC'
					ELSE 'ASC'
				END;

			SET @OrderBy =	
				CASE @SortColumn
					WHEN 'EAN' THEN 'R.EAN' 
					WHEN 'STORE_CODE' THEN 'R.STORE_CODE' 
					WHEN 'ENCODE_QTY' THEN 'R.ENCODE_QTY'
					WHEN 'RETURN_QTY' THEN 'R.RETURN_QTY'
					WHEN 'DIFFERENCE_QTY' THEN 'R.DIFFERENCE_QTY'
					WHEN 'STATUS' THEN 'R.STATUS'
					WHEN 'POS_TYPE' THEN 'R.POS_TYPE'
					WHEN 'MATERIAL' THEN 'R.MATERIAL'
					ELSE 'R.BILL_DATE'
				END
				+ ' ' + @SortDirection
				+ ', R.BILL_DATE ASC';
			
           	SET @SQL = N'SELECT *FROM (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY ' + @OrderBy + N') AS RowNumber,* FROM #SUMMARYDATARETURN R ) T
			WHERE RowNumber BETWEEN @StartRow AND @EndRow
			ORDER BY RowNumber;';

			EXEC sp_executesql	@SQL,N'@StartRow INT, @EndRow INT',
			@StartRow = @StartRow,	@EndRow   = @EndRow;

	   -- Clean up the temporary table
		DROP TABLE #SUMMARYDATARETURN;
	END

	ELSE If(@status='EXPORT_SHOW_SUMMARY_DATA_FOR_RETURN')
	BEGIN 
	  -- SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY (SELECT 1) ASC) AS RowNumber,
	  -- CAST ([dbo].[VPOS_SALE_RET_DTL].BILL_DATE AS date) AS 'RETURN_DATE',[dbo].[VPOS_SALE_RET_DTL].POS_TYPE,[dbo].[VPOS_SALE_RET_DTL].STORE_CODE,
	  -- COUNTER_NO,SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD AS 'EAN',ITEM_CD AS 'MATERIAL',
	  -- CAST(ISNULL(RET_QTY,0) AS int) AS 'RETURN_QTY',
	  -- CASE WHEN ISNULL(CAST(RET_QTY AS INT), 0) < ISNULL(E.ENCODE_QTY, 0) THEN ISNULL(CAST(RET_QTY AS INT), 0) ELSE ISNULL(E.ENCODE_QTY, 0) END AS 'ENCODE_QTY',
	  -- CAST(ISNULL(RET_QTY,0) AS int) - CASE WHEN ISNULL(CAST(RET_QTY AS INT), 0) < ISNULL(E.ENCODE_QTY, 0) THEN ISNULL(CAST(RET_QTY AS INT), 0) 
	  -- ELSE ISNULL(E.ENCODE_QTY, 0) END AS 'DIFFERENCE_QTY',
	  -- CASE WHEN CAST(ISNULL(RET_QTY,0) AS INT) = CASE WHEN ISNULL(CAST(RET_QTY AS INT), 0) < ISNULL(E.ENCODE_QTY, 0) THEN ISNULL(CAST(RET_QTY AS INT), 0) 
	  -- ELSE ISNULL(E.ENCODE_QTY, 0) END THEN 'COMPLETE' ELSE 'PENDING' END AS 'STATUS'
	  -- FROM [dbo].[VPOS_SALE_RET_DTL] 
	  -- LEFT JOIN 
			--(SELECT COUNT([dbo].[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Encode_Trans_ID) AS 'ENCODE_QTY',[dbo].[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].EAN,[dbo].[tbl_Store_Master].Store_Code
			--FROM [dbo].[tbl_Encoding_Dtl]  LEFT JOIN [dbo].[tbl_Store_Master] ON [dbo].[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Store_ID =  [dbo].[tbl_Store_Master].Store_ID
			--WHERE CAST([dbo].[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].encode_date AS DATE) >=@BILL_DATE 
			--AND [dbo].[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].STORE_FLOOR_ID IS NOT NULL AND [dbo].[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].STORE_ID IS NOT NULL AND [dbo].[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].WH_ID IS NULL
			--GROUP BY[dbo].[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].EAN,[dbo].[tbl_Store_Master].Store_Code) AS E 
   
	  -- ON [dbo].[VPOS_SALE_RET_DTL].SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD=E.EAN AND [dbo].[VPOS_SALE_RET_DTL].STORE_CODE=E.Store_Code
	  -- WHERE [dbo].[VPOS_SALE_RET_DTL].BILL_DATE= @BILL_DATE  AND SUBSTRING (dbo.[VPOS_SALE_RET_DTL].item_cd,1,3) in ('111','112','113')
	  -- AND [dbo].[VPOS_SALE_RET_DTL].COUNTER_NO IN ('RO','RE')
	  -- AND (@Store_code = '' OR [dbo].[VPOS_SALE_RET_DTL].STORE_CODE =@Store_code)
	  -- AND (@COUNTER_NO = '' OR [dbo].[VPOS_SALE_RET_DTL].COUNTER_NO = @COUNTER_NO)
	  -- AND (@EAN = '' OR [dbo].[VPOS_SALE_RET_DTL].SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD = @EAN);


	   ;WITH EncodingCTE AS (
		SELECT COUNT([dbo].[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Encode_Trans_ID) AS 'ENCODE_QTY',[dbo].[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].EAN,[dbo].[tbl_Store_Master].Store_Code,
		CAST([dbo].[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Encode_DATE AS DATE) AS 'ENCODE_DATE'
		FROM [dbo].[tbl_Encoding_Dtl] INNER JOIN [dbo].[tbl_Store_Master] ON [dbo].[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Store_ID =  [dbo].[tbl_Store_Master].Store_ID
		WHERE [dbo].[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].STORE_FLOOR_ID IS NOT NULL AND [dbo].[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].STORE_ID IS NOT NULL AND [dbo].[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].WH_ID IS NULL
		AND (@BILL_DATE ='' OR [dbo].[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].encode_date  >= @BILL_DATE) AND (@BILL_DATE ='' OR [dbo].[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].encode_date  < DATEADD(DAY, 1, @BILL_DATE)) 
		GROUP BY[dbo].[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].EAN,[dbo].[tbl_Store_Master].Store_Code,CAST([dbo].[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Encode_DATE AS DATE)),

		SalesCTE AS (
		 SELECT CAST ([dbo].[VPOS_SALE_RET_DTL].BILL_DATE AS DATE) AS 'BILL_DATE',[dbo].[VPOS_SALE_RET_DTL].STORE_CODE,COUNTER_NO,SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD,ITEM_CD,POS_TYPE,
		 CAST(ISNULL(RET_QTY,0) AS int) AS 'RET_QTY'
		 FROM [dbo].[VPOS_SALE_RET_DTL]
		 WHERE (@BILL_DATE ='' OR [dbo].[VPOS_SALE_RET_DTL].BILL_DATE >= @BILL_DATE) AND (@BILL_DATE ='' OR [dbo].[VPOS_SALE_RET_DTL].BILL_DATE < DATEADD(DAY, 1, @BILL_DATE)) AND
		 (dbo.[VPOS_SALE_RET_DTL].item_cd LIKE '111%' OR dbo.[VPOS_SALE_RET_DTL].item_cd LIKE '112%' OR dbo.[VPOS_SALE_RET_DTL].item_cd LIKE '113%') 
		 AND [dbo].[VPOS_SALE_RET_DTL].COUNTER_NO IN ('RO','RE')
		 AND (@Store_code = '' OR [dbo].[VPOS_SALE_RET_DTL].STORE_CODE =@Store_code)
		 AND (@COUNTER_NO = '' OR [dbo].[VPOS_SALE_RET_DTL].COUNTER_NO = @COUNTER_NO)
		 AND (@EAN = '' OR [dbo].[VPOS_SALE_RET_DTL].SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD = @EAN))

		SELECT S.BILL_DATE,S.POS_TYPE,S.STORE_CODE,S.COUNTER_NO,SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD AS 'EAN',ITEM_CD AS 'MATERIAL',SUM(S.RET_QTY) AS 'RETURN_QTY',
		ISNULL(SUM(CASE WHEN S.RET_QTY < ISNULL(E.ENCODE_QTY, 0) THEN S.RET_QTY ELSE ISNULL(E.ENCODE_QTY, 0)END), 0) AS 'ENCODE_QTY',
		ISNULL(SUM(S.RET_QTY), 0) - ISNULL(SUM(CASE WHEN S.RET_QTY < ISNULL(E.ENCODE_QTY, 0) THEN S.RET_QTY ELSE ISNULL(E.ENCODE_QTY, 0) END), 0) AS 'DIFFERENCE_QTY',
		CASE WHEN ISNULL(SUM(S.RET_QTY), 0) = ISNULL(SUM(CASE WHEN S.RET_QTY < ISNULL(E.ENCODE_QTY, 0)THEN S.RET_QTY ELSE ISNULL(E.ENCODE_QTY, 0)END), 0) THEN 'COMPLETE' ELSE 'PENDING' END AS 'STATUS'
		FROM SalesCTE S LEFT JOIN EncodingCTE E ON S.SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD = E.EAN AND S.STORE_CODE = E.Store_Code AND S.BILL_DATE=E.ENCODE_DATE
		GROUP BY S.BILL_DATE,S.POS_TYPE,S.STORE_CODE,S.COUNTER_NO,SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD,S.ITEM_CD;
	END

	-----------------------FOR VOID QTY-------------------------------------------------------------
	ELSE If(@status='BIND_COUNTER_FOR_VOID')
    BEGIN 
         SELECT DISTINCT BB.COUNTER_NO FROM (
		 SELECT COUNTER_NO,ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY EPC_TAG,STORE_CODE ORDER BY LAST_UPDATED_DATETIME DESC) AS rn FROM [dbo].[VPOS_SCAN_ITEMS_DTL] V
		 WHERE (V.item_cd LIKE '111%' OR V.item_cd LIKE '112%' OR V.item_cd LIKE '113%') 
		 AND V.EPC_TAG NOT IN (SELECT VP.EPC_TAG FROM VPOS_SCAN_ITEMS_DTL VP GROUP BY VP.EPC_TAG HAVING COUNT(CASE WHEN VP.EXT_COL = 'V' THEN 1 END) > 0 
			 AND COUNT(CASE WHEN VP.EXT_COL IS NULL THEN 1 END) > 0)
		 AND V.EXT_COL = 'V'
		 AND (@fromdate ='' OR V.LAST_UPDATED_DATETIME >= @fromdate) AND (@Todate ='' OR V.LAST_UPDATED_DATETIME < DATEADD(DAY, 1, @Todate)) 
		 AND V.STORE_CODE = @Store_code
		 )BB WHERE BB.RN=1;
    End

	ELSE If(@status='BIND_EAN_FOR_VOID')
	BEGIN 
       SELECT DISTINCT TOP 10 BB.SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD AS 'EAN' FROM (
	   SELECT V.SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD,V.COUNTER_NO,ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY EPC_TAG,STORE_CODE ORDER BY LAST_UPDATED_DATETIME DESC) AS rn FROM [dbo].[VPOS_SCAN_ITEMS_DTL] V
	   WHERE (V.item_cd LIKE '111%' OR V.item_cd LIKE '112%' OR V.item_cd LIKE '113%') 
	   AND V.EPC_TAG NOT IN (SELECT VP.EPC_TAG FROM VPOS_SCAN_ITEMS_DTL VP GROUP BY VP.EPC_TAG HAVING COUNT(CASE WHEN VP.EXT_COL = 'V' THEN 1 END) > 0 
	       AND COUNT(CASE WHEN VP.EXT_COL IS NULL THEN 1 END) > 0)
	   AND V.EXT_COL = 'V'
	   AND (@fromdate ='' OR V.LAST_UPDATED_DATETIME >= @fromdate) AND (@Todate ='' OR V.LAST_UPDATED_DATETIME < DATEADD(DAY, 1, @Todate)) 
	   AND (@Store_code = '' OR V.STORE_CODE =@Store_code)
	   AND (@COUNTER_NO = '' OR V.COUNTER_NO = @COUNTER_NO)
	   )BB WHERE BB.RN=1 AND (@SearchTerm = '' OR BB.SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%');

	End

	ELSE If(@status='SEARCH_BIND_EAN_FOR_VOID')
	BEGIN 
       SELECT DISTINCT BB.SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD AS 'EAN' FROM (
	   SELECT V.SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD,V.COUNTER_NO,ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY EPC_TAG,STORE_CODE ORDER BY LAST_UPDATED_DATETIME DESC) AS rn FROM [dbo].[VPOS_SCAN_ITEMS_DTL] V
	   WHERE (V.item_cd LIKE '111%' OR V.item_cd LIKE '112%' OR V.item_cd LIKE '113%') 
	   AND V.EPC_TAG NOT IN (SELECT VP.EPC_TAG FROM VPOS_SCAN_ITEMS_DTL VP GROUP BY VP.EPC_TAG HAVING COUNT(CASE WHEN VP.EXT_COL = 'V' THEN 1 END) > 0 
	       AND COUNT(CASE WHEN VP.EXT_COL IS NULL THEN 1 END) > 0)
	   AND V.EXT_COL = 'V'
	   AND (@fromdate ='' OR V.LAST_UPDATED_DATETIME >= @fromdate) AND (@Todate ='' OR V.LAST_UPDATED_DATETIME < DATEADD(DAY, 1, @Todate)) 
	   AND (@Store_code = '' OR V.STORE_CODE =@Store_code)
	   AND (@COUNTER_NO = '' OR V.COUNTER_NO = @COUNTER_NO)
	   )BB WHERE BB.RN=1 AND (@SearchTerm = '' OR BB.SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%');
	END

	ELSE If(@status='SHOW_SUMMARY_FOR_VOID')
	BEGIN
        ;WITH 
		--BadEPC AS (SELECT EPC_TAG FROM dbo.VPOS_SCAN_ITEMS_DTL WHERE (@fromdate ='' OR LAST_UPDATED_DATETIME >= @fromdate) AND (@Todate ='' OR LAST_UPDATED_DATETIME < DATEADD(DAY, 1, @Todate))
		-- GROUP BY EPC_TAG HAVING COUNT(CASE WHEN EXT_COL = 'V' THEN 1 END) > 0 AND COUNT(CASE WHEN EXT_COL IS NULL THEN 1 END) > 0),

		 VoidBase AS (SELECT V.LAST_UPDATED_DATETIME,V.STORE_CODE,V.COUNTER_NO,V.SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD,V.EPC_TAG,ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY V.EPC_TAG, V.STORE_CODE ORDER BY V.LAST_UPDATED_DATETIME DESC) AS rn FROM dbo.VPOS_SCAN_ITEMS_DTL V
					   WHERE (@fromdate ='' OR V.LAST_UPDATED_DATETIME >= @fromdate) AND (@Todate ='' OR V.LAST_UPDATED_DATETIME < DATEADD(DAY, 1, @Todate))
					   AND (V.item_cd LIKE '111%' OR V.item_cd LIKE '112%' OR V.item_cd LIKE '113%')
					   AND V.EXT_COL = 'V' AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM dbo.VPOS_SCAN_ITEMS_DTL B WHERE B.EPC_TAG = V.EPC_TAG 
                                                                  AND (
                                                                       (B.EXT_COL = 'V' AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM dbo.VPOS_SCAN_ITEMS_DTL X WHERE X.EPC_TAG = B.EPC_TAG AND X.EXT_COL IS NULL))
                                                                      )
                                                           )--(SELECT 1 FROM BadEPC B WHERE B.EPC_TAG = V.EPC_TAG)
					   AND (@STORE_CODE = '' OR V.STORE_CODE = @STORE_CODE)
					   AND (@COUNTER_NO = '' OR V.COUNTER_NO = @COUNTER_NO)
					   AND (@EAN = '' OR V.SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD = @EAN)),

		 VoidCTE AS (SELECT CONVERT(DATE,LAST_UPDATED_DATETIME) AS 'VOID_DATE',STORE_CODE,COUNTER_NO,SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD,COUNT(EPC_TAG) AS  VOID_QTY FROM VoidBase WHERE rn = 1 GROUP BY STORE_CODE,SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD,CONVERT(DATE,LAST_UPDATED_DATETIME),COUNTER_NO),
	  
		 EncodeCTE AS (SELECT SM.Store_Code,E.EAN,COUNT(E.Encode_Trans_ID) AS ENCODE_QTY,CONVERT(DATE,E.Encode_Date) AS ENCODE_DATE FROM dbo.tbl_Encoding_Dtl E JOIN dbo.tbl_Store_Master SM ON E.Store_ID = SM.Store_ID
					   WHERE (@fromdate ='' OR E.encode_date >= @fromdate) AND (@Todate ='' OR E.encode_date < DATEADD(DAY, 1, CAST(GETDATE() AS DATE)))
					   AND E.STORE_FLOOR_ID IS NOT NULL AND E.STORE_ID IS NOT NULL AND E.WH_ID IS NULL GROUP BY SM.Store_Code, E.EAN,CONVERT(DATE,E.Encode_Date))

		 SELECT  V.VOID_DATE,SM.Store_Code AS 'STORE_CODE',V.COUNTER_NO,ISNULL(SUM(V.VOID_QTY), 0) AS 'VOID_QTY',
		 ISNULL(SUM(CASE WHEN V.VOID_QTY < ISNULL(E.ENCODE_QTY, 0) THEN V.VOID_QTY ELSE ISNULL(E.ENCODE_QTY, 0)END), 0) AS 'ENCODE_QTY',
		 ISNULL(SUM(V.VOID_QTY), 0) - ISNULL(SUM(CASE WHEN V.VOID_QTY < ISNULL(E.ENCODE_QTY, 0) THEN V.VOID_QTY ELSE ISNULL(E.ENCODE_QTY, 0) END), 0) AS DIFFERENCE_QTY,
		 CASE WHEN ISNULL(SUM(V.VOID_QTY), 0) = ISNULL(SUM(CASE WHEN V.VOID_QTY < ISNULL(E.ENCODE_QTY, 0)THEN V.VOID_QTY ELSE ISNULL(E.ENCODE_QTY, 0)END), 0) THEN 'COMPLETE' ELSE 'PENDING'
		 END AS STATUS
		 INTO #VOIDSUMMARY
		 FROM dbo.tbl_Store_Master SM 
		 JOIN VoidCTE V  ON SM.Store_Code = V.STORE_CODE
		 LEFT JOIN EncodeCTE E ON SM.Store_Code = E.Store_Code AND V.SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD = E.EAN AND V.VOID_DATE=E.ENCODE_DATE
		 WHERE (@SearchTerm = '' OR V.COUNTER_NO LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%')
		 GROUP BY V.VOID_DATE,V.COUNTER_NO, SM.Store_Code ORDER BY V.VOID_DATE DESC;

        SELECT @RecordCount = COUNT(*),@QTY = SUM(VOID_QTY),@ENCQTY = SUM(ENCODE_QTY) ,@DIFFQTY = SUM(DIFFERENCE_QTY) FROM #VOIDSUMMARY;

    
	    SET @SortDirection =
				CASE 
					WHEN LOWER(@SortDirection) = 'desc' THEN 'DESC'
					ELSE 'ASC'
				END;

			SET @OrderBy =	
				CASE @SortColumn
					WHEN 'STORE_CODE' THEN 'R.STORE_CODE' 
					WHEN 'COUNTER_NO' THEN 'R.COUNTER_NO'
					WHEN 'VOID_QTY' THEN 'R.VOID_QTY'
					WHEN 'ENCODE_QTY' THEN 'R.ENCODE_QTY'
					WHEN 'DIFFERENCE_QTY' THEN 'R.DIFFERENCE_QTY'
					WHEN 'ARTICLE_DESC' THEN 'R.ARTICLE_DESC'
					WHEN 'STATUS' THEN 'R.STATUS'
					ELSE 'R.VOID_DATE'
				END
				+ ' ' + @SortDirection
				+ ', R.VOID_DATE';
			
            SET @SQL = N'SELECT *FROM (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY ' + @OrderBy + N') AS RowNumber,* FROM #VOIDSUMMARY R ) T
			WHERE RowNumber BETWEEN @StartRow AND @EndRow
			ORDER BY RowNumber;';

			EXEC sp_executesql
			@SQL,N'@StartRow INT, @EndRow INT',
			@StartRow = @StartRow,	@EndRow   = @EndRow;

		-- Clean up the temporary table
		DROP TABLE #VOIDSUMMARY;
	END

	ELSE If(@status='EXPORT_SHOW_SUMMARY_FOR_VOID')
	BEGIN
        ;WITH 
		 --BadEPC AS (SELECT EPC_TAG FROM dbo.VPOS_SCAN_ITEMS_DTL WHERE (@fromdate ='' OR LAST_UPDATED_DATETIME >= @fromdate) AND (@Todate ='' OR LAST_UPDATED_DATETIME < DATEADD(DAY, 1, @Todate))
		 --GROUP BY EPC_TAG HAVING COUNT(CASE WHEN EXT_COL = 'V' THEN 1 END) > 0 AND COUNT(CASE WHEN EXT_COL IS NULL THEN 1 END) > 0),

		 VoidBase AS (SELECT V.LAST_UPDATED_DATETIME,V.STORE_CODE,V.COUNTER_NO,V.SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD,V.EPC_TAG,ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY V.EPC_TAG, V.STORE_CODE ORDER BY V.LAST_UPDATED_DATETIME DESC) AS rn FROM dbo.VPOS_SCAN_ITEMS_DTL V
					   WHERE (@fromdate ='' OR V.LAST_UPDATED_DATETIME >= @fromdate) AND (@Todate ='' OR V.LAST_UPDATED_DATETIME < DATEADD(DAY, 1, @Todate))
					   AND (V.item_cd LIKE '111%' OR V.item_cd LIKE '112%' OR V.item_cd LIKE '113%')
					   AND V.EXT_COL = 'V' AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM dbo.VPOS_SCAN_ITEMS_DTL B WHERE B.EPC_TAG = V.EPC_TAG 
                                                                  AND (
                                                                       (B.EXT_COL = 'V' AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM dbo.VPOS_SCAN_ITEMS_DTL X WHERE X.EPC_TAG = B.EPC_TAG AND X.EXT_COL IS NULL))
                                                                      )
                                                           )--(SELECT 1 FROM BadEPC B WHERE B.EPC_TAG = V.EPC_TAG)
					   AND (@STORE_CODE = '' OR V.STORE_CODE = @STORE_CODE)
					   AND (@COUNTER_NO = '' OR V.COUNTER_NO = @COUNTER_NO)
					   AND (@EAN = '' OR V.SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD = @EAN)),

		 VoidCTE AS (SELECT CONVERT(DATE,LAST_UPDATED_DATETIME) AS 'VOID_DATE',STORE_CODE,COUNTER_NO,SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD,COUNT(EPC_TAG) AS VOID_QTY FROM VoidBase WHERE rn = 1 GROUP BY STORE_CODE,SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD,CONVERT(DATE,LAST_UPDATED_DATETIME),COUNTER_NO),
	  
		 EncodeCTE AS (SELECT SM.Store_Code,E.EAN,COUNT(E.Encode_Trans_ID) AS ENCODE_QTY,CONVERT(DATE,E.Encode_Date) AS ENCODE_DATE FROM dbo.tbl_Encoding_Dtl E JOIN dbo.tbl_Store_Master SM ON E.Store_ID = SM.Store_ID
					   WHERE (@fromdate ='' OR E.encode_date >= @fromdate) AND (@Todate ='' OR E.encode_date < DATEADD(DAY, 1, CAST(GETDATE() AS DATE)))
					   AND E.STORE_FLOOR_ID IS NOT NULL AND E.STORE_ID IS NOT NULL AND E.WH_ID IS NULL GROUP BY SM.Store_Code, E.EAN,CONVERT(DATE,E.Encode_Date))

		 SELECT  V.VOID_DATE,SM.Store_Code AS 'STORE_CODE',V.COUNTER_NO,ISNULL(SUM(V.VOID_QTY), 0) AS 'VOID_QTY',
		 ISNULL(SUM(CASE WHEN V.VOID_QTY < ISNULL(E.ENCODE_QTY, 0) THEN V.VOID_QTY ELSE ISNULL(E.ENCODE_QTY, 0)END), 0) AS 'ENCODE_QTY',
		 ISNULL(SUM(V.VOID_QTY), 0) - ISNULL(SUM(CASE WHEN V.VOID_QTY < ISNULL(E.ENCODE_QTY, 0) THEN V.VOID_QTY ELSE ISNULL(E.ENCODE_QTY, 0) END), 0) AS DIFFERENCE_QTY,
		 CASE WHEN ISNULL(SUM(V.VOID_QTY), 0) = ISNULL(SUM(CASE WHEN V.VOID_QTY < ISNULL(E.ENCODE_QTY, 0)THEN V.VOID_QTY ELSE ISNULL(E.ENCODE_QTY, 0)END), 0) THEN 'COMPLETE' ELSE 'PENDING'
		 END AS STATUS
	     FROM dbo.tbl_Store_Master SM 
		 JOIN VoidCTE V  ON SM.Store_Code = V.STORE_CODE
		 LEFT JOIN EncodeCTE E ON SM.Store_Code = E.Store_Code AND V.SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD = E.EAN AND V.VOID_DATE=E.ENCODE_DATE
		 WHERE (@SearchTerm = '' OR V.COUNTER_NO LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%')
		 GROUP BY V.VOID_DATE,V.COUNTER_NO, SM.Store_Code ORDER BY V.VOID_DATE DESC;
       
	END

    ELSE If(@status='SHOW_SUMMARY_DATA_FOR_VOID')
	BEGIN 
        ;WITH BadEPC AS (SELECT EPC_TAG FROM dbo.VPOS_SCAN_ITEMS_DTL WHERE (@fromdate ='' OR LAST_UPDATED_DATETIME >= @fromdate) AND (@Todate ='' OR LAST_UPDATED_DATETIME < DATEADD(DAY, 1, @Todate))
		GROUP BY EPC_TAG HAVING COUNT(CASE WHEN EXT_COL = 'V' THEN 1 END) > 0 AND COUNT(CASE WHEN EXT_COL IS NULL THEN 1 END) > 0),

        VoidBase AS (SELECT V.LAST_UPDATED_DATETIME,V.POS_TYPE,V.STORE_CODE,V.COUNTER_NO,V.SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD,V.ITEM_CD,V.EPC_TAG,ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY V.EPC_TAG, V.STORE_CODE ORDER BY V.LAST_UPDATED_DATETIME DESC) AS rn FROM dbo.VPOS_SCAN_ITEMS_DTL V
                   WHERE LAST_UPDATED_DATETIME >= @BILL_DATE AND LAST_UPDATED_DATETIME < DATEADD(DAY, 1, @BILL_DATE)
				   AND (V.item_cd LIKE '111%' OR V.item_cd LIKE '112%' OR V.item_cd LIKE '113%')
                   AND V.EXT_COL = 'V' AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM BadEPC B WHERE B.EPC_TAG = V.EPC_TAG)
                   AND (@STORE_CODE = '' OR V.STORE_CODE = @STORE_CODE)
                   AND (@COUNTER_NO = '' OR V.COUNTER_NO = @COUNTER_NO)
                   AND (@EAN = '' OR V.SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD = @EAN)),

        VoidCTE AS (SELECT CONVERT(DATE,LAST_UPDATED_DATETIME) AS 'VOID_DATE',POS_TYPE,STORE_CODE,COUNTER_NO,SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD,ITEM_CD,COUNT(EPC_TAG) AS VOID_QTY FROM VoidBase WHERE rn = 1 GROUP BY STORE_CODE,SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD,CONVERT(DATE,LAST_UPDATED_DATETIME),POS_TYPE,ITEM_CD,COUNTER_NO),
	  
        EncodeCTE AS (SELECT SM.Store_Code,E.EAN,COUNT(E.Encode_Trans_ID) AS ENCODE_QTY,CONVERT(DATE,E.Encode_Date) AS ENCODE_DATE FROM dbo.tbl_Encoding_Dtl E JOIN dbo.tbl_Store_Master SM ON E.Store_ID = SM.Store_ID
                   WHERE (E.encode_date >= @BILL_DATE) AND (E.encode_date < DATEADD(DAY, 1, CAST(GETDATE() AS DATE)))
                   AND E.STORE_FLOOR_ID IS NOT NULL AND E.STORE_ID IS NOT NULL AND E.WH_ID IS NULL GROUP BY SM.Store_Code, E.EAN,CONVERT(DATE,E.Encode_Date))

        SELECT  V.VOID_DATE,V.POS_TYPE,SM.Store_Code AS 'STORE_CODE',V.COUNTER_NO,V.SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD AS 'EAN',V.ITEM_CD AS 'MATERIAL',ISNULL(SUM(V.VOID_QTY), 0) AS 'VOID_QTY',
        ISNULL(SUM(CASE WHEN V.VOID_QTY < ISNULL(E.ENCODE_QTY, 0) THEN V.VOID_QTY ELSE ISNULL(E.ENCODE_QTY, 0)END), 0) AS 'ENCODE_QTY',
        ISNULL(SUM(V.VOID_QTY), 0) - ISNULL(SUM(CASE WHEN V.VOID_QTY < ISNULL(E.ENCODE_QTY, 0) THEN V.VOID_QTY ELSE ISNULL(E.ENCODE_QTY, 0) END), 0) AS 'DIFFERENCE_QTY',
        CASE WHEN ISNULL(SUM(V.VOID_QTY), 0) = ISNULL(SUM(CASE WHEN V.VOID_QTY < ISNULL(E.ENCODE_QTY, 0)THEN V.VOID_QTY ELSE ISNULL(E.ENCODE_QTY, 0)END), 0) THEN 'COMPLETE' ELSE 'PENDING'
        END AS STATUS
	    INTO #VOIDDATASUMMARY
        FROM dbo.tbl_Store_Master SM 
	    JOIN VoidCTE V  ON SM.Store_Code = V.STORE_CODE
        LEFT JOIN EncodeCTE E ON SM.Store_Code = E.Store_Code AND V.SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD = E.EAN AND V.VOID_DATE=E.ENCODE_DATE
		WHERE (@SearchTerm = '' OR V.SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%' OR V.ITEM_CD LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%')
        GROUP BY  V.VOID_DATE,V.POS_TYPE, SM.Store_Code,V.COUNTER_NO,V.SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD,V.ITEM_CD ORDER BY V.VOID_DATE DESC;

		SELECT @RecordCount = COUNT(*),@QTY = SUM(VOID_QTY),@ENCQTY = SUM(ENCODE_QTY),@DIFFQTY = SUM(DIFFERENCE_QTY) FROM #VOIDDATASUMMARY;

        SET @SortDirection =
				CASE 
					WHEN LOWER(@SortDirection) = 'desc' THEN 'DESC'
					ELSE 'ASC'
				END;

			SET @OrderBy =	
				CASE @SortColumn
					WHEN 'STORE_CODE' THEN 'R.STORE_CODE'
					WHEN 'POS_TYPE' THEN 'R.POS_TYPE'
					WHEN 'COUNTER_NO' THEN 'R.COUNTER_NO'
					WHEN 'EAN' THEN 'R.EAN'
					WHEN 'MATERIAL' THEN 'R.MATERIAL'
					WHEN 'VOID_QTY' THEN 'R.VOID_QTY'
					WHEN 'ENCODE_QTY' THEN 'R.ENCODE_QTY'
					WHEN 'DIFFERENCE_QTY' THEN 'R.DIFFERENCE_QTY'
					WHEN 'STATUS' THEN 'R.STATUS'
					ELSE 'R.VOID_DATE'
				END
				+ ' ' + @SortDirection
				+ ', R.VOID_DATE';
			
            SET @SQL = N'SELECT *FROM (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY ' + @OrderBy + N') AS RowNumber,* FROM #VOIDDATASUMMARY R ) T
			WHERE RowNumber BETWEEN @StartRow AND @EndRow
			ORDER BY RowNumber;';

			EXEC sp_executesql
			@SQL,N'@StartRow INT, @EndRow INT',
			@StartRow = @StartRow,	@EndRow   = @EndRow;
		-- Clean up the temporary table

		DROP TABLE #VOIDDATASUMMARY;
	END

	ELSE If(@status='EXPORT_SHOW_SUMMARY_DATA_FOR_VOID')
	BEGIN 
        ;WITH BadEPC AS (SELECT EPC_TAG FROM dbo.VPOS_SCAN_ITEMS_DTL WHERE (@fromdate ='' OR LAST_UPDATED_DATETIME >= @fromdate) AND (@Todate ='' OR LAST_UPDATED_DATETIME < DATEADD(DAY, 1, @Todate))
		GROUP BY EPC_TAG HAVING COUNT(CASE WHEN EXT_COL = 'V' THEN 1 END) > 0 AND COUNT(CASE WHEN EXT_COL IS NULL THEN 1 END) > 0),

        VoidBase AS (SELECT V.LAST_UPDATED_DATETIME,V.POS_TYPE,V.STORE_CODE,V.COUNTER_NO,V.SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD,V.ITEM_CD,V.EPC_TAG,ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY V.EPC_TAG, V.STORE_CODE ORDER BY V.LAST_UPDATED_DATETIME DESC) AS rn FROM dbo.VPOS_SCAN_ITEMS_DTL V
                   WHERE LAST_UPDATED_DATETIME >= @BILL_DATE AND LAST_UPDATED_DATETIME < DATEADD(DAY, 1, @BILL_DATE)
				   AND (V.item_cd LIKE '111%' OR V.item_cd LIKE '112%' OR V.item_cd LIKE '113%')
                   AND V.EXT_COL = 'V' AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM BadEPC B WHERE B.EPC_TAG = V.EPC_TAG)
                   AND (@STORE_CODE = '' OR V.STORE_CODE = @STORE_CODE)
                   AND (@COUNTER_NO = '' OR V.COUNTER_NO = @COUNTER_NO)
                   AND (@EAN = '' OR V.SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD = @EAN)),

        VoidCTE AS (SELECT CONVERT(DATE,LAST_UPDATED_DATETIME) AS 'VOID_DATE',POS_TYPE,STORE_CODE,COUNTER_NO,SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD,ITEM_CD,COUNT(EPC_TAG) AS VOID_QTY FROM VoidBase WHERE rn = 1 GROUP BY STORE_CODE,SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD,CONVERT(DATE,LAST_UPDATED_DATETIME),POS_TYPE,ITEM_CD,COUNTER_NO),
	  
        EncodeCTE AS (SELECT SM.Store_Code,E.EAN,COUNT(E.Encode_Trans_ID) AS ENCODE_QTY,CONVERT(DATE,E.Encode_Date) AS ENCODE_DATE FROM dbo.tbl_Encoding_Dtl E JOIN dbo.tbl_Store_Master SM ON E.Store_ID = SM.Store_ID
                   WHERE (E.encode_date >= @BILL_DATE) AND (E.encode_date < DATEADD(DAY, 1, CAST(GETDATE() AS DATE)))
                   AND E.STORE_FLOOR_ID IS NOT NULL AND E.STORE_ID IS NOT NULL AND E.WH_ID IS NULL GROUP BY SM.Store_Code, E.EAN,CONVERT(DATE,E.Encode_Date))

        SELECT  V.VOID_DATE,V.POS_TYPE,SM.Store_Code AS 'STORE_CODE',V.COUNTER_NO,V.SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD AS 'EAN',V.ITEM_CD AS 'MATERIAL',ISNULL(SUM(V.VOID_QTY), 0) AS 'VOID_QTY',
        ISNULL(SUM(CASE WHEN V.VOID_QTY < ISNULL(E.ENCODE_QTY, 0) THEN V.VOID_QTY ELSE ISNULL(E.ENCODE_QTY, 0)END), 0) AS 'ENCODE_QTY',
        ISNULL(SUM(V.VOID_QTY), 0) - ISNULL(SUM(CASE WHEN V.VOID_QTY < ISNULL(E.ENCODE_QTY, 0) THEN V.VOID_QTY ELSE ISNULL(E.ENCODE_QTY, 0) END), 0) AS 'DIFFERENCE_QTY',
        CASE WHEN ISNULL(SUM(V.VOID_QTY), 0) = ISNULL(SUM(CASE WHEN V.VOID_QTY < ISNULL(E.ENCODE_QTY, 0)THEN V.VOID_QTY ELSE ISNULL(E.ENCODE_QTY, 0)END), 0) THEN 'COMPLETE' ELSE 'PENDING'
        END AS STATUS
        FROM dbo.tbl_Store_Master SM 
	    JOIN VoidCTE V  ON SM.Store_Code = V.STORE_CODE
        LEFT JOIN EncodeCTE E ON SM.Store_Code = E.Store_Code AND V.SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD = E.EAN AND V.VOID_DATE=E.ENCODE_DATE
		WHERE (@SearchTerm = '' OR V.SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%' OR V.ITEM_CD LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%')
        GROUP BY  V.VOID_DATE,V.POS_TYPE, SM.Store_Code,V.COUNTER_NO,V.SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD,V.ITEM_CD ORDER BY V.VOID_DATE DESC;
	END

	------------FOR STORE QTY----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

	ELSE If(@status='BIND_MATERIAL_FOR_STORE_ENCODING')
	BEGIN
		select DISTINCT top 10 dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].Material as 'ARTICLE' from dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl] 
		LEFT JOIN dbo.[tbl_Ean_Mat_Mst] ON dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].ean=dbo.[tbl_Ean_Mat_Mst].EAN_UPC
		LEFT JOIN dbo.[tbl_Material_Master] ON dbo.[tbl_Ean_Mat_Mst].Material=dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].Material
		LEFT JOIN dbo.tbl_Store_Master  ON dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Store_ID = dbo.tbl_Store_Master.Store_ID
		where dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Store_ID IS NOT NULL AND Store_Floor_ID IS NOT NULL 
		AND convert (date,dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Encode_Date,103) between 
			   convert (date,COALESCE(NULLIF(@fromdate, ''),dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Encode_date),103)
			  and convert (date,COALESCE(NULLIF(@Todate, ''),dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Encode_date),103)
		AND dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].Material IS NOT NULL
		AND dbo.[tbl_Store_Master].Store_Code = COALESCE(NULLIF(@Store_Code, ''), dbo.[tbl_Store_Master].Store_Code)
		AND(@SearchTerm = '' OR dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].Material LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%');
	END

	ELSE If(@status='SEARCH_BIND_MATERIAL_FOR_STORE_ENCODING')
	BEGIN
		select DISTINCT dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].Material as 'ARTICLE' from dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl] 
		LEFT JOIN dbo.[tbl_Ean_Mat_Mst] ON dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].ean=dbo.[tbl_Ean_Mat_Mst].EAN_UPC
		LEFT JOIN dbo.[tbl_Material_Master] ON dbo.[tbl_Ean_Mat_Mst].Material=dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].Material
		LEFT JOIN dbo.tbl_Store_Master ON dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Store_ID = dbo.tbl_Store_Master.Store_ID
		where dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Store_ID IS NOT NULL AND Store_Floor_ID IS NOT NULL 
		AND convert (date,dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Encode_Date,103) between 
			   convert (date,COALESCE(NULLIF(@fromdate, ''),dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Encode_date),103)
			  and convert (date,COALESCE(NULLIF(@Todate, ''),dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Encode_date),103)
		AND dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].Material IS NOT NULL
		AND dbo.[tbl_Store_Master].Store_Code = COALESCE(NULLIF(@Store_Code, ''), dbo.[tbl_Store_Master].Store_Code)
