		AND(@SearchTerm = '' OR dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].Material LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%');
	END

	
	ELSE If(@status='BIND_EAN_FOR_STORE_ENCODING')
	BEGIN

		LEFT JOIN dbo.[tbl_Ean_Mat_Mst] ON dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].ean=dbo.[tbl_Ean_Mat_Mst].EAN_UPC

		LEFT JOIN dbo.tbl_Store_Master  ON dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Store_ID = dbo.tbl_Store_Master.Store_ID
		wHERE dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Store_ID IS NOT NULL AND Store_Floor_ID IS NOT NULL

		and dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].Material is not null
		AND dbo.[tbl_Store_Master].Store_Code = COALESCE(NULLIF(@Store_Code, ''), dbo.[tbl_Store_Master].Store_code)
		AND(@SearchTerm = '' OR dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].EAN LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%');
    END

    ELSE If(@status='SEARCH_BIND_EAN_FOR_STORE_ENCODING')
    BEGIN
		SELECT DISTINCT dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].EAN  from dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl]
		LEFT JOIN dbo.[tbl_Ean_Mat_Mst] ON dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].ean=dbo.[tbl_Ean_Mat_Mst].EAN_UPC
		LEFT JOIN dbo.[tbl_Material_Master] ON dbo.[tbl_Ean_Mat_Mst].Material=dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].Material
		LEFT JOIN dbo.tbl_Store_Master  ON dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Store_ID = dbo.tbl_Store_Master.Store_ID
		wHERE dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Store_ID IS NOT NULL AND Store_Floor_ID IS NOT NULL
		AND dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].Material = COALESCE(NULLIF(@Material, ''), dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].Material)
		AND convert (date,dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Encode_Date,103) between 
			convert (date,COALESCE(NULLIF(@fromdate, ''),dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Encode_date),103)
			and convert (date,COALESCE(NULLIF(@Todate, ''),dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Encode_date),103)
		and dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].Material is not null
		AND dbo.[tbl_Store_Master].Store_Code = COALESCE(NULLIF(@Store_Code, ''), dbo.[tbl_Store_Master].Store_code)
		AND(@SearchTerm = '' OR dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].EAN LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%');
    END

	ELSE If(@status='SHOW_DATA_FOR_STORE_ENCODING')
    BEGIN   
		SELECT  --ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY FORMAT(dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Encode_Date,'yyyy-MM-dd') DESC) AS RowNumber,
		dbo.[tbl_Store_Master].Store_Code AS 'Store_Code',
		dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].EAN,
		COUNT(distinct dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Encode_EPC) AS 'ENCODING_Tags',
		ISNULL(dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].Material, 'NA') AS 'ARTICLE',
		ISNULL(dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].ART_DESC, 'NA') AS 'ARTICLE_DESC',
		FORMAT(dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Encode_Date,'yyyy-MM-dd') as 'Encode_Date'
		INTO #ENCODINGFORSTORE
		FROM dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl]
		LEFT JOIN dbo.[tbl_Ean_Mat_Mst] ON dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].ean = dbo.[tbl_Ean_Mat_Mst].EAN_UPC
		LEFT JOIN dbo.[tbl_Material_Master] ON dbo.[tbl_Ean_Mat_Mst].Material = dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].Material
		LEFT JOIN dbo.[tbl_Store_Master]  ON dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Store_ID = dbo.[tbl_Store_Master].Store_ID
		WHERE dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Store_ID IS NOT NULL AND Store_Floor_ID IS NOT NULL
		AND dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].EAN = COALESCE(NULLIF(@EAN, ''), dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].EAN)
		AND  ((@Material='' and (dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].Material IS NULL)) 
		OR dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].Material = COALESCE(NULLIF(@material, ''), dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].Material))
		AND convert (date,dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Encode_Date,103) between 
				convert (date,COALESCE(NULLIF(@fromdate, ''),dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Encode_date),103)
				and convert (date,COALESCE(NULLIF(@Todate, ''),dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Encode_date),103)
		AND dbo.[tbl_Store_Master].Store_Code = COALESCE(NULLIF(@Store_Code, ''), dbo.[tbl_Store_Master].Store_code)
		AND
		(@SearchTerm = '' OR dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].Material LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%' 
		OR dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].ART_DESC LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%'
		OR dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].EAN LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%'
		OR FORMAT(dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Encode_Date,'yyyy-MM-dd') LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%')
		GROUP BY dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].EAN,dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].Material,dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].ART_DESC,FORMAT(dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Encode_Date,'yyyy-MM-dd'),
		dbo.[tbl_Store_Master].Store_Code

		-- Get the total record count
		SELECT @RecordCount = COUNT(*) FROM #ENCODINGFORSTORE;
		SELECT @TotalCount = SUM(ENCODING_Tags) FROM #ENCODINGFORSTORE;


		SET @SortDirection =
				CASE 
					WHEN LOWER(@SortDirection) = 'desc' THEN 'DESC'
					ELSE 'ASC'
				END;

			SET @OrderBy =	
				CASE @SortColumn
					WHEN 'Store_Code' THEN 'R.Store_Code' 
					WHEN 'EAN' THEN 'R.EAN' 
					WHEN 'ENCODING_Tags' THEN 'R.ENCODING_Tags'
					WHEN 'Encode_Date' THEN 'R.Encode_Date'
					WHEN 'ARTICLE_DESC' THEN 'R.ARTICLE_DESC'
					ELSE 'R.ARTICLE'
				END
				+ ' ' + @SortDirection
				+ ', R.ARTICLE ASC';
			
           	SET @SQL = N'SELECT *FROM (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY ' + @OrderBy + N') AS RowNumber,* FROM #ENCODINGFORSTORE R ) T
			WHERE RowNumber BETWEEN @StartRow AND @EndRow
			ORDER BY RowNumber;';

				EXEC sp_executesql	@SQL,N'@StartRow INT, @EndRow INT',
			@StartRow = @StartRow,	@EndRow   = @EndRow;


		-- Clean up the temporary table
		DROP TABLE #ENCODINGFORSTORE;
    END

		ELSE If(@status='EXPORT_SHOW_DATA_FOR_STORE_ENCODING')
    BEGIN   
		SELECT  ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY FORMAT(dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Encode_Date,'yyyy-MM-dd') DESC) AS RowNumber,
		dbo.[tbl_Store_Master].Store_Code AS 'Store_Code',dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].EAN,COUNT(distinct dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Encode_EPC) AS 'ENCODING_Tags',
		ISNULL(dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].Material, 'NA') AS 'ARTICLE',ISNULL(dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].ART_DESC, 'NA') AS 'ARTICLE_DESC',
		FORMAT(dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Encode_Date,'yyyy-MM-dd') as 'Encode_Date'
        FROM dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl]
		LEFT JOIN dbo.[tbl_Ean_Mat_Mst] ON dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].ean = dbo.[tbl_Ean_Mat_Mst].EAN_UPC
		LEFT JOIN dbo.[tbl_Material_Master] ON dbo.[tbl_Ean_Mat_Mst].Material = dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].Material
		LEFT JOIN dbo.[tbl_Store_Master]  ON dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Store_ID = dbo.[tbl_Store_Master].Store_ID
		WHERE dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Store_ID IS NOT NULL AND Store_Floor_ID IS NOT NULL
		AND dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].EAN = COALESCE(NULLIF(@EAN, ''), dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].EAN)
		AND  ((@Material='' and (dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].Material IS NULL)) 
		OR dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].Material = COALESCE(NULLIF(@material, ''), dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].Material))
        AND convert (date,dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Encode_Date,103) between 
				convert (date,COALESCE(NULLIF(@fromdate, ''),dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Encode_date),103)
				and convert (date,COALESCE(NULLIF(@Todate, ''),dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Encode_date),103)
        AND dbo.[tbl_Store_Master].Store_Code = COALESCE(NULLIF(@Store_Code, ''), dbo.[tbl_Store_Master].Store_code)
		GROUP BY dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].EAN,dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].Material,dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].ART_DESC,FORMAT(dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Encode_Date,'yyyy-MM-dd'),
		dbo.[tbl_Store_Master].Store_Code;
    END

	ELSE If(@status='VIEW_SHOW_DATA_FOR_STORE_ENCODING')
    BEGIN	
		SELECT --ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY FORMAT(dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Encode_Date,'yyyy-MM-dd') DESC) AS RowNumber,
		dbo.[tbl_Store_Master].Store_Code,
		dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].EAN,
		dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Encode_EPC,
		CONVERT (varchar (19),dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Encode_Date,120) as 'Encode_Date',
		ISNULL(dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].Material, 'NA') AS 'ARTICLE',
		ISNULL(dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].ART_DESC, 'NA') AS 'ARTICLE_DESC'
		INTO #VIEWENCODINGFORSTORE
		from dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl] 
		LEFT JOIN dbo.[tbl_Ean_Mat_Mst] ON dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].ean = dbo.[tbl_Ean_Mat_Mst].EAN_UPC
		LEFT JOIN dbo.[tbl_Material_Master] ON dbo.[tbl_Ean_Mat_Mst].Material = dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].Material
		LEFT JOIN dbo.[tbl_Store_Master]  ON dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Store_ID = dbo.[tbl_Store_Master].Store_ID
		where dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Store_ID IS NOT NULL AND Store_Floor_ID IS NOT NULL
		AND dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].EAN = COALESCE(NULLIF(@EAN, ''), dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].EAN) 
		AND convert (date,dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Encode_Date,103) between 
			convert (date,COALESCE(NULLIF(@fromdate, ''),dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Encode_date),103)
			and convert (date,COALESCE(NULLIF(@Todate, ''),dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Encode_date),103)
		AND ((@Material='' and (dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].Material IS NULL)) 
			OR dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].Material = COALESCE(NULLIF(@material, ''), dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].Material))
		AND dbo.[tbl_Store_Master].Store_Code = COALESCE(NULLIF(@Store_Code, ''), dbo.[tbl_Store_Master].Store_code)
		ORDER BY convert (varchar (19),dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Encode_Date,120) DESC;

		SELECT @RecordCount = COUNT(*) FROM #VIEWENCODINGFORSTORE;
		SELECT @TotalCount = COUNT(Encode_EPC) FROM #VIEWENCODINGFORSTORE;


		
			SET @OrderBy =	
				CASE @SortColumn
					WHEN 'Store_Code' THEN 'R.Store_Code' 
					WHEN 'EAN' THEN 'R.EAN' 
					WHEN 'Encode_EPC' THEN 'R.Encode_EPC'
					WHEN 'Encode_Date' THEN 'R.Encode_Date'
					WHEN 'ARTICLE_DESC' THEN 'R.ARTICLE_DESC'
					ELSE 'R.ARTICLE'
				END
				+ ' ' + @SortDirection
				+ ', R.ARTICLE ASC';
			
           	SET @SQL = N'SELECT *FROM (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY ' + @OrderBy + N') AS RowNumber,* FROM #VIEWENCODINGFORSTORE R ) T
			WHERE RowNumber BETWEEN @StartRow AND @EndRow
			ORDER BY RowNumber;';

			EXEC sp_executesql	@SQL,N'@StartRow INT, @EndRow INT',
			@StartRow = @StartRow,	@EndRow   = @EndRow;


		-- Clean up the temporary table
		DROP TABLE #VIEWENCODINGFORSTORE;
    END

	------------FOR STORE QTY----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

	ELSE If(@status='BIND_MATERIAL_FOR_STOCK')
	BEGIN
		select DISTINCT top 10 dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].Material as 'ARTICLE' from dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl] 
		LEFT JOIN dbo.[tbl_Ean_Mat_Mst] ON dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].ean=dbo.[tbl_Ean_Mat_Mst].EAN_UPC
		LEFT JOIN dbo.[tbl_Material_Master] ON dbo.[tbl_Ean_Mat_Mst].Material=dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].Material
		LEFT JOIN dbo.tbl_Store_Master  ON dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Store_ID = dbo.tbl_Store_Master.Store_ID
		where dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Is_Status IN (1,2)
		AND convert (date,dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Encode_Date,103) between 
			convert (date,COALESCE(NULLIF(@fromdate, ''),dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Encode_date),103)
			and convert (date,COALESCE(NULLIF(@Todate, ''),dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Encode_date),103)
		AND dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].Material IS NOT NULL
		AND dbo.[tbl_Store_Master].Store_Code = COALESCE(NULLIF(@Store_Code, ''), dbo.[tbl_Store_Master].Store_Code)
		AND(@SearchTerm = '' OR dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].Material LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%');
	END

	ELSE If(@status='SEARCH_BIND_MATERIAL_FOR_STOCK')
	BEGIN
		select DISTINCT dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].Material as 'ARTICLE' from dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl] 
		LEFT JOIN dbo.[tbl_Ean_Mat_Mst] ON dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].ean=dbo.[tbl_Ean_Mat_Mst].EAN_UPC
		LEFT JOIN dbo.[tbl_Material_Master] ON dbo.[tbl_Ean_Mat_Mst].Material=dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].Material
		LEFT JOIN dbo.tbl_Store_Master ON dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Store_ID = dbo.tbl_Store_Master.Store_ID
		where dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Is_Status IN (1,2)
		AND convert (date,dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Encode_Date,103) between 
			convert (date,COALESCE(NULLIF(@fromdate, ''),dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Encode_date),103)
			and convert (date,COALESCE(NULLIF(@Todate, ''),dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Encode_date),103)
		AND dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].Material IS NOT NULL
		AND dbo.[tbl_Store_Master].Store_Code = COALESCE(NULLIF(@Store_Code, ''), dbo.[tbl_Store_Master].Store_Code)
		AND(@SearchTerm = '' OR dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].Material LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%');
	END

	ELSE If(@status='BIND_EAN_FOR_STOCK')
	BEGIN
		SELECT DISTINCT TOP 10 dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].EAN  from dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl]
		LEFT JOIN dbo.[tbl_Ean_Mat_Mst] ON dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].ean=dbo.[tbl_Ean_Mat_Mst].EAN_UPC
		LEFT JOIN dbo.[tbl_Material_Master] ON dbo.[tbl_Ean_Mat_Mst].Material=dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].Material
		LEFT JOIN dbo.tbl_Store_Master  ON dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Store_ID = dbo.tbl_Store_Master.Store_ID
		wHERE  dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Is_Status IN (1,2)
		AND dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].Material = COALESCE(NULLIF(@Material, ''), dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].Material)
		AND convert (date,dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Encode_Date,103) between 
			convert (date,COALESCE(NULLIF(@fromdate, ''),dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Encode_date),103)
			and convert (date,COALESCE(NULLIF(@Todate, ''),dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Encode_date),103)
		and dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].Material is not null
		AND dbo.[tbl_Store_Master].Store_Code = COALESCE(NULLIF(@Store_Code, ''), dbo.[tbl_Store_Master].Store_code)
		AND(@SearchTerm = '' OR dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].EAN LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%');
    END

    ELSE If(@status='SEARCH_BIND_EAN_FOR_STOCK')
    BEGIN
		SELECT DISTINCT dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].EAN  from dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl]
		LEFT JOIN dbo.[tbl_Ean_Mat_Mst] ON dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].ean=dbo.[tbl_Ean_Mat_Mst].EAN_UPC
		LEFT JOIN dbo.[tbl_Material_Master] ON dbo.[tbl_Ean_Mat_Mst].Material=dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].Material
		LEFT JOIN dbo.tbl_Store_Master  ON dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Store_ID = dbo.tbl_Store_Master.Store_ID
		wHERE dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Is_Status IN (1,2)
		AND dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].Material = COALESCE(NULLIF(@Material, ''), dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].Material)
		AND convert (date,dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Encode_Date,103) between 
			convert (date,COALESCE(NULLIF(@fromdate, ''),dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Encode_date),103)
			and convert (date,COALESCE(NULLIF(@Todate, ''),dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Encode_date),103)
		and dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].Material is not null
		AND dbo.[tbl_Store_Master].Store_Code = COALESCE(NULLIF(@Store_Code, ''), dbo.[tbl_Store_Master].Store_code)
		AND(@SearchTerm = '' OR dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].EAN LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%');
    END

	ELSE If(@status='SHOW_DATA_FOR_STOCK')
    BEGIN   
		SELECT  --ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY FORMAT(dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Encode_Date,'yyyy-MM-dd') DESC) AS RowNumber,
		dbo.[tbl_Store_Master].Store_Code AS 'Store_Code',dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].EAN,COUNT(distinct dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Encode_EPC) AS 'ENCODING_Tags',
		ISNULL(dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].Material, 'NA') AS 'ARTICLE',ISNULL(dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].ART_DESC, 'NA') AS 'ARTICLE_DESC',
		FORMAT(dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Encode_Date,'yyyy-MM-dd') as 'Encode_Date'
		INTO #STOCK
		FROM dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl]
		LEFT JOIN dbo.[tbl_Ean_Mat_Mst] ON dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].ean = dbo.[tbl_Ean_Mat_Mst].EAN_UPC
		LEFT JOIN dbo.[tbl_Material_Master] ON dbo.[tbl_Ean_Mat_Mst].Material = dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].Material
		LEFT JOIN dbo.[tbl_Store_Master]  ON dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Store_ID = dbo.[tbl_Store_Master].Store_ID
		WHERE dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Is_Status IN (1,2)
		AND dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].EAN = COALESCE(NULLIF(@EAN, ''), dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].EAN)
		AND  ((@Material='' and (dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].Material IS NULL)) 
		OR dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].Material = COALESCE(NULLIF(@material, ''), dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].Material))
		AND convert (date,dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Encode_Date,103) between 
				convert (date,COALESCE(NULLIF(@fromdate, ''),dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Encode_date),103)
				and convert (date,COALESCE(NULLIF(@Todate, ''),dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Encode_date),103)
		AND dbo.[tbl_Store_Master].Store_Code = COALESCE(NULLIF(@Store_Code, ''), dbo.[tbl_Store_Master].Store_code)
		AND
		(@SearchTerm = '' OR dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].Material LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%' 
		OR dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].ART_DESC LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%'
		OR dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].EAN LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%'
		OR FORMAT(dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Encode_Date,'yyyy-MM-dd') LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%')
		GROUP BY dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].EAN,dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].Material,dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].ART_DESC,FORMAT(dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Encode_Date,'yyyy-MM-dd'),
		dbo.[tbl_Store_Master].Store_Code

		-- Get the total record count
		SELECT @RecordCount = COUNT(*) FROM #STOCK;
		SELECT @TotalCount = SUM(ENCODING_Tags) FROM #STOCK;


			SET @SortDirection =
				CASE 
					WHEN LOWER(@SortDirection) = 'desc' THEN 'DESC'
					ELSE 'ASC'
				END;

			SET @OrderBy =	
				CASE @SortColumn
					WHEN 'EAN' THEN 'R.EAN' 
					WHEN 'ENCODING_Tags' THEN 'R.ENCODING_Tags' 
					WHEN 'STORE_CODE' THEN 'R.STORE_CODE'
					WHEN 'ARTICLE' THEN 'R.ARTICLE'
					WHEN 'ARTICLE_DESC' THEN 'R.ARTICLE_DESC'
					ELSE 'R.Encode_Date'
				END
				+ ' ' + @SortDirection
				+ ', R.Encode_Date ASC';
			
           	SET @SQL = N'SELECT *FROM (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY ' + @OrderBy + N') AS RowNumber,* FROM #STOCK R ) T
			WHERE RowNumber BETWEEN @StartRow AND @EndRow
			ORDER BY RowNumber;';

			EXEC sp_executesql	@SQL,N'@StartRow INT, @EndRow INT',
			@StartRow = @StartRow,	@EndRow   = @EndRow;


		-- Clean up the temporary table
		DROP TABLE #STOCK;
    END

	ELSE If(@status='EXPORT_SHOW_DATA_FOR_STOCK')
    BEGIN   
		SELECT  ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY FORMAT(dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Encode_Date,'yyyy-MM-dd') DESC) AS RowNumber,
		dbo.[tbl_Store_Master].Store_Code AS 'Store_Code',dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].EAN,COUNT(distinct dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Encode_EPC) AS 'ENCODING_Tags',
		ISNULL(dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].Material, 'NA') AS 'ARTICLE',ISNULL(dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].ART_DESC, 'NA') AS 'ARTICLE_DESC',
		FORMAT(dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Encode_Date,'yyyy-MM-dd') as 'Encode_Date'
        FROM dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl]
		LEFT JOIN dbo.[tbl_Ean_Mat_Mst] ON dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].ean = dbo.[tbl_Ean_Mat_Mst].EAN_UPC
		LEFT JOIN dbo.[tbl_Material_Master] ON dbo.[tbl_Ean_Mat_Mst].Material = dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].Material
		LEFT JOIN dbo.[tbl_Store_Master]  ON dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Store_ID = dbo.[tbl_Store_Master].Store_ID
		WHERE dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Is_Status IN (1,2)
		AND dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].EAN = COALESCE(NULLIF(@EAN, ''), dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].EAN)
		AND  ((@Material='' and (dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].Material IS NULL)) 
		OR dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].Material = COALESCE(NULLIF(@material, ''), dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].Material))
		AND convert (date,dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Encode_Date,103) between 
				convert (date,COALESCE(NULLIF(@fromdate, ''),dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Encode_date),103)
				and convert (date,COALESCE(NULLIF(@Todate, ''),dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Encode_date),103)
		AND dbo.[tbl_Store_Master].Store_Code = COALESCE(NULLIF(@Store_Code, ''), dbo.[tbl_Store_Master].Store_code)
		GROUP BY dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].EAN,dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].Material,dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].ART_DESC,FORMAT(dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Encode_Date,'yyyy-MM-dd'),
		dbo.[tbl_Store_Master].Store_Code
    END

	ELSE If(@status='VIEW_SHOW_DATA_FOR_STOCK')
    BEGIN	
		SELECT --ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY FORMAT(dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Encode_Date,'yyyy-MM-dd') DESC) AS RowNumber,
		dbo.[tbl_Store_Master].Store_Code,dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].EAN,dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Encode_EPC,
		CONVERT (varchar (19),dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Encode_Date,120) as 'Encode_Date',ISNULL(dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].Material, 'NA') AS 'ARTICLE',
		ISNULL(dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].ART_DESC, 'NA') AS 'ARTICLE_DESC'
		INTO #VIEWSTOCK
		from dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl] 
		LEFT JOIN dbo.[tbl_Ean_Mat_Mst] ON dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].ean = dbo.[tbl_Ean_Mat_Mst].EAN_UPC
		LEFT JOIN dbo.[tbl_Material_Master] ON dbo.[tbl_Ean_Mat_Mst].Material = dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].Material
		LEFT JOIN dbo.[tbl_Store_Master]  ON dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Store_ID = dbo.[tbl_Store_Master].Store_ID
		where dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Is_Status IN (1,2)
		AND dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].EAN = COALESCE(NULLIF(@EAN, ''), dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].EAN) 
		AND convert (date,dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Encode_Date,103) between 
			convert (date,COALESCE(NULLIF(@fromdate, ''),dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Encode_date),103)
			and convert (date,COALESCE(NULLIF(@Todate, ''),dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Encode_date),103)
		AND ((@Material='' and (dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].Material IS NULL)) 
			OR dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].Material = COALESCE(NULLIF(@material, ''), dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].Material))
		AND dbo.[tbl_Store_Master].Store_Code = COALESCE(NULLIF(@Store_Code, ''), dbo.[tbl_Store_Master].Store_code)
		ORDER BY convert (varchar (19),dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Encode_Date,120) DESC;

		SELECT @RecordCount = COUNT(*) FROM #VIEWSTOCK;
		SELECT @TotalCount = COUNT(Encode_EPC) FROM #VIEWSTOCK;


			SET @SortDirection =
				CASE 
					WHEN LOWER(@SortDirection) = 'desc' THEN 'DESC'
					ELSE 'ASC'
				END;

			SET @OrderBy =	
				CASE @SortColumn
					WHEN 'EAN' THEN 'R.EAN' 
					WHEN 'STORE_CODE' THEN 'R.STORE_CODE'
					WHEN 'ARTICLE' THEN 'R.ARTICLE'
					WHEN 'ARTICLE_DESC' THEN 'R.ARTICLE_DESC'
					WHEN  'Encode_EPC' THEN 'Encode_EPC'
					ELSE 'R.Encode_Date'
				END
				+ ' ' + @SortDirection
				+ ', R.Encode_Date ASC';
			
           	SET @SQL = N'SELECT *FROM (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY ' + @OrderBy + N') AS RowNumber,* FROM #VIEWSTOCK R ) T
			WHERE RowNumber BETWEEN @StartRow AND @EndRow
			ORDER BY RowNumber;';

			EXEC sp_executesql	@SQL,N'@StartRow INT, @EndRow INT',
			@StartRow = @StartRow,	@EndRow   = @EndRow;


		-- Clean up the temporary table
		DROP TABLE #VIEWSTOCK;
    END
	ELSE If(@status='EXPORT_VIEW_SHOW_DATA_FOR_STOCK')
    BEGIN	
		SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY FORMAT(dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Encode_Date,'yyyy-MM-dd') DESC) AS RowNumber,
		dbo.[tbl_Store_Master].Store_Code,dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].EAN,dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Encode_EPC,
		CONVERT (varchar (19),dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Encode_Date,120) as 'Encode_Date',ISNULL(dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].Material, 'NA') AS 'ARTICLE',
		ISNULL(dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].ART_DESC, 'NA') AS 'ARTICLE_DESC'
		from dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl] 
		LEFT JOIN dbo.[tbl_Ean_Mat_Mst] ON dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].ean = dbo.[tbl_Ean_Mat_Mst].EAN_UPC
		LEFT JOIN dbo.[tbl_Material_Master] ON dbo.[tbl_Ean_Mat_Mst].Material = dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].Material
		LEFT JOIN dbo.[tbl_Store_Master]  ON dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Store_ID = dbo.[tbl_Store_Master].Store_ID
		where dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Is_Status IN (1,2)
		AND dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].EAN = COALESCE(NULLIF(@EAN, ''), dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].EAN) 
		AND convert (date,dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Encode_Date,103) between 
			convert (date,COALESCE(NULLIF(@BILL_DATE, ''),dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Encode_date),103)
			and convert (date,COALESCE(NULLIF(@BILL_DATE, ''),dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Encode_date),103)
		AND ((@Material='' and (dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].Material IS NULL)) 
			OR dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].Material = COALESCE(NULLIF(@material, ''), dbo.[tbl_Material_Master].Material))
		AND dbo.[tbl_Store_Master].Store_Code = COALESCE(NULLIF(@Store_Code, ''), dbo.[tbl_Store_Master].Store_code)
		ORDER BY convert (varchar (19),dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Encode_Date,120) ASC;			
    END

	ELSE If(@status='RFID_FRESH_STOCK')
    BEGIN	
		SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY (SELECT 1) ASC) AS RowNumber,dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Encode_EPC
		INTO #FRESHSTOCK
		FROM [dbo].[tbl_Encoding_Dtl] 
		WHERE [dbo].[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Is_Status IN ('3','4')
	    AND convert (date,dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Checkout_DtTime,103) between 
			convert (date,COALESCE(NULLIF(@fromdate, ''),dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Checkout_DtTime),103)
			and convert (date,COALESCE(NULLIF(@Todate, ''),dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl].Checkout_DtTime),103);

        SELECT @RecordCount = COUNT(*) FROM #FRESHSTOCK;
		SELECT @TotalCount = COUNT(Encode_EPC) FROM #FRESHSTOCK;
        
		-- Retrieve the paginated results
		SELECT * FROM #FRESHSTOCK WHERE RowNumber BETWEEN (@PageIndex - 1) * @PageSize + 1 AND @PageIndex * @PageSize

        -- Clean up the temporary table
		DROP TABLE #FRESHSTOCK;
    END


	------------------FOR GRC DATA---------------------------------------------

	ELSE If(@status='BIND_HU_FOR_GRC')
	BEGIN
		SELECT DISTINCT TOP 10 G.HU FROM [dbo].[tbl_GRC_DETAILS] G 
		WHERE (@GRC_STATUS='' OR G.Is_Status= @GRC_STATUS)
		AND (@FromDate ='' OR TRY_CONVERT(date, G.GRC_DATE, 105) >= TRY_CONVERT(date, @FromDate, 105))
        AND (@ToDate='' OR TRY_CONVERT(date, G.GRC_DATE, 105) <= TRY_CONVERT(date, @ToDate, 105))
        AND (@Store_Code='' OR G.STORE_CODE = @Store_Code)
		AND(@SearchTerm = '' OR G.HU LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%');
	END

	ELSE If(@status='SEARCH_BIND_HU_FOR_GRC')
	BEGIN
		SELECT DISTINCT G.HU FROM [dbo].[tbl_GRC_DETAILS] G WHERE (@GRC_STATUS='' OR G.Is_Status= @GRC_STATUS)
		AND (@FromDate ='' OR TRY_CONVERT(date, G.GRC_DATE, 105) >= TRY_CONVERT(date, @FromDate, 105))
        AND (@ToDate='' OR TRY_CONVERT(date, G.GRC_DATE, 105) <= TRY_CONVERT(date, @ToDate, 105))
        AND (@Store_Code='' OR G.STORE_CODE = @Store_Code)
		AND(@SearchTerm = '' OR G.HU LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%');
	END

	ELSE If(@status='SHOW_GRC_DATA')
    BEGIN	
		SELECT DISTINCT G.HU ,G.STORE_CODE,
		CASE WHEN @GRC_STATUS='1' THEN 'VALIDATED AT WAREHOUSE' WHEN @GRC_STATUS='' THEN 'RECEIVED AT STORE' ELSE 'INVALID HU' END AS 'RECEIVED_STATUS',
		--IIF((G.IS_STATUS='1'),'VALIDATED','WRONG') AS 'RECEIVED_STATUS',
		G.GRC_DATE 
		INTO #GRCDATA
		FROM [dbo].[tbl_GRC_DETAILS] G WHERE  (@GRC_STATUS='' OR G.Is_Status= @GRC_STATUS)
		AND (@FromDate ='' OR TRY_CONVERT(date, G.GRC_DATE, 105) >= TRY_CONVERT(date, @FromDate, 105))
        AND (@ToDate='' OR TRY_CONVERT(date, G.GRC_DATE, 105) <= TRY_CONVERT(date, @ToDate, 105))
        AND (@Store_Code='' OR G.STORE_CODE = @Store_Code)
		AND (@HU_NO='' OR G.HU = @HU_NO)
		AND(@SearchTerm = '' OR G.HU LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%' OR G.GRC_DATE LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%');
	    --GROUP BY G.STORE_CODE,G.HU,G.IS_STATUS,G.GRC_DATE;

		SET @SortDirection =
				CASE 
					WHEN LOWER(@SortDirection) = 'desc' THEN 'DESC'
					ELSE 'ASC'
				END;

			SET @OrderBy =	
				CASE @SortColumn
					WHEN 'STORE_CODE' THEN 'R.STORE_CODE' 
					WHEN 'HU' THEN 'R.HU'
					WHEN 'RECEIVED_STATUS' THEN 'R.RECEIVED_STATUS'
					WHEN 'GRC_DATE' THEN 'R.GRC_DATE'
	                ELSE 'R.GRC_DATE'
				END
				+ ' ' + @SortDirection
				+ ', R.GRC_DATE ASC';
			
           	SET @SQL = N'SELECT *FROM (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY ' + @OrderBy + N') AS RowNumber,* FROM #GRCDATA R ) T
			WHERE RowNumber BETWEEN @StartRow AND @EndRow
			ORDER BY RowNumber;';

			EXEC sp_executesql	@SQL,N'@StartRow INT, @EndRow INT',
			@StartRow = @StartRow,	@EndRow   = @EndRow;

		 SELECT @RecordCount = COUNT(*) FROM #GRCDATA;
		
        -- Clean up the temporary table
		DROP TABLE #GRCDATA;
    END

	ELSE If(@status='EXPORT_SHOW_GRC_DATA')
    BEGIN	
		SELECT DISTINCT G.HU,G.STORE_CODE,
		CASE WHEN @GRC_STATUS='1' THEN 'VALIDATED AT WAREHOUSE' WHEN @GRC_STATUS='' THEN 'RECEIVED AT STORE' ELSE 'INVALID HU' END AS 'RECEIVED_STATUS',
		--IIF((G.IS_STATUS='1'),'VALIDATED','WRONG') AS 'RECEIVED_STATUS',
		G.GRC_DATE 
	    FROM [dbo].[tbl_GRC_DETAILS] G WHERE (@GRC_STATUS='' OR G.Is_Status= @GRC_STATUS)
		AND (@FromDate ='' OR TRY_CONVERT(date, G.GRC_DATE, 105) >= TRY_CONVERT(date, @FromDate, 105))
        AND (@ToDate='' OR TRY_CONVERT(date, G.GRC_DATE, 105) <= TRY_CONVERT(date, @ToDate, 105))
        AND (@Store_Code='' OR G.STORE_CODE = @Store_Code)
		AND (@HU_NO='' OR G.HU = @HU_NO)
	    --GROUP BY G.STORE_CODE,G.HU,G.IS_STATUS,G.GRC_DATE;
	END

	ELSE If(@status='VIEW_SHOW_GRC_DATA')
    BEGIN	
		SELECT DISTINCT G.STORE_CODE,STORE_NAME,G.HU,G.MAIN_ARTICLE,ISNULL(G.COMPONENT_ARTICLE,'NA') AS 'COMPONENT_ARTICLE',G.QUANTITY as 'QUANTITY',
		--IF((G.IS_STATUS='1'),'VALIDATED','WRONG') AS 'RECEIVED_STATUS',
		CASE WHEN @GRC_STATUS='1' THEN 'VALIDATED AT WAREHOUSE' WHEN @GRC_STATUS='' THEN 'RECEIVED AT STORE' ELSE 'INVALID HU' END AS 'RECEIVED_STATUS',
		G.GRC_DATE
	    INTO #VIEWGRCDATA
		FROM [dbo].[tbl_GRC_DETAILS] G 
	    WHERE(@GRC_STATUS='' OR G.Is_Status= @GRC_STATUS)
		AND (@FromDate ='' OR TRY_CONVERT(date, G.GRC_DATE, 105) >= TRY_CONVERT(date, @FromDate, 105))
        AND (@ToDate='' OR TRY_CONVERT(date, G.GRC_DATE, 105) <= TRY_CONVERT(date, @ToDate, 105))
        AND (@Store_Code='' OR G.STORE_CODE = @Store_Code) AND (@HU_NO='' OR G.HU = @HU_NO)
		AND(@SearchTerm = '' OR G.HU LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%' OR G.GRC_DATE LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%' OR G.MAIN_ARTICLE LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%' OR G.COMPONENT_ARTICLE LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%' OR G.QUANTITY LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%');
		SET @SortDirection =
				CASE 
					WHEN LOWER(@SortDirection) = 'desc' THEN 'DESC'
					ELSE 'ASC'
				END;

			SET @OrderBy =	
				CASE @SortColumn
					WHEN 'STORE_CODE' THEN 'R.STORE_CODE' 
					WHEN 'STORE_NAME' THEN 'R.STORE_NAME' 
					WHEN 'HU' THEN 'R.HU'
					WHEN 'MAIN_ARTICLE' THEN 'R.MAIN_ARTICLE'
					WHEN 'COMPONENT_ARTICLE' THEN 'R.COMPONENT_ARTICLE'
					WHEN 'QUANTITY' THEN 'R.QUANTITY' 
					WHEN 'RECEIVED_STATUS' THEN 'R.RECEIVED_STATUS'
					WHEN 'GRC_DATE' THEN 'R.GRC_DATE'
	                ELSE 'R.GRC_DATE'
				END
				+ ' ' + @SortDirection
				+ ', R.GRC_DATE ASC';
			
           	SET @SQL = N'SELECT *FROM (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY ' + @OrderBy + N') AS RowNumber,* FROM #VIEWGRCDATA R ) T
			WHERE RowNumber BETWEEN @StartRow AND @EndRow
			ORDER BY RowNumber;';

			EXEC sp_executesql	@SQL,N'@StartRow INT, @EndRow INT',
			@StartRow = @StartRow,	@EndRow   = @EndRow;

            SELECT @QTY = SUM(QUANTITY),@MATERIALCOUNT = COUNT(DISTINCT MAIN_ARTICLE), @RecordCount = COUNT(*) FROM #VIEWGRCDATA;
			--print @MATERIALCOUNT
            -- Clean up the temporary table
	        DROP TABLE #VIEWGRCDATA;
    END

	ELSE If(@status='EXPORT_VIEW_SHOW_GRC_DATA')
    BEGIN	
        SELECT DISTINCT G.STORE_CODE,STORE_NAME,G.HU,G.MAIN_ARTICLE,ISNULL(G.COMPONENT_ARTICLE,'NA') AS 'COMPONENT_ARTICLE',G.QUANTITY as 'QUANTITY',
		--IIF((G.IS_STATUS='1'),'VALIDATED','WRONG') AS 'RECEIVED_STATUS',
		CASE WHEN @GRC_STATUS='1' THEN 'VALIDATED AT WAREHOUSE' WHEN @GRC_STATUS='' THEN 'RECEIVED AT STORE' ELSE 'INVALID HU' END AS 'RECEIVED_STATUS',
		G.GRC_DATE
	    FROM [dbo].[tbl_GRC_DETAILS] G 
	    WHERE (@GRC_STATUS='' OR G.Is_Status= @GRC_STATUS)
		AND (@FromDate ='' OR TRY_CONVERT(date, G.GRC_DATE, 105) >= TRY_CONVERT(date, @FromDate, 105))
        AND (@ToDate='' OR TRY_CONVERT(date, G.GRC_DATE, 105) <= TRY_CONVERT(date, @ToDate, 105))
        AND (@Store_Code='' OR G.STORE_CODE = @Store_Code) 
		AND (@HU_NO='' OR G.HU = @HU_NO)
	END

	---------TOTAL_DPOS_SALE----------

	ELSE If(@status='BIND_COUNTER_FOR_POS_SALE')
	BEGIN 
	SELECT DISTINCT V.COUNTER_NO FROM [dbo].[VPOS_SALE_RET_DTL] V 
	WHERE V.COUNTER_NO NOT IN ('RO', 'RE') AND LEFT(V.ITEM_CD, 3) IN ('111','112','113')
    AND (@store_code ='' OR V.STORE_CODE = @store_code)
	AND (@fromdate ='' OR V.BILL_DATE >= @fromdate)
    AND (@Todate ='' OR V.BILL_DATE < DATEADD(DAY, 1, @Todate))
	AND(@SearchTerm = '' OR V.COUNTER_NO LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%');
	END

	ELSE If(@status='BIND_ARTICLE_FOR_POS_SALE')
	BEGIN
		SELECT DISTINCT TOP 10 V.ITEM_CD AS 'ARTICLE' FROM [dbo].[VPOS_SALE_RET_DTL] V 
		WHERE V.COUNTER_NO NOT IN ('RO', 'RE') AND LEFT(V.ITEM_CD, 3) IN ('111','112','113')
		AND (@store_code ='' OR V.STORE_CODE = @store_code)
	    AND (@COUNTER_NO ='' OR V.COUNTER_NO =@COUNTER_NO)
		AND (@fromdate ='' OR V.BILL_DATE >= @fromdate)
        AND (@Todate ='' OR V.BILL_DATE < DATEADD(DAY, 1, @Todate))
		AND(@SearchTerm = '' OR V.ITEM_CD LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%');
	END

	ELSE If(@status='SEARCH_BIND_ARTICLE_FOR_POS_SALE')
	BEGIN
		SELECT DISTINCT V.ITEM_CD AS 'ARTICLE' FROM [dbo].[VPOS_SALE_RET_DTL] V 
		WHERE V.COUNTER_NO NOT IN ('RO', 'RE') AND LEFT(V.ITEM_CD, 3) IN ('111','112','113')
		AND (@store_code ='' OR V.STORE_CODE = @store_code)
	    AND (@COUNTER_NO ='' OR V.COUNTER_NO =@COUNTER_NO)
		AND (@fromdate ='' OR V.BILL_DATE >= @fromdate)
        AND (@Todate ='' OR V.BILL_DATE < DATEADD(DAY, 1, @Todate))
		AND(@SearchTerm = '' OR V.ITEM_CD LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%');
	END

	ELSE If(@status='BIND_EAN_FOR_POS_SALE')
	BEGIN
		SELECT DISTINCT TOP 10 V.SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD AS 'EAN' FROM [dbo].[VPOS_SALE_RET_DTL] V 
		WHERE V.COUNTER_NO NOT IN ('RO', 'RE') AND LEFT(V.ITEM_CD, 3) IN ('111','112','113')
		AND (@store_code ='' OR V.STORE_CODE = @store_code)
	    AND (@COUNTER_NO ='' OR V.COUNTER_NO =@COUNTER_NO)
		AND (@Material ='' OR V.ITEM_CD=@Material)
		AND (@fromdate ='' OR V.BILL_DATE >= @fromdate)
        AND (@Todate ='' OR V.BILL_DATE < DATEADD(DAY, 1, @Todate))
		AND(@SearchTerm = '' OR V.SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%');
	END

	ELSE If(@status='SEARCH_BIND_EAN_FOR_POS_SALE')
	BEGIN
		SELECT DISTINCT  V.SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD AS 'EAN' FROM [dbo].[VPOS_SALE_RET_DTL] V 
		WHERE V.COUNTER_NO NOT IN ('RO', 'RE') AND LEFT(V.ITEM_CD, 3) IN ('111','112','113')
	    AND (@store_code ='' OR V.STORE_CODE = @store_code)
	    AND (@COUNTER_NO ='' OR V.COUNTER_NO =@COUNTER_NO)
		AND (@Material ='' OR V.ITEM_CD=@Material)
		AND (@fromdate ='' OR V.BILL_DATE >= @fromdate)
        AND (@Todate ='' OR V.BILL_DATE < DATEADD(DAY, 1, @Todate))
		AND(@SearchTerm = '' OR V.SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%');
	END

	ELSE If(@status='SHOW_POS_SALE_DATA')
    BEGIN	
		SELECT-- ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY V.BILL_DATE DESC) AS RowNumber,
		V.BILL_DATE, V.POS_TYPE, V.STORE_CODE,COUNTER_NO, COALESCE(NULLIF(V.MAKER_ID, ''), 'NA') AS MAKER_ID,
		ITEM_CD, COALESCE(NULLIF(V.SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD, ''), 'NA') AS 'EAN',CAST(SUM(SALE_QTY) AS INT) AS 'SALE_QTY' 
		INTO #POSSALEDATA
		FROM  [dbo].[VPOS_SALE_RET_DTL] V
		WHERE COUNTER_NO NOT IN ('RO', 'RE') AND LEFT(ITEM_CD, 3) IN ('111','112','113')
		AND (@store_code ='' OR V.STORE_CODE = @store_code)
	    AND (@COUNTER_NO ='' OR V.COUNTER_NO =@COUNTER_NO)
		AND (@EAN ='' OR V.SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD =@EAN)
		AND (@Material ='' OR V.ITEM_CD=@Material)
		AND (@fromdate ='' OR V.BILL_DATE >= @fromdate)
        AND (@Todate ='' OR V.BILL_DATE < DATEADD(DAY, 1, @Todate))
        AND(@SearchTerm = '' OR V.POS_TYPE LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%'OR V.COUNTER_NO LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%'
		OR V.MAKER_ID LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%'OR V.ITEM_CD LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%'
		OR V.SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%')
		GROUP BY V.BILL_DATE,V.POS_TYPE,V.STORE_CODE,COUNTER_NO,MAKER_ID,ITEM_CD,V.SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD;
		 

        SELECT @RecordCount = COUNT(*),@QTY=SUM(SALE_QTY) FROM #POSSALEDATA;
		--SELECT @TotalCount = COUNT() FROM #GRCDATA;
        
			SET @SortDirection =
				CASE 
					WHEN LOWER(@SortDirection) = 'desc' THEN 'DESC'
					ELSE 'ASC'
				END;

			SET @OrderBy =	
				CASE @SortColumn
					WHEN 'BILL_DATE' THEN 'R.BILL_DATE' 
					WHEN 'POS_TYPE' THEN 'R.POS_TYPE' 
					WHEN 'STORE_CODE' THEN 'R.STORE_CODE'
					WHEN 'COUNTER_NO' THEN 'R.COUNTER_NO'
					WHEN 'MAKER_ID' THEN 'R.MAKER_ID'
					WHEN 'SALE_QTY' THEN 'R.SALE_QTY'
					ELSE 'R.ITEM_CD'
				END
				+ ' ' + @SortDirection
				+ ', R.ITEM_CD ASC';
			
           	SET @SQL = N'SELECT *FROM (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY ' + @OrderBy + N') AS RowNumber,* FROM #POSSALEDATA R ) T
			WHERE RowNumber BETWEEN @StartRow AND @EndRow
			ORDER BY RowNumber;';

			EXEC sp_executesql	@SQL,N'@StartRow INT, @EndRow INT',
			@StartRow = @StartRow,	@EndRow   = @EndRow;

        -- Clean up the temporary table
		DROP TABLE #POSSALEDATA;
    END

	
	ELSE If(@status='EXPORT_SHOW_POS_SALE_DATA')
    BEGIN	
		SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY V.BILL_DATE DESC) AS RowNumber,
		V.BILL_DATE,V.POS_TYPE,V.STORE_CODE,COUNTER_NO,COALESCE(NULLIF(V.MAKER_ID, ''), 'NA') AS MAKER_ID,
		ITEM_CD,COALESCE(NULLIF(V.SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD, ''), 'NA') AS 'EAN',CAST(SUM(SALE_QTY) AS INT) AS 'SALE_QTY' 
        FROM  [dbo].[VPOS_SALE_RET_DTL] V
		WHERE COUNTER_NO NOT IN ('RO', 'RE') AND LEFT(ITEM_CD, 3) IN ('111','112','113')
		AND (@fromdate ='' OR v.BILL_DATE >= @fromdate)
        AND (@Todate ='' OR v.BILL_DATE < DATEADD(DAY, 1, @Todate))
		AND (@store_code ='' OR V.STORE_CODE = @store_code)
	    AND (@COUNTER_NO ='' OR V.COUNTER_NO =@COUNTER_NO)
		AND (@EAN ='' OR V.SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD =@EAN)
		GROUP BY V.BILL_DATE,V.POS_TYPE,V.STORE_CODE,COUNTER_NO,MAKER_ID,ITEM_CD,V.SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD;
	END

	------------------TOTAL_RFID_CHECKOUT-----------------------------------

	ELSE If(@status='SHOW_RFID_CHECKOUT_DATA')
    BEGIN	
	    SELECT-- ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY CAST(ed.Checkout_DtTime AS DATE) DESC) AS RowNumber,
        ed.CHECKOUT_STORE_CODE AS 'STORE_CODE',ed.EAN,ISNULL(mm.Material, 'NA') AS 'ITEM_CD',
        ISNULL(mm.ART_DESC, 'NA') AS 'ARTICLE_DESC',COUNT(DISTINCT ed.Encode_EPC) AS 'CHECKOUT_TAGS',
        CAST(ed.Checkout_DtTime AS DATE) AS 'CHECKOUT_DATE' 
		INTO #RFIDCHECKOUT
		FROM [dbo].[tbl_Encoding_Dtl] ed LEFT JOIN [dbo].[tbl_Ean_Mat_Mst] em ON ed.EAN = em.EAN_UPC
        LEFT JOIN [dbo].[tbl_Material_Master] mm ON em.Material = mm.Material
        WHERE ed.Is_Status IN ('3','4') 
	    AND (@store_code ='' OR ed.CHECKOUT_STORE_CODE = @store_code)
        AND (@fromdate ='' OR ed.Checkout_DtTime >= @fromdate)
        AND (@Todate ='' OR ed.Checkout_DtTime < DATEADD(DAY, 1, @Todate))
		AND(@SearchTerm = '' OR ed.EAN LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%'
		OR mm.Material LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%' OR mm.ART_DESC LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%'OR ed.Checkout_DtTime LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%')
        GROUP BY ed.EAN,mm.Material,mm.ART_DESC,CAST(ed.Checkout_DtTime AS DATE),ed.CHECKOUT_STORE_CODE;
		 
        SELECT @RecordCount = COUNT(*),@QTY=SUM(CHECKOUT_TAGS)  FROM #RFIDCHECKOUT;
		--SELECT @TotalCount = COUNT() FROM #GRCDATA;
        
			SET @SortDirection =
				CASE 
					WHEN LOWER(@SortDirection) = 'desc' THEN 'DESC'
					ELSE 'ASC'
				END;

			SET @OrderBy =	
				CASE @SortColumn
					WHEN 'STORE_CODE' THEN 'R.STORE_CODE' 
					WHEN 'CHECKOUT_TAGS' THEN 'R.CHECKOUT_TAGS' 
					WHEN 'ARTICLE_DESC' THEN 'R.ARTICLE_DESC'
					WHEN 'EAN' THEN 'R.EAN'
					WHEN 'CHECKOUT_DATE' THEN 'R.CHECKOUT_DATE'
					ELSE 'R.ITEM_CD'
				END
				+ ' ' + @SortDirection
				+ ', R.ITEM_CD ASC';
			
           	SET @SQL = N'SELECT *FROM (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY ' + @OrderBy + N') AS RowNumber,* FROM #RFIDCHECKOUT R ) T
			WHERE RowNumber BETWEEN @StartRow AND @EndRow
			ORDER BY RowNumber;';

			EXEC sp_executesql	@SQL,N'@StartRow INT, @EndRow INT',
			@StartRow = @StartRow,	@EndRow   = @EndRow;

        -- Clean up the temporary table
		DROP TABLE #RFIDCHECKOUT;
    END

	ELSE If(@status='EXPORT_SHOW_RFID_CHECKOUT_DATA')
    BEGIN	
	    SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY CAST(ed.Checkout_DtTime AS DATE) DESC) AS RowNumber,
        ed.CHECKOUT_STORE_CODE AS 'STORE_CODE',ed.EAN,ISNULL(mm.Material, 'NA') AS 'ITEM_CD',
        ISNULL(mm.ART_DESC, 'NA') AS 'ARTICLE_DESC',COUNT(DISTINCT ed.Encode_EPC) AS 'CHECKOUT_TAGS',
        CAST(ed.Checkout_DtTime AS DATE) AS 'CHECKOUT_DATE' 
        FROM [dbo].[tbl_Encoding_Dtl] ed LEFT JOIN [dbo].[tbl_Ean_Mat_Mst] em ON ed.EAN = em.EAN_UPC
        LEFT JOIN [dbo].[tbl_Material_Master] mm ON em.Material = mm.Material
        WHERE ed.Is_Status IN ('3','4') 
	    AND (@store_code ='' OR ed.CHECKOUT_STORE_CODE = @store_code)
        AND (@fromdate ='' OR ed.Checkout_DtTime >= @fromdate)
        AND (@Todate ='' OR ed.Checkout_DtTime < DATEADD(DAY, 1, @Todate))
        GROUP BY ed.EAN,mm.Material,mm.ART_DESC,CAST(ed.Checkout_DtTime AS DATE),ed.CHECKOUT_STORE_CODE;
    END

	ELSE If(@status='SHOW_RFID_DPOS_SALE_DATA')
    BEGIN 
		WITH LatestEPC AS 
		(
			SELECT V.*,ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY EPC_TAG ORDER BY V.CHECKOUT_DATETIME DESC) AS rn
			FROM dbo.VPOS_SCAN_ITEMS_DTL V WHERE V.Is_Status = '3' 
			AND (@store_code ='' OR V.STORE_CODE = @store_code) AND (@COUNTER_NO ='' OR V.COUNTER_NO =@COUNTER_NO)
		    AND (@Material ='' OR V.ITEM_CD=@Material) AND (@EAN ='' OR V.SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD =@EAN)
			AND (@fromdate ='' OR V.CHECKOUT_DATETIME >= @fromdate) AND (@Todate ='' OR V.CHECKOUT_DATETIME < DATEADD(DAY, 1, @Todate))
		)

	    SELECT --ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY CONVERT(DATE,CHECKOUT_DATETIME) DESC) AS RowNumber,
		CONVERT(DATE,CHECKOUT_DATETIME) AS 'CHECKOUT_DATE',POS_TYPE,STORE_CODE,COUNTER_NO,ITEM_CD,SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD AS 'EAN',
		COUNT(DISTINCT EPC_TAG) AS 'CHECKOUT_TAGS'
		INTO #RFIDDPOSSALE FROM LatestEPC WHERE rn=1
        AND(@SearchTerm = '' OR POS_TYPE LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%'OR COUNTER_NO LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%'
		OR ITEM_CD LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%'OR SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%')
		GROUP BY CONVERT(DATE,CHECKOUT_DATETIME),POS_TYPE,STORE_CODE,COUNTER_NO,ITEM_CD,SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD;
		 

        SELECT @RecordCount = COUNT(*),@QTY=SUM(CHECKOUT_TAGS) FROM #RFIDDPOSSALE;
		--SELECT @TotalCount = COUNT() FROM #GRCDATA;
        
			SET @SortDirection =
				CASE 
					WHEN LOWER(@SortDirection) = 'desc' THEN 'DESC'
					ELSE 'ASC'
				END;

			SET @OrderBy =	
				CASE @SortColumn
					WHEN 'POS_TYPE' THEN 'R.POS_TYPE' 
					WHEN 'STORE_CODE' THEN 'R.STORE_CODE' 
					WHEN 'COUNTER_NO' THEN 'R.COUNTER_NO'
					WHEN 'EAN' THEN 'R.EAN'
					WHEN 'CHECKOUT_DATE' THEN 'R.CHECKOUT_DATE'
					WHEN 'CHECKOUT_TAGS' THEN 'R.CHECKOUT_TAGS'
					ELSE 'R.ITEM_CD'
				END
				+ ' ' + @SortDirection
				+ ', R.ITEM_CD ASC';
			
           	SET @SQL = N'SELECT *FROM (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY ' + @OrderBy + N') AS RowNumber,* FROM #RFIDDPOSSALE R ) T
			WHERE RowNumber BETWEEN @StartRow AND @EndRow
			ORDER BY RowNumber;';

			EXEC sp_executesql	@SQL,N'@StartRow INT, @EndRow INT',
			@StartRow = @StartRow,	@EndRow   = @EndRow;

        -- Clean up the temporary table
		DROP TABLE #RFIDDPOSSALE;
    END

	ELSE If(@status='EXPORT_SHOW_RFID_DPOS_SALE_DATA')
    BEGIN 
		WITH LatestEPC AS 
		(
			SELECT V.*,ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY EPC_TAG ORDER BY V.CHECKOUT_DATETIME DESC) AS rn
			FROM dbo.VPOS_SCAN_ITEMS_DTL V WHERE V.Is_Status = '3' 
			AND (@store_code ='' OR V.STORE_CODE = @store_code) AND (@COUNTER_NO ='' OR V.COUNTER_NO =@COUNTER_NO)
		    AND (@Material ='' OR V.ITEM_CD=@Material) AND (@EAN ='' OR V.SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD =@EAN)
			AND (@fromdate ='' OR V.CHECKOUT_DATETIME >= @fromdate) AND (@Todate ='' OR V.CHECKOUT_DATETIME < DATEADD(DAY, 1, @Todate))
		)

	    SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY CONVERT(DATE,CHECKOUT_DATETIME) DESC) AS RowNumber,
		CONVERT(DATE,CHECKOUT_DATETIME) AS 'CHECKOUT_DATE',POS_TYPE,STORE_CODE,COUNTER_NO,ITEM_CD,SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD AS 'EAN',
		COUNT(DISTINCT EPC_TAG) AS 'CHECKOUT_TAGS'
		FROM LatestEPC WHERE rn=1
		GROUP BY CONVERT(DATE,CHECKOUT_DATETIME),POS_TYPE,STORE_CODE,COUNTER_NO,ITEM_CD,SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD;
		 
    END

	----------RFID_CHECKOUT_MATCHING_WITH_DPOS_SALE---------------------------
	ELSE If(@status='SHOW_RFID_CHECKOUT_MATCHING_WITH_DPOS_SALE_DATA')
    BEGIN
		WITH LatestEPC AS 
		(
			SELECT s.CHECKOUT_DATETIME,s.POS_TYPE,s.STORE_CODE,s.COUNTER_NO,s.ITEM_CD,s.SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD,e.Encode_EPC,

			AND e.CHECKOUT_STORE_CODE = s.STORE_CODE AND (@fromdate='' OR e.Checkout_DtTime >= @fromdate) AND 
			(@todate='' OR e.Checkout_DtTime < DATEADD(DAY, 1, @Todate)) 
			WHERE s.Is_Status = '3' AND (@fromdate='' OR s.CHECKOUT_DATETIME >= @fromdate) AND (@todate='' OR s.CHECKOUT_DATETIME < DATEADD(DAY, 1, @Todate))

		    OR ITEM_CD LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%'OR SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%')
			GROUP BY CONVERT(DATE, CHECKOUT_DATETIME), POS_TYPE,STORE_CODE,COUNTER_NO, ITEM_CD,SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD;

		--SELECT @TotalCount = COUNT() FROM #GRCDATA;
       		SET @SortDirection =
				CASE 
					WHEN LOWER(@SortDirection) = 'desc' THEN 'DESC'
					ELSE 'ASC'
				END;

				CASE @SortColumn
					WHEN 'POS_TYPE' THEN 'R.POS_TYPE' 
					WHEN 'STORE_CODE' THEN 'R.STORE_CODE'
					WHEN 'COUNTER_NO' THEN 'R.COUNTER_NO'
					WHEN 'SALE_QTY' THEN 'R.SALE_QTY'
					WHEN 'EAN' THEN 'R.EAN'
					WHEN 'CHECKOUT_TAGS' THEN 'R.CHECKOUT_TAGS'
					WHEN 'CHECKOUT_DATE' THEN 'CHECKOUT_DATE'
					ELSE 'R.ITEM_CD'
				END
				+ ' ' + @SortDirection
				+ ', R.ITEM_CD ASC';
			
           	SET @SQL = N'SELECT *FROM (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY ' + @OrderBy + N') AS RowNumber,* FROM #RFIDCHECKOUTMATCHINGWITHDPOS R ) T
			WHERE RowNumber BETWEEN @StartRow AND @EndRow
			ORDER BY RowNumber;';

			EXEC sp_executesql	@SQL,N'@StartRow INT, @EndRow INT',
			@StartRow = @StartRow,	@EndRow   = @EndRow;

        -- Clean up the temporary table
			DROP TABLE #RFIDCHECKOUTMATCHINGWITHDPOS;
    END


	ELSE If(@status='EXPORT_SHOW_RFID_CHECKOUT_MATCHING_WITH_DPOS_SALE_DATA')
    BEGIN
		WITH LatestEPC AS 
		(
			SELECT s.CHECKOUT_DATETIME,s.POS_TYPE,s.STORE_CODE,s.COUNTER_NO,s.ITEM_CD,s.SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD,e.Encode_EPC,
			ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY s.EPC_TAG ORDER BY s.CHECKOUT_DATETIME DESC) AS rn
			FROM VPOS_SCAN_ITEMS_DTL s JOIN tbl_Encoding_Dtl e ON e.Encode_EPC = s.EPC_TAG 
			AND (@store_code ='' OR s.STORE_CODE = @store_code) 
			AND (@COUNTER_NO ='' OR s.COUNTER_NO =@COUNTER_NO)
		   AND (@Material ='' OR s.ITEM_CD=@Material) AND (@EAN ='' OR s.SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD =@EAN)
			AND e.CHECKOUT_STORE_CODE = s.STORE_CODE AND (@fromdate='' OR e.Checkout_DtTime >= @fromdate) AND 
			(@todate='' OR e.Checkout_DtTime < DATEADD(DAY, 1, @Todate)) 
			WHERE s.Is_Status = '3' AND (@fromdate='' OR s.CHECKOUT_DATETIME >= @fromdate) AND (@todate='' OR s.CHECKOUT_DATETIME < DATEADD(DAY, 1, @Todate))
		)

			SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY CONVERT(DATE,CHECKOUT_DATETIME) DESC) AS RowNumber,
			CONVERT(DATE, CHECKOUT_DATETIME) AS CHECKOUT_DATE,POS_TYPE,STORE_CODE,COUNTER_NO,ITEM_CD,SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD AS 'EAN',
			COUNT(DISTINCT Encode_EPC) AS 'CHECKOUT_TAGS' FROM LatestEPC
		    WHERE rn = 1 
		    GROUP BY CONVERT(DATE, CHECKOUT_DATETIME), POS_TYPE,STORE_CODE,COUNTER_NO, ITEM_CD,SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD;

	------------RFID_CHECKOUT_NOT_MATCHING_WITH_DPOS_SALE---------------------------------
	ELSE If(@status='SHOW_RFID_CHECKOUT_NOT_MATCHING_WITH_DPOS_SALE_DATA')
    BEGIN
		;WITH LatestEPC AS
	    (
		 SELECT s.POS_TYPE,s.STORE_CODE,s.COUNTER_NO,s.ITEM_CD,s.SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD,s.EPC_TAG,
	     CONVERT(DATE,s.CHECKOUT_DATETIME) AS 'CHECKOUT_DATE',
	     ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY s.EPC_TAG ORDER BY s.CHECKOUT_DATETIME DESC) AS RN
	     FROM VPOS_SCAN_ITEMS_DTL s LEFT JOIN tbl_Encoding_Dtl e ON e.Encode_EPC = s.EPC_TAG AND e.CHECKOUT_STORE_CODE = s.STORE_CODE
	   AND (@fromdate='' OR e.Checkout_DtTime >= @fromdate) AND (@todate='' OR e.Checkout_DtTime < DATEADD(DAY, 1, @Todate)) 
	     WHERE s.Is_Status = '3'
	     AND (@fromdate='' OR s.CHECKOUT_DATETIME >= @fromdate) AND (@todate='' OR s.CHECKOUT_DATETIME < DATEADD(DAY, 1, @Todate))
	     AND (e.Is_Status NOT IN ('3','4'))
	    )

	    SELECT-- ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY CHECKOUT_DATE DESC) AS RowNumber,
		CHECKOUT_DATE,POS_TYPE,STORE_CODE,COUNTER_NO,ITEM_CD,SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD AS 'EAN',COUNT(EPC_TAG) AS 'CHECKOUT_TAGS'
		INTO #RFIDCHECKOUTNOTMATCHINGWITHDPOS_SALE
	    FROM LatestEPC WHERE RN = 1 AND (@store_code ='' OR STORE_CODE = @store_code) 
		AND(@SearchTerm = '' OR POS_TYPE LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%' OR COUNTER_NO LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%'
		OR ITEM_CD LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%'OR SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%')
	    GROUP BY POS_TYPE,STORE_CODE,COUNTER_NO,ITEM_CD,SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD,CHECKOUT_DATE ORDER BY STORE_CODE,CHECKOUT_DATE;

		
		SELECT @RecordCount = COUNT(*),@QTY=SUM(CHECKOUT_TAGS) FROM #RFIDCHECKOUTNOTMATCHINGWITHDPOS_SALE;
		--SELECT @TotalCount = COUNT() FROM #GRCDATA;
        SET @SortDirection =
				CASE 
					WHEN LOWER(@SortDirection) = 'desc' THEN 'DESC'
					ELSE 'ASC'
				END;

			SET @OrderBy =	
				CASE @SortColumn
					WHEN 'POS_TYPE' THEN 'R.POS_TYPE' 
					WHEN 'STORE_CODE' THEN 'R.STORE_CODE'
					WHEN 'COUNTER_NO' THEN 'R.COUNTER_NO'
					WHEN 'SALE_QTY' THEN 'R.SALE_QTY'
					WHEN 'EAN' THEN 'R.EAN'
					WHEN 'CHECKOUT_TAGS' THEN 'R.CHECKOUT_TAGS'
					WHEN 'CHECKOUT_DATE' THEN 'CHECKOUT_DATE'
					ELSE 'R.ITEM_CD'
				END
				+ ' ' + @SortDirection
				+ ', R.ITEM_CD ASC';
			
           	SET @SQL = N'SELECT *FROM (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY ' + @OrderBy + N') AS RowNumber,* FROM #RFIDCHECKOUTNOTMATCHINGWITHDPOS_SALE R ) T
			WHERE RowNumber BETWEEN @StartRow AND @EndRow
			ORDER BY RowNumber;';

			EXEC sp_executesql
			@SQL,N'@StartRow INT, @EndRow INT',
			@StartRow = @StartRow,	@EndRow   = @EndRow;

        -- Clean up the temporary table
		DROP TABLE #RFIDCHECKOUTNOTMATCHINGWITHDPOS_SALE;
    END


	ELSE If(@status='EXPORT_SHOW_RFID_CHECKOUT_NOT_MATCHING_WITH_DPOS_SALE_DATA')
    BEGIN
		;WITH LatestEPC AS
	    (
		 SELECT s.POS_TYPE,s.STORE_CODE,s.COUNTER_NO,s.ITEM_CD,s.SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD,s.EPC_TAG,
	     CONVERT(DATE,s.CHECKOUT_DATETIME) AS 'CHECKOUT_DATE',
	     ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY s.EPC_TAG ORDER BY s.CHECKOUT_DATETIME DESC) AS RN
	     FROM VPOS_SCAN_ITEMS_DTL s LEFT JOIN tbl_Encoding_Dtl e ON e.Encode_EPC = s.EPC_TAG AND e.CHECKOUT_STORE_CODE = s.STORE_CODE
	     AND (@fromdate='' OR e.Checkout_DtTime >= @fromdate) AND (@todate='' OR e.Checkout_DtTime < DATEADD(DAY, 1, @Todate)) 
	     WHERE s.Is_Status = '3'
	     AND (@fromdate='' OR s.CHECKOUT_DATETIME >= @fromdate) AND (@todate='' OR s.CHECKOUT_DATETIME < DATEADD(DAY, 1, @Todate))
	     AND (e.Is_Status NOT IN ('3','4'))
	    )

	    SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY CHECKOUT_DATE DESC) AS RowNumber,
		CHECKOUT_DATE,POS_TYPE,STORE_CODE,COUNTER_NO,ITEM_CD,SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD AS 'EAN',COUNT(EPC_TAG) AS 'CHECKOUT_TAGS'
	    FROM LatestEPC WHERE RN = 1 AND (@store_code ='' OR STORE_CODE = @store_code) 
	    GROUP BY POS_TYPE,STORE_CODE,COUNTER_NO,ITEM_CD,SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD,CHECKOUT_DATE ORDER BY STORE_CODE,CHECKOUT_DATE;
    END




	------------DPOS_SALE_NOT_MATCHING_WITH_RFID_CHECKOUT---------------------------------
	ELSE If(@status='SHOW_DPOS_SALE_NOT_MATCHING_WITH_RFID_CHECKOUT_DATA')

	     AND (@fromdate='' OR s.CHECKOUT_DATETIME >= @fromdate) AND (@todate='' OR s.CHECKOUT_DATETIME < DATEADD(DAY, 1, @Todate))
	    AND (e.Encode_EPC IS NULL OR e.Is_Status NOT IN ('3','4'))
	    )

	    SELECT --ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY CHECKOUT_DATE DESC) AS RowNumber,
		CHECKOUT_DATE,POS_TYPE,STORE_CODE,COUNTER_NO,ITEM_CD,SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD AS 'EAN',COUNT(EPC_TAG) AS 'CHECKOUT_TAGS'

		OR ITEM_CD LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%' OR SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%')
	    GROUP BY POS_TYPE,STORE_CODE,COUNTER_NO,ITEM_CD,SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD,CHECKOUT_DATE ORDER BY STORE_CODE,CHECKOUT_DATE;

					WHEN 'POS_TYPE' THEN 'R.POS_TYPE'

					WHEN 'EAN' THEN 'R.EAN'

           	SET @SQL = N'SELECT *FROM (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY ' + @OrderBy + N') AS RowNumber,* FROM #DPOSSALENOTMATCHINGWITHRFIDCHECKOUT R ) T
			WHERE RowNumber BETWEEN @StartRow AND @EndRow
			ORDER BY RowNumber;';

			EXEC sp_executesql
			@SQL,N'@StartRow INT, @EndRow INT',
			@StartRow = @StartRow,	@EndRow   = @EndRow;
	

        -- Clean up the temporary table
		DROP TABLE #DPOSSALENOTMATCHINGWITHRFIDCHECKOUT;
    END

	ELSE If(@status='EXPORT_SHOW_DPOS_SALE_NOT_MATCHING_WITH_RFID_CHECKOUT_DATA')
    BEGIN
		;WITH LatestEPC AS

		 SELECT s.POS_TYPE,s.STORE_CODE,s.COUNTER_NO,s.ITEM_CD,s.SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD,s.EPC_TAG,

	-----------TOTAL_MANUAL_SALE---------------------------------------------

	ELSE If(@status='SHOW_MANUAL_SALE_DATA')
    BEGIN 
		WITH LatestEPC AS 
		(
			SELECT V.*,ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY EPC_TAG ORDER BY V.CHECKOUT_DATETIME DESC) AS rn
			FROM dbo.VPOS_SCAN_ITEMS_DTL V WHERE V.Is_Status = '3' AND V.MANUAL_SCAN_FLAG = '1'
			AND (@store_code ='' OR V.STORE_CODE = @store_code) AND (@fromdate ='' OR V.CHECKOUT_DATETIME >= @fromdate) 
			AND (@Todate ='' OR V.CHECKOUT_DATETIME < DATEADD(DAY, 1, @Todate))
		)

	    SELECT-- ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY CONVERT(DATE,CHECKOUT_DATETIME) DESC) AS RowNumber,
		CONVERT(DATE,CHECKOUT_DATETIME) AS 'CHECKOUT_DATE',POS_TYPE,STORE_CODE,COUNTER_NO,ITEM_CD,SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD AS 'EAN',
		COUNT(DISTINCT EPC_TAG) AS 'CHECKOUT_TAGS'
		INTO #MANUALSALE FROM LatestEPC WHERE rn=1
        AND(@SearchTerm = '' OR POS_TYPE LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%' OR COUNTER_NO LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%'
		OR ITEM_CD LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%' OR SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%')
		GROUP BY CONVERT(DATE,CHECKOUT_DATETIME),POS_TYPE,STORE_CODE,COUNTER_NO,ITEM_CD,SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD;
		 

        SELECT @RecordCount = COUNT(*),@QTY=SUM(CHECKOUT_TAGS) FROM #MANUALSALE;
		--SELECT @TotalCount = COUNT() FROM #GRCDATA;
        
		SET @SortDirection =
				CASE 
					WHEN LOWER(@SortDirection) = 'desc' THEN 'DESC'
					ELSE 'ASC'
				END;

			SET @OrderBy =	
				CASE @SortColumn
					WHEN 'POS_TYPE' THEN 'R.POS_TYPE' 
					WHEN 'STORE_CODE' THEN 'R.STORE_CODE'
					WHEN 'COUNTER_NO' THEN 'R.COUNTER_NO'
					WHEN 'EAN' THEN 'R.EAN'
					WHEN 'CHECKOUT_TAGS' THEN 'R.CHECKOUT_TAGS'
					WHEN 'CHECKOUT_DATE' THEN 'CHECKOUT_DATE'
					ELSE 'R.ITEM_CD'
				END
				+ ' ' + @SortDirection
				+ ', R.ITEM_CD ASC';
			
           	SET @SQL = N'SELECT *FROM (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY ' + @OrderBy + N') AS RowNumber,* FROM #MANUALSALE R ) T
			WHERE RowNumber BETWEEN @StartRow AND @EndRow
			ORDER BY RowNumber;';

			EXEC sp_executesql
			@SQL,N'@StartRow INT, @EndRow INT',
			@StartRow = @StartRow,	@EndRow   = @EndRow;

			AND (@store_code ='' OR V.STORE_CODE = @store_code) AND (@fromdate ='' OR V.CHECKOUT_DATETIME >= @fromdate) 
			AND (@Todate ='' OR V.CHECKOUT_DATETIME < DATEADD(DAY, 1, @Todate))
		)

	    SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY CONVERT(DATE,CHECKOUT_DATETIME) DESC) AS RowNumber,
		CONVERT(DATE,CHECKOUT_DATETIME) AS 'CHECKOUT_DATE',POS_TYPE,STORE_CODE,COUNTER_NO,ITEM_CD,SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD AS 'EAN',

        AND(@SearchTerm = '' OR POS_TYPE LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%' OR COUNTER_NO LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%'
		OR ITEM_CD LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%' OR SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%')
		GROUP BY CONVERT(DATE,CHECKOUT_DATETIME),POS_TYPE,STORE_CODE,COUNTER_NO,ITEM_CD,SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD;
    END

	---------RFID_CHECKOUT_MATCHING_WITH_DPOS_VOID---------

	ELSE If(@status='BIND_COUNTER_FOR_RFID_CHECKOUT_MATCHING_WITH_DPOS_VOID')
    BEGIN 
         SELECT DISTINCT BB.COUNTER_NO FROM (
		 SELECT COUNTER_NO,ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY EPC_TAG,STORE_CODE ORDER BY LAST_UPDATED_DATETIME DESC) AS rn FROM [dbo].[VPOS_SCAN_ITEMS_DTL] V
		 WHERE (V.item_cd LIKE '111%' OR V.item_cd LIKE '112%' OR V.item_cd LIKE '113%') 
		 AND NOT EXISTS
        (SELECT 1 FROM VPOS_SCAN_ITEMS_DTL VP WHERE VP.EPC_TAG = V.EPC_TAG GROUP BY VP.EPC_TAG HAVING COUNT(CASE WHEN VP.EXT_COL = 'V' THEN 1 END) > 0 
         AND COUNT(CASE WHEN VP.EXT_COL IS NULL THEN 1 END) > 0)
		 AND V.EXT_COL = 'V' AND V.Void_Sync_Flg='1'
		 AND (@fromdate ='' OR V.LAST_UPDATED_DATETIME >= @fromdate) AND (@Todate ='' OR V.LAST_UPDATED_DATETIME < DATEADD(DAY, 1, @Todate)) 
		 AND V.STORE_CODE = @Store_code
		 )BB WHERE BB.RN=1;
    End

   ELSE If(@status='BIND_MATERIAL_FOR_RFID_CHECKOUT_MATCHING_WITH_DPOS_VOID')
   BEGIN 
       SELECT DISTINCT TOP 10 BB.ITEM_CD AS 'ARTICLE' FROM (
	   SELECT V.SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD,V.COUNTER_NO,V.ITEM_CD,ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY EPC_TAG,STORE_CODE ORDER BY LAST_UPDATED_DATETIME DESC) AS rn FROM [dbo].[VPOS_SCAN_ITEMS_DTL] V
	   WHERE (V.item_cd LIKE '111%' OR V.item_cd LIKE '112%' OR V.item_cd LIKE '113%') 
	   AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM VPOS_SCAN_ITEMS_DTL VP WHERE VP.EPC_TAG = V.EPC_TAG GROUP BY VP.EPC_TAG HAVING COUNT(CASE WHEN VP.EXT_COL = 'V' THEN 1 END) > 0 
           AND COUNT(CASE WHEN VP.EXT_COL IS NULL THEN 1 END) > 0)
	   AND V.EXT_COL = 'V' AND V.Void_Sync_Flg='1'
	   AND (@fromdate ='' OR V.LAST_UPDATED_DATETIME >= @fromdate) AND (@Todate ='' OR V.LAST_UPDATED_DATETIME < DATEADD(DAY, 1, @Todate)) 
	   AND (@Store_code = '' OR V.STORE_CODE =@Store_code)
	   AND (@COUNTER_NO = '' OR V.COUNTER_NO = @COUNTER_NO)
	   )BB WHERE BB.RN=1 AND (@SearchTerm = '' OR BB.ITEM_CD LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%');
  END

  ELSE If(@status='SEARCH_BIND_MATERIAL_FOR_RFID_CHECKOUT_MATCHING_WITH_DPOS_VOID')
  BEGIN 
       SELECT DISTINCT BB.ITEM_CD AS 'ARTICLE' FROM (
	   SELECT V.SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD,V.COUNTER_NO,V.ITEM_CD,ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY EPC_TAG,STORE_CODE ORDER BY LAST_UPDATED_DATETIME DESC) AS rn FROM [dbo].[VPOS_SCAN_ITEMS_DTL] V
	   WHERE (V.item_cd LIKE '111%' OR V.item_cd LIKE '112%' OR V.item_cd LIKE '113%') 
	   AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM VPOS_SCAN_ITEMS_DTL VP WHERE VP.EPC_TAG = V.EPC_TAG GROUP BY VP.EPC_TAG HAVING COUNT(CASE WHEN VP.EXT_COL = 'V' THEN 1 END) > 0 
           AND COUNT(CASE WHEN VP.EXT_COL IS NULL THEN 1 END) > 0)
	   AND V.EXT_COL = 'V' AND V.Void_Sync_Flg='1'
	   AND (@fromdate ='' OR V.LAST_UPDATED_DATETIME >= @fromdate) AND (@Todate ='' OR V.LAST_UPDATED_DATETIME < DATEADD(DAY, 1, @Todate)) 
	   AND (@Store_code = '' OR V.STORE_CODE =@Store_code)
	   AND (@COUNTER_NO = '' OR V.COUNTER_NO = @COUNTER_NO)
	   )BB WHERE BB.RN=1 AND (@SearchTerm = '' OR BB.ITEM_CD LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%');
  END

  ELSE If(@status='BIND_EAN_FOR_RFID_CHECKOUT_MATCHING_WITH_DPOS_VOID')
	BEGIN 
       SELECT DISTINCT TOP 10 BB.SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD AS 'EAN' FROM (
	   SELECT V.SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD,V.COUNTER_NO,ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY EPC_TAG,STORE_CODE ORDER BY LAST_UPDATED_DATETIME DESC) AS rn FROM [dbo].[VPOS_SCAN_ITEMS_DTL] V
	   WHERE (V.item_cd LIKE '111%' OR V.item_cd LIKE '112%' OR V.item_cd LIKE '113%') 
	   AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM VPOS_SCAN_ITEMS_DTL VP WHERE VP.EPC_TAG = V.EPC_TAG GROUP BY VP.EPC_TAG HAVING COUNT(CASE WHEN VP.EXT_COL = 'V' THEN 1 END) > 0 
           AND COUNT(CASE WHEN VP.EXT_COL IS NULL THEN 1 END) > 0)
	   AND V.EXT_COL = 'V' AND V.Void_Sync_Flg='1'
	   AND (@fromdate ='' OR V.LAST_UPDATED_DATETIME >= @fromdate) AND (@Todate ='' OR V.LAST_UPDATED_DATETIME < DATEADD(DAY, 1, @Todate)) 
	   AND (@Store_code = '' OR V.STORE_CODE =@Store_code)
	   AND (@COUNTER_NO = '' OR V.COUNTER_NO = @COUNTER_NO)
	   AND (@Material = '' OR V.ITEM_CD = @Material)
	   )BB WHERE BB.RN=1 AND (@SearchTerm = '' OR BB.SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%');
	END

	ELSE If(@status='SEARCH_BIND_EAN_FOR_RFID_CHECKOUT_MATCHING_WITH_DPOS_VOID')
	BEGIN 
       SELECT DISTINCT BB.SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD AS 'EAN' FROM (
	   SELECT V.SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD,V.COUNTER_NO,ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY EPC_TAG,STORE_CODE ORDER BY LAST_UPDATED_DATETIME DESC) AS rn FROM [dbo].[VPOS_SCAN_ITEMS_DTL] V
	   WHERE (V.item_cd LIKE '111%' OR V.item_cd LIKE '112%' OR V.item_cd LIKE '113%') 
	   AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM VPOS_SCAN_ITEMS_DTL VP WHERE VP.EPC_TAG = V.EPC_TAG GROUP BY VP.EPC_TAG HAVING COUNT(CASE WHEN VP.EXT_COL = 'V' THEN 1 END) > 0 
           AND COUNT(CASE WHEN VP.EXT_COL IS NULL THEN 1 END) > 0)
	   AND V.EXT_COL = 'V' AND V.Void_Sync_Flg='1'
	   AND (@fromdate ='' OR V.LAST_UPDATED_DATETIME >= @fromdate) AND (@Todate ='' OR V.LAST_UPDATED_DATETIME < DATEADD(DAY, 1, @Todate)) 
	   AND (@Store_code = '' OR V.STORE_CODE =@Store_code)
	   AND (@COUNTER_NO = '' OR V.COUNTER_NO = @COUNTER_NO)
	   AND (@Material = '' OR V.ITEM_CD = @Material)
	   )BB WHERE BB.RN=1 AND (@SearchTerm = '' OR BB.SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%');
   End

  ELSE If(@status='BIND_DATA_FOR_RFID_CHECKOUT_MATCHING_WITH_DPOS_VOID')
  BEGIN
  
		 SELECT CONVERT(DATE,BB.LAST_UPDATED_DATETIME) AS 'VOID_DATE',BB.STORE_CODE,BB.COUNTER_NO,BB.SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD AS 'EAN',BB.ITEM_CD,
		 COUNT(BB.EPC_TAG) AS 'VOID_QTY' 
		 INTO #MATCHINGDPOSVOID
		 FROM (
				 SELECT V.LAST_UPDATED_DATETIME,V.STORE_CODE,V.COUNTER_NO,SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD,ITEM_CD,V.EPC_TAG,ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY V.EPC_TAG, V.STORE_CODE ORDER BY V.LAST_UPDATED_DATETIME DESC) AS rn
				 FROM VPOS_SCAN_ITEMS_DTL V WHERE V.EXT_COL = 'V' AND V.Void_Sync_Flg='1' and (V.item_cd LIKE '111%' OR V.item_cd LIKE '112%' OR V.item_cd LIKE '113%') 
				 AND NOT EXISTS
				 (SELECT 1 FROM VPOS_SCAN_ITEMS_DTL VP WHERE VP.EPC_TAG = V.EPC_TAG GROUP BY VP.EPC_TAG HAVING COUNT(CASE WHEN VP.EXT_COL = 'V' THEN 1 END) > 0 
				 AND COUNT(CASE WHEN VP.EXT_COL IS NULL THEN 1 END) > 0)
				 AND (@fromdate ='' OR V.LAST_UPDATED_DATETIME >= @fromdate) AND (@Todate ='' OR V.LAST_UPDATED_DATETIME < DATEADD(DAY, 1, @Todate)) 
				 AND (@Store_code = '' OR V.STORE_CODE =@Store_code)
				 AND (@COUNTER_NO = '' OR V.COUNTER_NO = @COUNTER_NO)
				 AND (@EAN = '' OR V.SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD = @EAN)
				 AND (@Material = '' OR V.ITEM_CD = @Material)
              )
		BB WHERE BB.RN=1 GROUP BY BB.STORE_CODE,CONVERT(DATE,BB.LAST_UPDATED_DATETIME),BB.STORE_CODE,BB.COUNTER_NO,BB.SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD,BB.ITEM_CD
	

        SELECT @RecordCount = COUNT(*),@QTY = SUM(VOID_QTY)FROM #MATCHINGDPOSVOID;

    
	    SET @SortDirection =
				CASE 
					WHEN LOWER(@SortDirection) = 'desc' THEN 'DESC'
					ELSE 'ASC'
				END;

			SET @OrderBy =	
				CASE @SortColumn
					WHEN 'STORE CODE' THEN 'R.STORE_CODE' 
					WHEN 'COUNTER NO' THEN 'R.COUNTER_NO'
					WHEN 'EAN' THEN 'R.EAN'
					WHEN 'ITEM_CD' THEN 'R.ITEM_CD'
					WHEN 'VOID QTY' THEN 'R.VOID_QTY'
					ELSE 'R.VOID_DATE'
				END
				+ ' ' + @SortDirection
				+ ', R.VOID_DATE';
			
            SET @SQL = N'SELECT *FROM (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY ' + @OrderBy + N') AS RowNumber,* FROM #MATCHINGDPOSVOID R ) T
			WHERE RowNumber BETWEEN @StartRow AND @EndRow
			ORDER BY RowNumber;';

			EXEC sp_executesql
			@SQL,N'@StartRow INT, @EndRow INT',
			@StartRow = @StartRow,	@EndRow   = @EndRow;

		-- Clean up the temporary table
		DROP TABLE #MATCHINGDPOSVOID;
     
  END

  ELSE If(@status='EXPORT_BIND_DATA_FOR_RFID_CHECKOUT_MATCHING_WITH_DPOS_VOID')
  BEGIN
      SELECT CONVERT(DATE,BB.LAST_UPDATED_DATETIME) AS 'VOID_DATE',BB.STORE_CODE,BB.COUNTER_NO,BB.SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD AS 'EAN',BB.ITEM_CD AS 'MATERIAL',
		 COUNT(BB.EPC_TAG) AS 'VOID_QTY' 
		 FROM (
				 SELECT V.LAST_UPDATED_DATETIME,V.STORE_CODE,V.COUNTER_NO,SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD,ITEM_CD,V.EPC_TAG,ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY V.EPC_TAG, V.STORE_CODE ORDER BY V.LAST_UPDATED_DATETIME DESC) AS rn
				 FROM VPOS_SCAN_ITEMS_DTL V WHERE V.EXT_COL = 'V' AND V.Void_Sync_Flg='1' and (V.item_cd LIKE '111%' OR V.item_cd LIKE '112%' OR V.item_cd LIKE '113%') 
				 AND NOT EXISTS
				 (SELECT 1 FROM VPOS_SCAN_ITEMS_DTL VP WHERE VP.EPC_TAG = V.EPC_TAG GROUP BY VP.EPC_TAG HAVING COUNT(CASE WHEN VP.EXT_COL = 'V' THEN 1 END) > 0 
				 AND COUNT(CASE WHEN VP.EXT_COL IS NULL THEN 1 END) > 0)
				 AND (@fromdate ='' OR V.LAST_UPDATED_DATETIME >= @fromdate) AND (@Todate ='' OR V.LAST_UPDATED_DATETIME < DATEADD(DAY, 1, @Todate)) 
				 AND (@Store_code = '' OR V.STORE_CODE =@Store_code)
				 AND (@COUNTER_NO = '' OR V.COUNTER_NO = @COUNTER_NO)
				 AND (@EAN = '' OR V.SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD = @EAN)
				 AND (@Material = '' OR V.ITEM_CD = @Material)
              )
		BB WHERE BB.RN=1 GROUP BY BB.STORE_CODE,CONVERT(DATE,BB.LAST_UPDATED_DATETIME),BB.STORE_CODE,BB.COUNTER_NO,BB.SCAN_ITEM_EAN_CD,BB.ITEM_CD
    END

	--	C.BARCODE AS 'EAN',C.BARCODE_QTY AS 'EAN_QTY',C.START_DATE,C.END_DATE,C.START_TIME,C.END_TIME,C.Actual_Qty,C.Scanned_Qty,

	--		SET @OrderBy =
