	--ELSE If(@status='EXPORT_CYCLE_COUNT_REPORT')
 --   BEGIN 
 --       SELECT C.Ref_ID,S.Store_Code AS 'STORE_CODE',S.Store_Name  AS 'STORE_NAME',C.STOCK_TYPE,C.ECODE,M.itemMcCode AS 'MC',M.itemMcDesc AS 'MC_TEXT',M.Material AS 'ARTICLE',M.ART_DESC AS 'ARTICLE_DESC', 
	--	C.BARCODE AS 'EAN',C.BARCODE_QTY AS 'EAN_QTY',C.START_DATE,C.END_DATE,C.START_TIME,C.END_TIME,C.Actual_Qty,C.Scanned_Qty,	
	--	CASE  WHEN Actual_Qty > Scanned_Qty THEN '-' + Scanned_Qty-Actual_Qty ELSE '+' + Scanned_Qty-Actual_Qty END as Variance		
	--	FROM [dbo].[tbl_Store_Master] S 
	--	JOIN [dbo].[tbl_Cycle_count_Dtl] C ON S.Store_Code = C.Site_Code
	--	JOIN [dbo].[tbl_Ean_Mat_Mst] E ON C.BARCODE=E.EAN_UPC
	--	JOIN [dbo].[tbl_Material_Master] M ON E.Material=M.Material
	--	WHERE (C.ENTRY_DT >= @fromdate) AND (C.ENTRY_DT < DATEADD(DAY, 1,@todate))
	--	AND S.Store_Code =@Store_code OR C.Ref_ID=@ref_No
 --   END

 ---------------------------Commented by Manish------------------------------------ 
	ELSE If(@status='CYCLE_COUNT_REPORT_VIEW')
   BEGIN 
		SELECT CONVERT(DATE,Entry_Dt) AS 'DATE',C.Ref_ID,S.Store_Code AS 'STORE_CODE',S.Store_Name  AS 'STORE_NAME',
		CASE WHEN C.CYCLE_COUNT_TYPE='TA' THEN 'ARTICLE LEVEL' WHEN C.CYCLE_COUNT_TYPE='MC' THEN 'MC LEVEL' ELSE C.CYCLE_COUNT_TYPE END AS 'CYCLE_COUNT_TYPE',
		COUNT(C.Barcode) AS 'NO_OF_ARTICLE',
		SUM(C.Actual_Qty) AS 'SYSTEM_STOCK',SUM(C.Scanned_Qty) AS 'SCANNED_QTY',
		SUM(C.Scanned_Qty)-SUM(C.Actual_Qty) AS 'NET_DIFF',
		SUM( CASE WHEN(Scanned_Qty - Actual_Qty) < 0 THEN (Scanned_Qty - Actual_Qty) ELSE 0 END) AS 'SHORT_QTY',
		SUM(CASE WHEN(Scanned_Qty - Actual_Qty) > 0 THEN (Scanned_Qty - Actual_Qty) ELSE 0 END) AS 'EXCESS_QTY',

		ISNULL((SELECT CONVERT(VARCHAR(8),MIN(TRY_CONVERT(datetime,NULLIF(C2.START_DATE,'') + ' ' +ISNULL(CONVERT(varchar(8), C2.Start_Time,108),''))), 108)
        FROM dbo.tbl_Cycle_count_Dtl C2 WHERE C2.Ref_id = C.Ref_id),'NA') AS 'Start_DateTime',

        ISNULL((SELECT CONVERT(VARCHAR(8),MAX(TRY_CONVERT(datetime,NULLIF(C2.END_DATE,'') + ' ' +ISNULL(CONVERT(varchar(8), C2.END_Time,108),''))), 108)
        FROM dbo.tbl_Cycle_count_Dtl C2 WHERE C2.Ref_id = C.Ref_id),'NA') AS 'END_DateTime',

        ISNULL(CONVERT(VARCHAR(8),DATEADD(SECOND,DATEDIFF(SECOND,
        (SELECT MIN(TRY_CONVERT(datetime,NULLIF(C2.START_DATE,'') + ' ' +ISNULL(CONVERT(varchar(8), C2.Start_Time,108),'')))
        FROM dbo.tbl_Cycle_count_Dtl C2 WHERE C2.Ref_id = C.Ref_id),

        (SELECT MAX(TRY_CONVERT(datetime,NULLIF(C2.END_DATE,'') + ' ' +ISNULL(CONVERT(varchar(8), C2.END_Time,108),'')))FROM dbo.tbl_Cycle_count_Dtl C2
        WHERE C2.Ref_id = C.Ref_id)
		 
         ),0), 108),'NA') AS 'Time_Taken'

		INTO #CYCLECOUNTREPORTVIEW

		FROM [dbo].[tbl_Store_Master] S 
		JOIN [dbo].[tbl_Cycle_count_Dtl] C ON S.Store_Code = C.Site_Code
		JOIN [dbo].[tbl_Ean_Mat_Mst] E ON C.BARCODE=E.EAN_UPC
		JOIN [dbo].[tbl_Material_Master] M ON E.Material=M.Material
		WHERE (@fromdate ='' OR C.ENTRY_DT >= @fromdate) AND (@Todate ='' OR C.ENTRY_DT < DATEADD(DAY, 1,@todate))
		AND (@Store_code='' OR S.Store_Code =@Store_code) 
		AND (@SearchTerm = ''
			OR C.Ref_ID LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%'
			OR S.Store_Code LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%'
			OR S.Store_Name LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%'
			OR C.Entry_Dt LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%'
			OR C.CYCLE_COUNT_TYPE LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%'
			OR C.Barcode LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%'
			OR C.Actual_Qty LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%'
			OR C.Scanned_Qty LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%'
		)
		
		GROUP BY C.Ref_ID,S.Store_Code,S.Store_Name,CONVERT(DATE,Entry_Dt),C.CYCLE_COUNT_TYPE
		ORDER BY  CONVERT(DATE, Entry_Dt) DESC, C.Ref_ID DESC;

 
		SELECT @RecordCount = COUNT(*) FROM #CYCLECOUNTREPORTVIEW;
		SET @SortDirection =
					CASE 
						WHEN LOWER(@SortDirection) = 'desc' THEN 'DESC'
						ELSE 'ASC'
					END;
					SET @OrderBy =	
					CASE @SortColumn
						WHEN 'DATE' THEN 'R.DATE ' + @SortDirection + ', R.Ref_ID ' + @SortDirection
						WHEN 'Ref_ID'         THEN 'R.Ref_ID ' + @SortDirection
						WHEN 'STORE_CODE'     THEN 'R.STORE_CODE ' + @SortDirection
						WHEN 'STORE_NAME'     THEN 'R.STORE_NAME ' + @SortDirection
						WHEN 'NO_OF_ARTICLE'  THEN 'R.NO_OF_ARTICLE ' + @SortDirection
						WHEN 'SYSTEM_STOCK'   THEN 'R.SYSTEM_STOCK ' + @SortDirection
						WHEN 'SCANNED_QTY'    THEN 'R.SCANNED_QTY ' + @SortDirection
						WHEN 'NET_DIFF'       THEN 'R.NET_DIFF ' + @SortDirection
						WHEN 'SHORT_QTY'      THEN 'R.SHORT_QTY ' + @SortDirection
						WHEN 'EXCESS_QTY'     THEN 'R.EXCESS_QTY ' + @SortDirection
						WHEN 'Start_DateTime' THEN 'R.Start_DateTime ' + @SortDirection
						WHEN 'END_DateTime'   THEN 'R.END_DateTime ' + @SortDirection
						WHEN 'Time_Taken'     THEN 'R.Time_Taken ' + @SortDirection
        
						
						ELSE 'R.DATE DESC, R.Ref_ID DESC'
					END;

				SET @SQL = N'SELECT *FROM (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY ' + @OrderBy + N') AS RowNumber,* FROM #CYCLECOUNTREPORTVIEW R ) T
				WHERE RowNumber BETWEEN @StartRow AND @EndRow
				ORDER BY RowNumber;';
				EXEC sp_executesql

				@SQL,N'@StartRow INT, @EndRow INT',
				@StartRow = @StartRow,	@EndRow   = @EndRow;

		   -- Clean up the temporary table\
			DROP TABLE #CYCLECOUNTREPORTVIEW;
	END

   ELSE If(@status='EXPORT_CYCLE_COUNT_REPORT_VIEW')
   BEGIN 
		SELECT CONVERT(DATE,Entry_Dt) AS 'DATE',C.Ref_ID,S.Store_Code AS 'STORE_CODE',S.Store_Name  AS 'STORE_NAME',
		CASE WHEN C.CYCLE_COUNT_TYPE='TA' THEN 'ARTICLE LEVEL' WHEN C.CYCLE_COUNT_TYPE='MC' THEN 'MC LEVEL' ELSE C.CYCLE_COUNT_TYPE END AS 'CYCLE_COUNT_TYPE',
		COUNT(C.Barcode) AS 'NO_OF_ARTICLE',
		SUM(C.Actual_Qty) AS 'SYSTEM_STOCK',SUM(C.Scanned_Qty) AS 'SCANNED_QTY',
		SUM(C.Scanned_Qty)-SUM(C.Actual_Qty) AS 'NET_DIFF',
		SUM( CASE WHEN(Scanned_Qty - Actual_Qty) < 0 THEN (Scanned_Qty - Actual_Qty) ELSE 0 END) AS 'SHORT_QTY',
		SUM(CASE WHEN(Scanned_Qty - Actual_Qty) > 0 THEN (Scanned_Qty - Actual_Qty) ELSE 0 END) AS 'EXCESS_QTY',

		ISNULL((SELECT CONVERT(VARCHAR(8),MIN(TRY_CONVERT(datetime,NULLIF(C2.START_DATE,'') + ' ' +ISNULL(CONVERT(varchar(8), C2.Start_Time,108),''))), 108)
        FROM dbo.tbl_Cycle_count_Dtl C2 WHERE C2.Ref_id = C.Ref_id),'NA') AS 'Start_DateTime',

        ISNULL((SELECT CONVERT(VARCHAR(8),MAX(TRY_CONVERT(datetime,NULLIF(C2.END_DATE,'') + ' ' +ISNULL(CONVERT(varchar(8), C2.END_Time,108),''))), 108)
        FROM dbo.tbl_Cycle_count_Dtl C2 WHERE C2.Ref_id = C.Ref_id),'NA') AS 'END_DateTime',

        ISNULL(CONVERT(VARCHAR(8),DATEADD(SECOND,DATEDIFF(SECOND,
        (SELECT MIN(TRY_CONVERT(datetime,NULLIF(C2.START_DATE,'') + ' ' +ISNULL(CONVERT(varchar(8), C2.Start_Time,108),'')))
        FROM dbo.tbl_Cycle_count_Dtl C2 WHERE C2.Ref_id = C.Ref_id),

        (SELECT MAX(TRY_CONVERT(datetime,NULLIF(C2.END_DATE,'') + ' ' +ISNULL(CONVERT(varchar(8), C2.END_Time,108),'')))FROM dbo.tbl_Cycle_count_Dtl C2
        WHERE C2.Ref_id = C.Ref_id)
		 
         ),0), 108),'NA') AS 'Time_Taken'

        FROM [dbo].[tbl_Store_Master] S 
		JOIN [dbo].[tbl_Cycle_count_Dtl] C ON S.Store_Code = C.Site_Code
		JOIN [dbo].[tbl_Ean_Mat_Mst] E ON C.BARCODE=E.EAN_UPC
		JOIN [dbo].[tbl_Material_Master] M ON E.Material=M.Material
		WHERE (@fromdate ='' OR C.ENTRY_DT >= @fromdate) AND (@Todate ='' OR C.ENTRY_DT < DATEADD(DAY, 1,@todate))
		AND (@Store_code='' OR S.Store_Code =@Store_code) 
		GROUP BY C.Ref_ID,S.Store_Code,S.Store_Name,CONVERT(DATE,Entry_Dt),C.CYCLE_COUNT_TYPE
		ORDER BY  CONVERT(DATE, Entry_Dt) DESC, C.Ref_ID DESC;
      END

   ELSE If(@status='CYCLE_COUNT_REPORT')
   BEGIN 
        SELECT C.Ref_ID,S.Store_Code AS 'STORE_CODE',S.Store_Name  AS 'STORE_NAME',C.STOCK_TYPE,C.ECODE,M.itemMcCode AS 'MC',M.itemMcDesc AS 'MC_TEXT',M.Material AS 'ARTICLE',M.ART_DESC AS 'ARTICLE_DESC', 
		C.BARCODE AS 'EAN',C.BARCODE_QTY AS 'EAN_QTY',C.START_DATE,C.END_DATE,C.START_TIME,C.END_TIME,C.Actual_Qty,C.Scanned_Qty,	
		CAST(C.Scanned_Qty - C.Actual_Qty AS VARCHAR(20)) AS Variance

		INTO #CYCLECOUNTREPORT
		FROM [dbo].[tbl_Store_Master] S 
		JOIN [dbo].[tbl_Cycle_count_Dtl] C ON S.Store_Code = C.Site_Code
		JOIN [dbo].[tbl_Ean_Mat_Mst] E ON C.BARCODE=E.EAN_UPC
		JOIN [dbo].[tbl_Material_Master] M ON E.Material=M.Material
		WHERE (@fromdate ='' OR C.ENTRY_DT >= @fromdate) AND (@Todate ='' OR C.ENTRY_DT < DATEADD(DAY, 1,@todate))
		AND (@Store_code='' OR S.Store_Code =@Store_code) 
		AND (@ref_No ='' OR C.Ref_ID=@ref_No)
		--AND( (@ref_No <> '' AND C.Ref_ID = @ref_No) OR (@ref_No = '' AND C.Ref_ID IN ( SELECT Ref_ID FROM ( SELECT Ref_ID, ROW_NUMBER() OVER ( PARTITION BY CAST(ENTRY_DT AS DATE) ORDER BY ENTRY_DT DESC) AS rn FROM tbl_Cycle_count_Dtl WHERE ENTRY_DT >= @fromdate AND ENTRY_DT < DATEADD(DAY,1,@todate) ) X WHERE rn = 1 ) ) )
		--AND( (@ref_No <> '' AND C.Ref_ID = @ref_No) OR (@ref_No = '' AND C.Ref_ID IN ( SELECT MAX(Ref_ID) FROM tbl_Cycle_count_Dtl WHERE Site_Code = @Store_code AND ENTRY_DT >= @fromdate AND ENTRY_DT < DATEADD(DAY,1,@todate) GROUP BY CAST(ENTRY_DT AS DATE)) ) ) 
		AND(@SearchTerm = '' OR C.STOCK_TYPE LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%' OR C.ECODE LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%' OR M.itemMcCode LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%'
		OR M.Material LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%' OR M.ART_DESC LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%' OR C.BARCODE LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%' OR C.Ref_ID LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%')

	
		--------SELECT @RecordCount = COUNT(*),@QTY=SUM(EAN_QTY),@Ttl_Act_QTY=SUM(Actual_Qty),@Ttl_Scanned_Qty=SUM(Scanned_Qty),@Excess_Qty=SUM(CASE WHEN ISNULL(Scanned_Qty, 0) > Actual_Qty  THEN ISNULL(Scanned_Qty, 0) - Actual_Qty ELSE 0  END),@DIFFQTY=SUM(Actual_Qty) - SUM(IsNull(Scanned_Qty,0)) FROM #CYCLECOUNTREPORT;
		--SELECT @RecordCount = COUNT(*),@QTY=SUM(EAN_QTY),@Ttl_Act_QTY=SUM(Actual_Qty),@Sum_Scanned_Qty=SUM(Scanned_Qty),
		--@Excess_Qty=SUM(CASE WHEN Scanned_Qty > Actual_Qty THEN (Scanned_Qty - Actual_Qty) ELSE 0 END),
		--@DIFFQTY= SUM(CASE WHEN Scanned_Qty < Actual_Qty THEN (Scanned_Qty - Actual_Qty) ELSE 0 END)

		SELECT 
			@RecordCount = COUNT(*),
			@QTY = SUM(EAN_QTY),
			@Ttl_Act_QTY = SUM(Actual_Qty),
			@Sum_Scanned_Qty = SUM(Scanned_Qty),
			--@Excess_Qty = SUM( CASE WHEN( CASE WHEN Actual_Qty < 0 THEN Actual_Qty + Scanned_Qty ELSE Scanned_Qty - Actual_Qty END) > 0 THEN ( CASE WHEN Actual_Qty < 0 THEN Actual_Qty + Scanned_Qty ELSE Scanned_Qty - Actual_Qty END ) ELSE 0 END) ,
			@Excess_Qty = SUM( CASE WHEN(Scanned_Qty - Actual_Qty) > 0 THEN (Scanned_Qty - Actual_Qty) ELSE 0 END),
			@DIFFQTY = SUM( CASE WHEN(Scanned_Qty - Actual_Qty) < 0 THEN (Scanned_Qty - Actual_Qty) ELSE 0 END)

		FROM #CYCLECOUNTREPORT;

	    SET @SortDirection =
				CASE 
					WHEN LOWER(@SortDirection) = 'desc' THEN 'DESC'
					ELSE 'ASC'
				END;

			SET @OrderBy =	
				CASE @SortColumn
					WHEN 'Ref_ID' THEN 'R.Ref_ID' 
					WHEN 'STORE_CODE' THEN 'R.STORE_CODE' 
					WHEN 'STORE_NAME' THEN 'R.STORE_NAME'
					WHEN 'STOCK_TYPE' THEN 'R.STOCK_TYPE'
					WHEN 'MC_TEXT' THEN 'R.MC_TEXT'
					WHEN 'ARTICLE' THEN 'R.ARTICLE'
					WHEN 'ARTICLE_DESC' THEN 'R.ARTICLE_DESC'
					WHEN 'EAN' THEN 'R.EAN'
					WHEN 'EAN_QTY' THEN 'R.EAN_QTY'
					WHEN 'START_DATE' THEN 'R.START_DATE'
					WHEN 'END_DATE' THEN 'R.END_DATE'
					WHEN 'START_TIME' THEN 'R.START_TIME'
					WHEN 'END_TIME' THEN 'R.END_TIME'
					WHEN 'Actual_Qty' THEN 'R.Actual_Qty'				
					WHEN 'Scanned_Qty' THEN 'R.Scanned_Qty'
					WHEN 'Variance' THEN 'TRY_CAST(R.Variance AS INT)'	
					--WHEN 'MRP' THEN 'R.MRP'
					ELSE 'R.STORE_CODE'
				END
				+ ' ' + @SortDirection
				+ ', R.END_TIME';
			
            SET @SQL = N'SELECT *FROM (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY ' + @OrderBy + N') AS RowNumber,* FROM #CYCLECOUNTREPORT R ) T
			WHERE RowNumber BETWEEN @StartRow AND @EndRow
			ORDER BY RowNumber;';

			EXEC sp_executesql
			@SQL,N'@StartRow INT, @EndRow INT',
			@StartRow = @StartRow,	@EndRow   = @EndRow;

	   -- Clean up the temporary table
		DROP TABLE #CYCLECOUNTREPORT;
    END

   ELSE If(@status='EXPORT_CYCLE_COUNT_REPORT')
   BEGIN 
        SELECT C.Ref_ID,S.Store_Code AS 'STORE_CODE',S.Store_Name  AS 'STORE_NAME',C.STOCK_TYPE,C.ECODE,M.itemMcCode AS 'MC',M.itemMcDesc AS 'MC_TEXT',M.Material AS 'ARTICLE',M.ART_DESC AS 'ARTICLE_DESC', 
		C.BARCODE AS 'EAN',C.BARCODE_QTY AS 'EAN_QTY',
		ISNULL(NULLIF(C.START_DATE,''), 'NA') AS START_DATE,
		ISNULL(NULLIF(C.START_TIME,''), 'NA') AS START_TIME,
		ISNULL(NULLIF(C.END_DATE,''), 'NA') AS END_DATE,
		ISNULL(NULLIF(C.END_TIME,''), 'NA') AS END_TIME,
		C.Actual_Qty,C.Scanned_Qty,
		CAST(C.Scanned_Qty - C.Actual_Qty AS VARCHAR(20)) AS Variance

		----CASE  WHEN Actual_Qty > Scanned_Qty THEN '-' + Scanned_Qty-Actual_Qty ELSE '+' + Scanned_Qty-Actual_Qty END as Variance		
		FROM [dbo].[tbl_Store_Master] S 
		JOIN [dbo].[tbl_Cycle_count_Dtl] C ON S.Store_Code = C.Site_Code
		JOIN [dbo].[tbl_Ean_Mat_Mst] E ON C.BARCODE=E.EAN_UPC
		JOIN [dbo].[tbl_Material_Master] M ON E.Material=M.Material
		WHERE (C.ENTRY_DT >= @fromdate) 
		AND (C.ENTRY_DT < DATEADD(DAY, 1,@todate))
		AND (@Store_code='' OR S.Store_Code =@Store_code)
		AND (@ref_No ='' OR C.Ref_ID=@ref_No)
		--AND( (@ref_No <> '' AND C.Ref_ID = @ref_No) OR (@ref_No = '' AND C.Ref_ID IN ( SELECT Ref_ID FROM ( SELECT Ref_ID, ROW_NUMBER() OVER ( PARTITION BY CAST(ENTRY_DT AS DATE) ORDER BY ENTRY_DT DESC) AS rn FROM tbl_Cycle_count_Dtl WHERE ENTRY_DT >= @fromdate AND ENTRY_DT < DATEADD(DAY,1,@todate) ) X WHERE rn = 1 ) ) )
		--AND( (@ref_No <> '' AND C.Ref_ID = @ref_No) OR (@ref_No = '' AND C.Ref_ID IN ( SELECT MAX(Ref_ID) FROM tbl_Cycle_count_Dtl WHERE Site_Code = @Store_code AND ENTRY_DT >= @fromdate AND ENTRY_DT < DATEADD(DAY,1,@todate) GROUP BY CAST(ENTRY_DT AS DATE)) ) ) 
	END

	-------------LIVE STOCK REPORT------------------------

   ELSE If(@status='BIND_MATERIAL_FOR_LIVE_STOCK')
   BEGIN 
       SELECT DISTINCT TOP 10 Z.ARTICLE AS 'MATERIAL' FROM [dbo].[tbl_ZCURR_STOCK] Z
	   WHERE (@fromdate ='' OR Z.Entry_Dt >= @fromdate) AND (@Todate ='' OR Z.Entry_Dt < DATEADD(DAY, 1, @Todate)) 
       AND (@Store_code = '' OR Z.PLANT =@Store_code) AND (@SearchTerm = '' OR Z.ARTICLE LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%');
  END

   ELSE If(@status='SEARCH_BIND_MATERIAL_FOR_LIVE_STOCK')
   BEGIN 
       SELECT DISTINCT Z.ARTICLE AS 'MATERIAL' FROM [dbo].[tbl_ZCURR_STOCK] Z
       WHERE (@fromdate ='' OR Z.Entry_Dt >= @fromdate) AND (@Todate ='' OR Z.Entry_Dt < DATEADD(DAY, 1, @Todate)) 
       AND (@Store_code = '' OR Z.PLANT =@Store_code) AND (@SearchTerm = '' OR Z.ARTICLE LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%');
   END

   ELSE If(@status='LIVE_STOCK_REPORT')
    BEGIN 
        ;WITH SAP_STOCK AS (SELECT CONVERT(DATE, Entry_Dt) AS EntryDate,PLANT,ARTICLE,SUM(STOCK) AS SAP_STOCK FROM [dbo].[tbl_ZCURR_STOCK]
        WHERE (@fromdate ='' OR ENTRY_DT >= @fromdate) AND (@Todate ='' OR ENTRY_DT < DATEADD(DAY, 1,@todate)) 
        AND (@Store_code='' OR PLANT =@Store_code)  AND (@Material='' OR ARTICLE =@Material) GROUP BY CONVERT(DATE, Entry_Dt), PLANT, ARTICLE),

        RFID_STOCK AS (SELECT S.Store_Code,S.Store_Name,EN.Material AS ARTICLE,COUNT(DISTINCT E.ENCODE_EPC) AS RFID_STOCK FROM dbo.tbl_Ean_Mat_Mst EN
        JOIN dbo.tbl_Encoding_Dtl E ON EN.EAN_UPC = E.EAN JOIN dbo.tbl_store_master S ON E.STORE_ID = S.STORE_ID 
        WHERE E.Is_Status IN ('1','2') AND E.Store_ID IS NOT NULL 
        AND (@Store_code='' OR S.Store_Code =@Store_code)  AND (@Material='' OR EN.Material =@Material)
        GROUP BY EN.Material, S.Store_Code,S.Store_Name)
		
  --      SELECT S.EntryDate AS 'STOCK_DATE',S.PLANT AS 'STORE_CODE',R.Store_Name AS 'STORE_NAME',S.ARTICLE,CAST(SUM(S.SAP_STOCK) AS INT) AS SAP_STOCK,CAST(SUM(ISNULL(R.RFID_STOCK,0)) AS INT) AS RFID_STOCK,
  --      CAST(SUM(S.SAP_STOCK) - SUM(ISNULL(R.RFID_STOCK,0)) AS INT) AS DIFF INTO #STOCKREPORT
  --      FROM SAP_STOCK S 
		--LEFT JOIN RFID_STOCK R ON S.ARTICLE = R.ARTICLE AND S.PLANT=R.Store_Code
		--LEFT JOIN dbo.tbl_store_master SM  ON S.PLANT = SM.Store_Code
  --      WHERE (@SearchTerm = '' OR S.ARTICLE LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%')
  --      GROUP BY S.EntryDate, S.PLANT,S.ARTICLE,R.Store_Name

  	SELECT S.EntryDate AS STOCK_DATE,S.PLANT AS STORE_CODE,SM.Store_Name,S.ARTICLE,
    CAST(SUM(S.SAP_STOCK) AS INT) AS SAP_STOCK,CAST(SUM(ISNULL(R.RFID_STOCK,0)) AS INT) AS RFID_STOCK,CAST(SUM(S.SAP_STOCK) - SUM(ISNULL(R.RFID_STOCK,0)) AS INT) AS DIFF 
	INTO #STOCKREPORT
    FROM SAP_STOCK S 
    LEFT JOIN RFID_STOCK R ON S.ARTICLE = R.ARTICLE AND S.PLANT = R.Store_Code 
    LEFT JOIN dbo.tbl_store_master SM  ON S.PLANT = SM.Store_Code   -- 🔥 important fix
	WHERE (@SearchTerm = '' OR S.ARTICLE LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%')
    GROUP BY S.EntryDate, S.PLANT, SM.Store_Name,S.ARTICLE;
      
              
        SELECT @RecordCount = COUNT(*),@QTY=SUM(SAP_STOCK),@ENCQTY=SUM(RFID_STOCK),@DIFFQTY=SUM(DIFF) FROM #STOCKREPORT;

        SET @SortDirection =
            CASE 
                WHEN LOWER(@SortDirection) = 'desc' THEN 'DESC'
                ELSE 'ASC'
            END;

            SET @OrderBy =              
                CASE @SortColumn
                            WHEN 'STORE_CODE' THEN 'R.STORE_CODE' 
							WHEN 'STORE_NAME' THEN 'R.STORE_NAME' 
                            WHEN 'ARTICLE' THEN 'R.ARTICLE'
							WHEN 'SAP_STOCK' THEN 'R.SAP_STOCK'
                            WHEN 'RFID_STOCK' THEN 'R.RFID_STOCK'
                            WHEN 'DIFF' THEN 'R.DIFF'
                            WHEN 'STOCK_DATE' THEN 'R.STOCK_DATE'
                            ELSE 'R.STOCK_DATE'
                END
                + ' ' + @SortDirection
                + ', R.STOCK_DATE';
                                           
            SET @SQL = N'SELECT *FROM (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY ' + @OrderBy + N') AS RowNumber,* FROM #STOCKREPORT R ) T
            WHERE RowNumber BETWEEN @StartRow AND @EndRow
            ORDER BY RowNumber;';

            EXEC sp_executesql
            @SQL,N'@StartRow INT, @EndRow INT',
            @StartRow = @StartRow,  @EndRow   = @EndRow;

            -- Clean up the temporary table
            DROP TABLE #STOCKREPORT;
    END

              
    ELSE If(@status='EXPORT_LIVE_STOCK_REPORT')
    BEGIN 
		;WITH SAP_STOCK AS (SELECT CONVERT(DATE, Entry_Dt) AS EntryDate,PLANT,ARTICLE,SUM(STOCK) AS SAP_STOCK FROM [dbo].[tbl_ZCURR_STOCK]
		WHERE (@fromdate ='' OR ENTRY_DT >= @fromdate) AND (@Todate ='' OR ENTRY_DT < DATEADD(DAY, 1,@todate)) 
		AND (@Store_code='' OR PLANT =@Store_code)  AND (@Material='' OR ARTICLE =@Material) GROUP BY CONVERT(DATE, Entry_Dt), PLANT, ARTICLE),

		RFID_STOCK AS (SELECT S.Store_Code,S.Store_Name,EN.Material AS ARTICLE,COUNT(DISTINCT E.ENCODE_EPC) AS RFID_STOCK FROM dbo.tbl_Ean_Mat_Mst EN
		JOIN dbo.tbl_Encoding_Dtl E ON EN.EAN_UPC = E.EAN JOIN dbo.tbl_store_master S ON E.STORE_ID = S.STORE_ID 
		WHERE E.Is_Status IN ('1','2') AND E.Store_ID IS NOT NULL 
		AND (@Store_code='' OR S.Store_Code =@Store_code)  AND (@Material='' OR EN.Material =@Material)
		GROUP BY EN.Material, S.Store_Code,S.Store_Name)

		
		--SELECT S.EntryDate AS 'STOCK_DATE',S.PLANT AS 'STORE_CODE',S.ARTICLE,CAST(SUM(S.SAP_STOCK) AS INT) AS SAP_STOCK,CAST(SUM(ISNULL(R.RFID_STOCK,0)) AS INT) AS RFID_STOCK,
		--CAST(SUM(S.SAP_STOCK) - SUM(ISNULL(R.RFID_STOCK,0)) AS INT) AS DIFF
		--FROM SAP_STOCK S LEFT JOIN RFID_STOCK R ON S.ARTICLE = R.ARTICLE AND S.PLANT=R.Store_Code 
		--GROUP BY S.EntryDate, S.PLANT,S.ARTICLE

	    SELECT S.EntryDate AS STOCK_DATE,S.PLANT AS STORE_CODE,SM.Store_Name,S.ARTICLE,
        CAST(SUM(S.SAP_STOCK) AS INT) AS SAP_STOCK,CAST(SUM(ISNULL(R.RFID_STOCK,0)) AS INT) AS RFID_STOCK,CAST(SUM(S.SAP_STOCK) - SUM(ISNULL(R.RFID_STOCK,0)) AS INT) AS DIFF 
        FROM SAP_STOCK S 
        LEFT JOIN RFID_STOCK R ON S.ARTICLE = R.ARTICLE AND S.PLANT = R.Store_Code 
        LEFT JOIN dbo.tbl_store_master SM  ON S.PLANT = SM.Store_Code   
        GROUP BY S.EntryDate, S.PLANT, SM.Store_Name,S.ARTICLE;
    END
	------------------FOR HHTGRC DATA---------------------------------------------

	ELSE If(@status='BIND_HU_FOR_HHTGRC')
	BEGIN
		SELECT DISTINCT TOP 10 SGM.STORE_GRC_HUNO AS 'HU' FROM [dbo].[tbl_STORE_GRC_MST_DTL] SGM 
		INNER JOIN dbo.tbl_GRC_DETAILS g ON g.HU = sgm.STORE_GRC_HUNO AND g.STORE_CODE = sgm.Reciving_Plant
		WHERE (@GRC_STATUS=''OR  SGM.Store_GRC_Complete_Status_Flg = @GRC_STATUS)
		 AND (@FromDate ='' OR TRY_CONVERT(date, G.GRC_DATE, 105) >= TRY_CONVERT(date, @FromDate, 105))
         AND (@ToDate='' OR TRY_CONVERT(date, G.GRC_DATE, 105) <= TRY_CONVERT(date, @ToDate, 105))
		--AND (@fromdate ='' OR sgm.Store_GRC_Complete_Status_Dt >= @fromdate) AND (@Todate ='' OR sgm.Store_GRC_Complete_Status_Dt < DATEADD(DAY, 1,@todate)) 
        AND (@Store_Code='' OR SGM.Reciving_Plant = @Store_Code)
		AND(@SearchTerm = '' OR SGM.STORE_GRC_HUNO LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%');
	END

	ELSE If(@status='SEARCH_BIND_HU_FOR_HHTGRC')
	BEGIN
	SELECT DISTINCT SGM.STORE_GRC_HUNO AS 'HU' FROM [dbo].[tbl_STORE_GRC_MST_DTL] SGM 
	INNER JOIN dbo.tbl_GRC_DETAILS g ON g.HU = sgm.STORE_GRC_HUNO AND g.STORE_CODE = sgm.Reciving_Plant
		WHERE (@GRC_STATUS=''OR  SGM.Store_GRC_Complete_Status_Flg = @GRC_STATUS)
		 AND (@FromDate ='' OR TRY_CONVERT(date, G.GRC_DATE, 105) >= TRY_CONVERT(date, @FromDate, 105))
         AND (@ToDate='' OR TRY_CONVERT(date, G.GRC_DATE, 105) <= TRY_CONVERT(date, @ToDate, 105))
		--AND (@fromdate ='' OR sgm.Store_GRC_Complete_Status_Dt >= @fromdate) AND (@Todate ='' OR sgm.Store_GRC_Complete_Status_Dt < DATEADD(DAY, 1,@todate)) 
        AND (@Store_Code='' OR SGM.Reciving_Plant = @Store_Code)
		AND(@SearchTerm = '' OR SGM.STORE_GRC_HUNO LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%');
	END

	ELSE If(@status='SHOW_HHTGRC_DATA')
    BEGIN	
		SELECT DISTINCT SGM.STORE_GRC_HUNO AS 'HU',SGM.Reciving_Plant AS 'STORE_CODE',IIF((SGM.Store_GRC_Complete_Status_Flg='1'),'VALIDATED AT STORE',SGM.Submit_Remark) AS 'RECEIVED_STATUS'
		--,FORMAT(SGM.Store_GRC_Complete_Status_Dt,'yyyy-MM-dd') AS 'GRC_DATE'
		,G.GRC_DATE AS 'GRC_DATE' 
		INTO #HHTGRCDATA
		FROM [dbo].[tbl_STORE_GRC_MST_DTL] SGM  
		INNER JOIN dbo.tbl_GRC_DETAILS g ON g.HU = sgm.STORE_GRC_HUNO AND g.STORE_CODE = sgm.Reciving_Plant
		WHERE (@GRC_STATUS=''OR  SGM.Store_GRC_Complete_Status_Flg = @GRC_STATUS) 
		 AND (@FromDate ='' OR TRY_CONVERT(date, G.GRC_DATE, 105) >= TRY_CONVERT(date, @FromDate, 105))
         AND (@ToDate='' OR TRY_CONVERT(date, G.GRC_DATE, 105) <= TRY_CONVERT(date, @ToDate, 105))
		--AND (@fromdate ='' OR SGM.Store_GRC_Complete_Status_Dt >= @fromdate) AND (@Todate ='' OR SGM.Store_GRC_Complete_Status_Dt < DATEADD(DAY, 1,@todate))
        AND (@Store_Code='' OR SGM.Reciving_Plant = @Store_Code)
		AND (@HU_NO='' OR SGM.STORE_GRC_HUNO = @HU_NO)
		AND(@SearchTerm = '' OR SGM.STORE_GRC_HUNO LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%' OR SGM.STORE_GRC_HUNO LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%')
	    --GROUP BY SGM.Reciving_Plant,SGM.STORE_GRC_HUNO,SGM.Store_GRC_Complete_Status_Flg,FORMAT(SGM.Store_GRC_Complete_Status_Dt,'yyyy-MM-dd')
		;

		SET @SortDirection =
				CASE 
					WHEN LOWER(@SortDirection) = 'desc' THEN 'DESC'
					ELSE 'ASC'
				END;

			SET @OrderBy =	
				CASE @SortColumn
					WHEN 'STORE_CODE' THEN 'R.STORE_CODE' 
					WHEN 'HU' THEN 'R.HU'
					WHEN 'RECEIVED_STATUS' THEN 'R.RECEIVED_STATUS'
					WHEN 'GRC_DATE' THEN 'R.GRC_DATE'
	                ELSE 'R.GRC_DATE'
				END
				+ ' ' + @SortDirection
				+ ', R.GRC_DATE ASC';
			
           	SET @SQL = N'SELECT *FROM (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY ' + @OrderBy + N') AS RowNumber,* FROM #HHTGRCDATA R ) T
			WHERE RowNumber BETWEEN @StartRow AND @EndRow
			ORDER BY RowNumber;';

			EXEC sp_executesql	@SQL,N'@StartRow INT, @EndRow INT',
			@StartRow = @StartRow,	@EndRow   = @EndRow;

		 SELECT @RecordCount = COUNT(*) FROM #HHTGRCDATA;
		
        -- Clean up the temporary table
		DROP TABLE #HHTGRCDATA;
    END

	ELSE If(@status='EXPORT_SHOW_HHTGRC_DATA')
    BEGIN	
		SELECT DISTINCT SGM.STORE_GRC_HUNO AS 'HU',SGM.Reciving_Plant AS 'STORE_CODE',IIF((SGM.Store_GRC_Complete_Status_Flg='1'),'VALIDATED AT STORE',SGM.Submit_Remark) AS 'RECEIVED_STATUS'
		--,FORMAT(SGM.Store_GRC_Complete_Status_Dt,'yyyy-MM-dd') AS 'GRC_DATE'
		,G.GRC_DATE AS 'GRC_DATE' 
		FROM [dbo].[tbl_STORE_GRC_MST_DTL] SGM  
		INNER JOIN dbo.tbl_GRC_DETAILS g ON g.HU = sgm.STORE_GRC_HUNO AND g.STORE_CODE = sgm.Reciving_Plant
		WHERE (@GRC_STATUS=''OR  SGM.Store_GRC_Complete_Status_Flg = @GRC_STATUS)
		 AND (@FromDate ='' OR TRY_CONVERT(date, G.GRC_DATE, 105) >= TRY_CONVERT(date, @FromDate, 105))
         AND (@ToDate='' OR TRY_CONVERT(date, G.GRC_DATE, 105) <= TRY_CONVERT(date, @ToDate, 105))
		--AND (@fromdate ='' OR SGM.Store_GRC_Complete_Status_Dt >= @fromdate) AND (@Todate ='' OR SGM.Store_GRC_Complete_Status_Dt < DATEADD(DAY, 1,@todate))
        AND (@Store_Code='' OR SGM.Reciving_Plant = @Store_Code)
		AND (@HU_NO='' OR SGM.STORE_GRC_HUNO = @HU_NO)
		--GROUP BY SGM.Reciving_Plant,SGM.STORE_GRC_HUNO,SGM.Store_GRC_Complete_Status_Flg,FORMAT(SGM.Store_GRC_Complete_Status_Dt,'yyyy-MM-dd')
		;
	END
	
	ELSE If(@status='VIEW_SHOW_HHTGRC_DATA')
    BEGIN	
		SELECT DISTINCT SGM.Reciving_Plant AS STORE_CODE,SM.STORE_NAME,SGM.STORE_GRC_HUNO AS 'HU',SGM.MAIN_ARTICLE,ISNULL(SGM.Article_Code,'NA') AS 'COMPONENT_ARTICLE',
		SGM.Act_Qty AS 'ACTUAL_QTY',SGM.Store_GRC_Qty AS 'QUANTITY',IIF((SGM.Store_GRC_Complete_Status_Flg='1'),'VALIDATED AT STORE',SGM.Submit_Remark) AS 'RECEIVED_STATUS',SGM.Store_GRC_Complete_Status_Dt AS 'GRC_DATE'
	    INTO #VIEWHHTGRCDATA
		FROM [dbo].[tbl_STORE_GRC_MST_DTL] SGM 
		INNER JOIN dbo.tbl_GRC_DETAILS g ON g.HU = sgm.STORE_GRC_HUNO AND g.STORE_CODE = sgm.Reciving_Plant
		LEFT JOIN [dbo].[tbl_Store_Master] SM ON SGM.Reciving_Plant=SM.Store_Code
	    WHERE(@GRC_STATUS=''OR  SGM.Store_GRC_Complete_Status_Flg = @GRC_STATUS) 
		 AND (@FromDate ='' OR TRY_CONVERT(date, G.GRC_DATE, 105) >= TRY_CONVERT(date, @FromDate, 105))
         AND (@ToDate='' OR TRY_CONVERT(date, G.GRC_DATE, 105) <= TRY_CONVERT(date, @ToDate, 105))
		--AND (@fromdate ='' OR SGM.Store_GRC_Complete_Status_Dt >= @fromdate) AND (@Todate ='' OR SGM.Store_GRC_Complete_Status_Dt < DATEADD(DAY, 1,@todate))
        AND (@Store_Code='' OR SGM.Reciving_Plant = @Store_Code)
		AND (@HU_NO='' OR SGM.STORE_GRC_HUNO = @HU_NO)
		AND(@SearchTerm = '' OR SGM.STORE_GRC_HUNO LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%' OR SGM.Store_GRC_Complete_Status_Dt LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%' OR SGM.MAIN_ARTICLE LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%' OR SGM.Article_Code LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%' OR SGM.Store_GRC_Qty LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%');
		SET @SortDirection =
				CASE 
					WHEN LOWER(@SortDirection) = 'desc' THEN 'DESC'
					ELSE 'ASC'
				END;

			SET @OrderBy =	
				CASE @SortColumn
					WHEN 'STORE_CODE' THEN 'R.STORE_CODE' 
					WHEN 'STORE_NAME' THEN 'R.STORE_NAME' 
					WHEN 'HU' THEN 'R.HU'
					WHEN 'MAIN_ARTICLE' THEN 'R.MAIN_ARTICLE'
					WHEN 'COMPONENT_ARTICLE' THEN 'R.COMPONENT_ARTICLE'
					WHEN 'QUANTITY' THEN 'R.QUANTITY' 
					WHEN 'RECEIVED_STATUS' THEN 'R.RECEIVED_STATUS'
					WHEN 'GRC_DATE' THEN 'R.GRC_DATE'
	                ELSE 'R.GRC_DATE'
				END
				+ ' ' + @SortDirection
				+ ', R.GRC_DATE ASC';
			
           	SET @SQL = N'SELECT *FROM (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY ' + @OrderBy + N') AS RowNumber,* FROM #VIEWHHTGRCDATA R ) T
			WHERE RowNumber BETWEEN @StartRow AND @EndRow
			ORDER BY RowNumber;';

			EXEC sp_executesql	@SQL,N'@StartRow INT, @EndRow INT',
			@StartRow = @StartRow,	@EndRow   = @EndRow;

            SELECT @QTY = SUM(QUANTITY),@ACTUALQTY=SUM(ACTUAL_QTY),@MATERIALCOUNT = COUNT(DISTINCT MAIN_ARTICLE), @RecordCount = COUNT(*) FROM #VIEWHHTGRCDATA;

	        DROP TABLE #VIEWHHTGRCDATA;
    END

	ELSE If(@status='EXPORT_VIEW_SHOW_HHTGRC_DATA')
    BEGIN	
       SELECT DISTINCT SGM.Reciving_Plant AS STORE_CODE,SM.STORE_NAME,SGM.STORE_GRC_HUNO AS 'HU',SGM.MAIN_ARTICLE,ISNULL(SGM.Article_Code,'NA') AS 'COMPONENT_ARTICLE',SGM.Act_Qty AS 'ACTUAL_QTY',SGM.Store_GRC_Qty AS 'QUANTITY',IIF((SGM.Store_GRC_Complete_Status_Flg='1'),'VALIDATED AT STORE',SGM.Submit_Remark) AS 'RECEIVED_STATUS',SGM.Store_GRC_Complete_Status_Dt AS 'GRC_DATE'
	   FROM [dbo].[tbl_STORE_GRC_MST_DTL] SGM 
	   INNER JOIN dbo.tbl_GRC_DETAILS g ON g.HU = sgm.STORE_GRC_HUNO AND g.STORE_CODE = sgm.Reciving_Plant
	   LEFT JOIN [dbo].[tbl_Store_Master] SM ON SGM.Reciving_Plant=SM.Store_Code
	   WHERE (@GRC_STATUS=''OR  SGM.Store_GRC_Complete_Status_Flg = @GRC_STATUS)
	    AND (@FromDate ='' OR TRY_CONVERT(date, G.GRC_DATE, 105) >= TRY_CONVERT(date, @FromDate, 105))
         AND (@ToDate='' OR TRY_CONVERT(date, G.GRC_DATE, 105) <= TRY_CONVERT(date, @ToDate, 105))
	   --AND (@fromdate ='' OR SGM.Store_GRC_Complete_Status_Dt >= @fromdate) AND (@Todate ='' OR SGM.Store_GRC_Complete_Status_Dt < DATEADD(DAY, 1,@todate))
       AND (@Store_Code='' OR SGM.Reciving_Plant = @Store_Code)
	   AND (@HU_NO='' OR SGM.STORE_GRC_HUNO = @HU_NO)
	END

	ELSE If(@status='DISPATCH_REPORT_DATA')
    BEGIN	
       SELECT CAST(DLVD.Entry_Dt AS DATE ) AS 'TRANS_DATE',DLVD.VEHICLE_NO FROM [dbo].[tbl_WH_DISPATCH_LOADING_VEHICLE_DTL] DLVD JOIN [dbo].[tbl_WH_DISPATCH_LOADING_DTL] DLD ON DLVD.RefNo=DLD.RefNo
       WHERE DLD.Is_Status='4' AND (@fromdate ='' OR DLD.CompletedDateTime >= @fromdate) AND (@Todate ='' OR DLD.CompletedDateTime < DATEADD(DAY, 1,@todate))
	END

    ELSE If(@status='DISPATCH_REPORT_DATA_VIEW')
    BEGIN	
       SELECT CAST(DLVD.Entry_Dt AS date) AS 'TRANS_DATE',DLVD.VEHICLE_NO,DM.RDC_STATE+DM.RDC_CODE AS 'RDC',DLD.HU_No AS 'HU_NO',DLD.Store AS 'STORE',DLD.Store_Desc AS 'STORE_DESC',
	   DLD.WH_DESC,DLD.CREATED_DATE AS 'HU_CREATED_DATE',DLD.Transfer_Value AS 'TRANSFER_VALUE', DLD.COUNT,DLVD.BAY,DLVD.TPT_NAME,DLVD.LABOURE_TYPE,DLVD.OPS_EXECUTIVE_NAME,
	   DLVD.DRIVER_NAME,DLVD.OUTBOUND_INCHARGE,DLVD.TOTAL_PKG,DLVD.SEAL_NO,DLVD.VEHICLE_STATUS,DLVD.DRIVER_MO_NO,DLVD.HOLD_1,DLVD.HOLD_2,DLVD.FINAL,DLVD.TYPE_OF_VEH,
	   DLVD.LOADING_TEAM,DLVD.COUNTED_BY,DLVD.VEHICLE_SIZE
	   FROM [dbo].[tbl_WH_DISPATCH_LOADING_VEHICLE_DTL] DLVD 
	   JOIN [dbo].[tbl_WH_DISPATCH_LOADING_DTL] DLD ON DLVD.RefNo=DLD.RefNo
	   JOIN [dbo].[tbl_WH_DISPATCH_MST] DM ON DLD.Store=DM.SITE
	   WHERE DLD.Is_Status='4' AND (@VEHICLE_NO='' OR DLVD.VEHICLE_NO=@VEHICLE_NO) 
	   AND (@fromdate ='' OR DLVD.Entry_Dt >= @fromdate) AND (@Todate ='' OR DLVD.Entry_Dt < DATEADD(DAY, 1,@todate))
	END

	---------FOR STORE PENDING GRC---------

	ELSE If(@status='BIND_HU_FOR_STORE_PENDING_GRC')
	BEGIN
		SELECT DISTINCT TOP 10 G.HU FROM [dbo].[tbl_GRC_DETAILS] G 
		LEFT JOIN dbo.tbl_STORE_GRC_MST_DTL SGM ON G.HU = SGM.STORE_GRC_HUNO AND G.STORE_CODE = SGM.Reciving_Plant
        WHERE --G.IS_STATUS = 1 AND 
		(@FromDate ='' OR TRY_CONVERT(date, G.GRC_DATE, 105) >= TRY_CONVERT(date, @FromDate, 105))
        AND (@ToDate='' OR TRY_CONVERT(date, G.GRC_DATE, 105) <= TRY_CONVERT(date, @ToDate, 105))
		AND (SGM.STORE_GRC_HUNO IS NULL OR ISNULL(SGM.Store_GRC_Complete_Status_Flg,0) NOT IN('1','2'))
        AND (@Store_Code='' OR G.STORE_CODE = @Store_Code)
		AND(@SearchTerm = '' OR G.HU LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%');
	END

	ELSE If(@status='SEARCH_BIND_HU_FOR_STORE_PENDING_GRC')
	BEGIN
		SELECT DISTINCT G.HU FROM [dbo].[tbl_GRC_DETAILS] G 
		LEFT JOIN dbo.tbl_STORE_GRC_MST_DTL SGM ON G.HU = SGM.STORE_GRC_HUNO AND G.STORE_CODE = SGM.Reciving_Plant
        WHERE --G.IS_STATUS = 1 AND
		 (@FromDate ='' OR TRY_CONVERT(date, G.GRC_DATE, 105) >= TRY_CONVERT(date, @FromDate, 105))
        AND (@ToDate='' OR TRY_CONVERT(date, G.GRC_DATE, 105) <= TRY_CONVERT(date, @ToDate, 105))
		AND (SGM.STORE_GRC_HUNO IS NULL OR ISNULL(SGM.Store_GRC_Complete_Status_Flg,0) NOT IN('1','2'))
        AND (@Store_Code='' OR G.STORE_CODE = @Store_Code)
		AND(@SearchTerm = '' OR G.HU LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%');
	END

	ELSE If(@status='SHOW_STORE_PENDING_GRC_DATA')
    BEGIN	
		SELECT DISTINCT G.HU ,G.STORE_CODE,'PENDING FOR STORE VALIDATION' AS 'RECEIVED_STATUS',G.GRC_DATE 
		INTO #STOREPENDINGGRCDATA
		FROM [dbo].[tbl_GRC_DETAILS] G 
		LEFT JOIN dbo.tbl_STORE_GRC_MST_DTL SGM ON G.HU = SGM.STORE_GRC_HUNO AND G.STORE_CODE = SGM.Reciving_Plant
         WHERE --G.IS_STATUS = 1 AND
		(@FromDate ='' OR TRY_CONVERT(date, G.GRC_DATE, 105) >= TRY_CONVERT(date, @FromDate, 105))
        AND (@ToDate='' OR TRY_CONVERT(date, G.GRC_DATE, 105) <= TRY_CONVERT(date, @ToDate, 105))
		AND (SGM.STORE_GRC_HUNO IS NULL OR ISNULL(SGM.Store_GRC_Complete_Status_Flg,0) NOT IN('1','2'))
        AND (@Store_Code='' OR G.STORE_CODE = @Store_Code)
		AND (@HU_NO='' OR G.HU = @HU_NO)
		AND(@SearchTerm = '' OR G.HU LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%' OR G.GRC_DATE LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%')
	    --GROUP BY G.STORE_CODE,G.HU,G.IS_STATUS,G.GRC_DATE
		;

		SET @SortDirection =
				CASE 
					WHEN LOWER(@SortDirection) = 'desc' THEN 'DESC'
					ELSE 'ASC'
				END;

			SET @OrderBy =	
				CASE @SortColumn
					WHEN 'STORE_CODE' THEN 'R.STORE_CODE' 
					WHEN 'HU' THEN 'R.HU'
					WHEN 'RECEIVED_STATUS' THEN 'R.RECEIVED_STATUS'
					WHEN 'GRC_DATE' THEN 'R.GRC_DATE'
	                ELSE 'R.GRC_DATE'
				END
				+ ' ' + @SortDirection
				+ ', R.GRC_DATE ASC';
			
           	SET @SQL = N'SELECT *FROM (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY ' + @OrderBy + N') AS RowNumber,* FROM #STOREPENDINGGRCDATA R ) T
			WHERE RowNumber BETWEEN @StartRow AND @EndRow
			ORDER BY RowNumber;';

			EXEC sp_executesql	@SQL,N'@StartRow INT, @EndRow INT',
			@StartRow = @StartRow,	@EndRow   = @EndRow;

		 SELECT @RecordCount = COUNT(*) FROM #STOREPENDINGGRCDATA;
		
        -- Clean up the temporary table
		DROP TABLE #STOREPENDINGGRCDATA;
    END

	ELSE If(@status='EXPORT_SHOW_STORE_PENDING_GRC_DATA')
    BEGIN	
		SELECT DISTINCT G.HU ,G.STORE_CODE,'PENDING FOR STORE VALIDATION' AS 'RECEIVED_STATUS',G.GRC_DATE 
	    FROM [dbo].[tbl_GRC_DETAILS] G 
		LEFT JOIN dbo.tbl_STORE_GRC_MST_DTL SGM ON G.HU = SGM.STORE_GRC_HUNO AND G.STORE_CODE = SGM.Reciving_Plant
        WHERE --G.IS_STATUS = 1 AND
		(@FromDate ='' OR TRY_CONVERT(date, G.GRC_DATE, 105) >= TRY_CONVERT(date, @FromDate, 105))
        AND (@ToDate='' OR TRY_CONVERT(date, G.GRC_DATE, 105) <= TRY_CONVERT(date, @ToDate, 105))
		AND (SGM.STORE_GRC_HUNO IS NULL OR ISNULL(SGM.Store_GRC_Complete_Status_Flg,0) NOT IN('1','2'))
        AND (@Store_Code='' OR G.STORE_CODE = @Store_Code)
		AND (@HU_NO='' OR G.HU = @HU_NO)
		--GROUP BY G.STORE_CODE,G.HU,G.IS_STATUS,G.GRC_DATE ORDER BY G.GRC_DATE DESC
		;
	END

	ELSE If(@status='VIEW_SHOW_STORE_PENDING_GRC_DATA')
    BEGIN	
		SELECT DISTINCT G.STORE_CODE,STORE_NAME,G.HU,G.MAIN_ARTICLE,ISNULL(G.COMPONENT_ARTICLE,'NA') AS 'COMPONENT_ARTICLE',G.QUANTITY as 'QUANTITY','PENDING FOR STORE VALIDATION' AS 'RECEIVED_STATUS',G.GRC_DATE
	    INTO #VIEWSHOWSTOREPENDINGGRCDATA
		FROM [dbo].[tbl_GRC_DETAILS] G 
		LEFT JOIN dbo.tbl_STORE_GRC_MST_DTL SGM ON G.HU = SGM.STORE_GRC_HUNO AND G.STORE_CODE = SGM.Reciving_Plant
        WHERE --G.IS_STATUS = 1 AND
		 (@FromDate ='' OR TRY_CONVERT(date, G.GRC_DATE, 105) >= TRY_CONVERT(date, @FromDate, 105))
        AND (@ToDate='' OR TRY_CONVERT(date, G.GRC_DATE, 105) <= TRY_CONVERT(date, @ToDate, 105))
		AND (SGM.STORE_GRC_HUNO IS NULL OR ISNULL(SGM.Store_GRC_Complete_Status_Flg,0) NOT IN('1','2'))
        AND (@Store_Code='' OR G.STORE_CODE = @Store_Code) 
		AND (@HU_NO='' OR G.HU = @HU_NO)
		AND(@SearchTerm = '' OR G.HU LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%' OR G.GRC_DATE LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%' OR G.MAIN_ARTICLE LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%' 
		 OR G.COMPONENT_ARTICLE LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%' OR G.QUANTITY LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%');
		SET @SortDirection =
				CASE 
					WHEN LOWER(@SortDirection) = 'desc' THEN 'DESC'
					ELSE 'ASC'
				END;

			SET @OrderBy =	
				CASE @SortColumn
					WHEN 'STORE_CODE' THEN 'R.STORE_CODE' 
					WHEN 'STORE_NAME' THEN 'R.STORE_NAME' 
					WHEN 'HU' THEN 'R.HU'
					WHEN 'MAIN_ARTICLE' THEN 'R.MAIN_ARTICLE'
					WHEN 'COMPONENT_ARTICLE' THEN 'R.COMPONENT_ARTICLE'
					WHEN 'QUANTITY' THEN 'R.QUANTITY' 
					WHEN 'RECEIVED_STATUS' THEN 'R.RECEIVED_STATUS'
					WHEN 'GRC_DATE' THEN 'R.GRC_DATE'
	                ELSE 'R.GRC_DATE'
				END
				+ ' ' + @SortDirection
				+ ', R.GRC_DATE ASC';
			
           	SET @SQL = N'SELECT *FROM (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY ' + @OrderBy + N') AS RowNumber,* FROM #VIEWSHOWSTOREPENDINGGRCDATA R ) T
			WHERE RowNumber BETWEEN @StartRow AND @EndRow
			ORDER BY RowNumber;';

			EXEC sp_executesql	@SQL,N'@StartRow INT, @EndRow INT',
			@StartRow = @StartRow,	@EndRow   = @EndRow;

            SELECT @QTY = SUM(QUANTITY),@MATERIALCOUNT = COUNT(DISTINCT MAIN_ARTICLE), @RecordCount = COUNT(*) FROM #VIEWSHOWSTOREPENDINGGRCDATA;

            -- Clean up the temporary table
	        DROP TABLE #VIEWSHOWSTOREPENDINGGRCDATA;
    END

	ELSE If(@status='EXPORT_VIEW_SHOW_STORE_PENDING_GRC_DATA')
    BEGIN	
		SELECT DISTINCT G.STORE_CODE,STORE_NAME,G.HU,G.MAIN_ARTICLE,ISNULL(G.COMPONENT_ARTICLE,'NA') AS 'COMPONENT_ARTICLE',G.QUANTITY as 'QUANTITY','PENDING FOR STORE VALIDATION' AS 'RECEIVED_STATUS',G.GRC_DATE
		FROM [dbo].[tbl_GRC_DETAILS] G 
		LEFT JOIN dbo.tbl_STORE_GRC_MST_DTL SGM ON G.HU = SGM.STORE_GRC_HUNO AND G.STORE_CODE = SGM.Reciving_Plant
        WHERE --G.IS_STATUS = 1 AND
	    (@FromDate ='' OR TRY_CONVERT(date, G.GRC_DATE, 105) >= TRY_CONVERT(date, @FromDate, 105))
        AND (@ToDate='' OR TRY_CONVERT(date, G.GRC_DATE, 105) <= TRY_CONVERT(date, @ToDate, 105))
		AND (SGM.STORE_GRC_HUNO IS NULL OR ISNULL(SGM.Store_GRC_Complete_Status_Flg,0) NOT IN('1','2'))
        AND (@Store_Code='' OR G.STORE_CODE = @Store_Code) 
		AND (@HU_NO='' OR G.HU = @HU_NO) ORDER BY G.GRC_DATE DESC
	END

	ELSE IF (@Status='BIND_WAREHOUSE_ENCODE_USERS')
	BEGIN
		SELECT DISTINCT TOP 10 Encode_By,User_Name FROM  dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl] E JOIN [dbo].[User_Registration] U ON E.Encode_By=U.User_ID 
		WHERE E.WH_ID IS NOT NULL AND E.Encode_Date >= @fromdate AND E.Encode_Date < DATEADD(DAY,1,@todate);
	END

	ELSE If(@status='SEARCH_BIND_WAREHOUSE_ENCODE_USERS')
    BEGIN  
		SELECT DISTINCT Encode_By,User_Name FROM  dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl] E JOIN [dbo].[User_Registration] U ON E.Encode_By=U.User_ID 
		WHERE E.WH_ID IS NOT NULL AND E.Encode_Date >= @fromdate AND E.Encode_Date < DATEADD(DAY,1,@todate);
	END

	ELSE If(@status='SHOW_WAREHOUSE_ENCODE_DATA')
    BEGIN	

	
		--SELECT E.Encode_By, U.User_Name,
		--SUM(CASE WHEN DATEPART(HOUR,E.Encode_Date)=8  THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS 'N8_TO_9',
		--SUM(CASE WHEN DATEPART(HOUR,E.Encode_Date)=9  THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS 'N9_TO_10',
		--SUM(CASE WHEN DATEPART(HOUR,E.Encode_Date)=10 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS 'N10_TO_11',
		--SUM(CASE WHEN DATEPART(HOUR,E.Encode_Date)=11 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS 'N11_TO_12',
		--SUM(CASE WHEN DATEPART(HOUR,E.Encode_Date)=12 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS 'N12_TO_1',
		--SUM(CASE WHEN DATEPART(HOUR,E.Encode_Date)=13 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS 'N1_TO_2',
		--SUM(CASE WHEN DATEPART(HOUR,E.Encode_Date)=14 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS 'N2_TO_3',
		--SUM(CASE WHEN DATEPART(HOUR,E.Encode_Date)=15 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS 'N3_TO_4',
		--SUM(CASE WHEN DATEPART(HOUR,E.Encode_Date)=16 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS 'N4_TO_5',
		--SUM(CASE WHEN DATEPART(HOUR,E.Encode_Date)=17 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS 'N5_TO_6',
		--SUM(CASE WHEN DATEPART(HOUR,E.Encode_Date)=18 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS 'N6_TO_7',
		--SUM(CASE WHEN DATEPART(HOUR,E.Encode_Date)=19 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS 'N7_TO_8',
		--COUNT(*) AS 'TOTAL_ENCODE_EPC',CAST(E.Encode_Date AS date) AS 'ENCODE_DATE' 
		--INTO #SHOWWAREHOUSEENCODEDATA
		--FROM  dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl] E JOIN [dbo].[User_Registration] U ON E.Encode_By=U.User_ID WHERE E.WH_ID IS NOT NULL
		--AND E.Encode_Date >= @FROMDATE AND E.Encode_Date < DATEADD(DAY,1,@TODATE) AND (@User_ID ='' OR E.Encode_By =@User_ID)
		--AND(@SearchTerm = '' OR U.User_Name LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%' OR E.Encode_Date LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%') 
		--GROUP BY E.Encode_By,U.User_Name,CAST(E.Encode_Date AS date);

		;WITH ENCODE_DATA AS
        (SELECT E.Encode_By, U.User_Name,
		SUM(CASE WHEN DATEPART(HOUR,E.Encode_Date)=8  THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS 'N8_TO_9',
		SUM(CASE WHEN DATEPART(HOUR,E.Encode_Date)=9  THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS 'N9_TO_10',
		SUM(CASE WHEN DATEPART(HOUR,E.Encode_Date)=10 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS 'N10_TO_11',
		SUM(CASE WHEN DATEPART(HOUR,E.Encode_Date)=11 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS 'N11_TO_12',
		SUM(CASE WHEN DATEPART(HOUR,E.Encode_Date)=12 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS 'N12_TO_1',
		SUM(CASE WHEN DATEPART(HOUR,E.Encode_Date)=13 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS 'N1_TO_2',
		SUM(CASE WHEN DATEPART(HOUR,E.Encode_Date)=14 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS 'N2_TO_3',
		SUM(CASE WHEN DATEPART(HOUR,E.Encode_Date)=15 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS 'N3_TO_4',
		SUM(CASE WHEN DATEPART(HOUR,E.Encode_Date)=16 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS 'N4_TO_5',
		SUM(CASE WHEN DATEPART(HOUR,E.Encode_Date)=17 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS 'N5_TO_6',
		SUM(CASE WHEN DATEPART(HOUR,E.Encode_Date)=18 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS 'N6_TO_7',
		SUM(CASE WHEN DATEPART(HOUR,E.Encode_Date)=19 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS 'N7_TO_8',
		COUNT(*) AS 'TOTAL_ENCODE_EPC',CAST(E.Encode_Date AS date) AS 'ENCODE_DATE' 
		FROM  dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl] E JOIN [dbo].[User_Registration] U ON E.Encode_By=U.User_ID WHERE E.WH_ID IS NOT NULL
		AND E.Encode_Date >= @FROMDATE AND E.Encode_Date < DATEADD(DAY,1,@TODATE) AND (@User_ID ='' OR E.Encode_By =@User_ID)
		GROUP BY E.Encode_By,U.User_Name,CAST(E.Encode_Date AS date)
		),

	    ERROR_RN AS (SELECT *,ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY EPC, CAST(Error_Dt AS DATE) ORDER BY Error_Time ASC) AS RN FROM dbo.tbl_Encoding_Error_Log
        WHERE Error_Dt >= @FROMDATE AND Error_Dt < DATEADD(DAY,1,@TODATE) AND (@User_ID = '' OR Encode_By = @User_ID)
		AND NOT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM dbo.tbl_Encoding_Dtl D WHERE D.Encode_EPC = EPC AND CAST(D.Encode_Date AS DATE) = CAST(Error_Dt AS DATE))),

        ERROR_DATA AS
		(SELECT CAST(E.Error_Dt AS DATE) AS ERROR_DATE,E.Encode_By,
		 SUM(CASE WHEN DATEPART(HOUR,E.Error_Time)=8 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)  AS 'N8_TO_9_ERR',
		 SUM(CASE WHEN DATEPART(HOUR,E.Error_Time)=9 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)  AS 'N9_TO_10_ERR',
		 SUM(CASE WHEN DATEPART(HOUR,E.Error_Time)=10 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS 'N10_TO_11_ERR',
		 SUM(CASE WHEN DATEPART(HOUR,E.Error_Time)=11 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS 'N11_TO_12_ERR',
		 SUM(CASE WHEN DATEPART(HOUR,E.Error_Time)=12 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS 'N12_TO_1_ERR',
		 SUM(CASE WHEN DATEPART(HOUR,E.Error_Time)=13 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS 'N1_TO_2_ERR',
		 SUM(CASE WHEN DATEPART(HOUR,E.Error_Time)=14 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS 'N2_TO_3_ERR',
		 SUM(CASE WHEN DATEPART(HOUR,E.Error_Time)=15 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS 'N3_TO_4_ERR',
		 SUM(CASE WHEN DATEPART(HOUR,E.Error_Time)=16 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS 'N4_TO_5_ERR',
		 SUM(CASE WHEN DATEPART(HOUR,E.Error_Time)=17 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS 'N5_TO_6_ERR',
		 SUM(CASE WHEN DATEPART(HOUR,E.Error_Time)=18 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS 'N6_TO_7_ERR',
		 SUM(CASE WHEN DATEPART(HOUR,E.Error_Time)=19 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS 'N7_TO_8_ERR',
		 COUNT(*) AS ERROR_COUNT
		 FROM ERROR_RN E WHERE E.RN = 1 GROUP BY CAST(E.Error_Dt AS DATE),E.Encode_By)

		 SELECT E.ENCODE_DATE,E.Encode_By,E.User_Name,
		 E.N8_TO_9,ISNULL(ER.N8_TO_9_ERR,0) AS 'N8_TO_9_ERR',
		 E.N9_TO_10,ISNULL(ER.N9_TO_10_ERR,0) AS 'N9_TO_10_ERR',
		 E.N10_TO_11,ISNULL(ER.N10_TO_11_ERR,0) AS 'N10_TO_11_ERR',
		 E.N11_TO_12,ISNULL(ER.N11_TO_12_ERR,0) AS 'N11_TO_12_ERR',
		 E.N12_TO_1,ISNULL(ER.N12_TO_1_ERR,0) AS 'N12_TO_1_ERR',
		 E.N1_TO_2,ISNULL(ER.N1_TO_2_ERR,0) AS 'N1_TO_2_ERR',
		 E.N2_TO_3,ISNULL(ER.N2_TO_3_ERR,0) AS 'N2_TO_3_ERR',
		 E.N3_TO_4,ISNULL(ER.N3_TO_4_ERR,0) AS 'N3_TO_4_ERR',
		 E.N4_TO_5,ISNULL(ER.N4_TO_5_ERR,0) AS 'N4_TO_5_ERR',
		 E.N5_TO_6,ISNULL(ER.N5_TO_6_ERR,0) AS 'N5_TO_6_ERR',
		 E.N6_TO_7,ISNULL(ER.N6_TO_7_ERR,0) AS 'N6_TO_7_ERR',
		 E.N7_TO_8,ISNULL(ER.N7_TO_8_ERR,0) AS 'N7_TO_8_ERR',
		 E.TOTAL_ENCODE_EPC,ISNULL(ER.ERROR_COUNT,0) AS 'TOTAL_ERROR_EPC',
		 CAST(CAST(ISNULL(ER.ERROR_COUNT,0) AS DECIMAL(18,2)) / NULLIF(E.TOTAL_ENCODE_EPC,0) * 100 AS DECIMAL(10,2)) AS 'ERR_PER'
		 INTO #SHOWWAREHOUSEENCODEDATA
		 FROM ENCODE_DATA E 
		 LEFT JOIN ERROR_DATA ER ON E.Encode_By = ER.Encode_By AND E.ENCODE_DATE = ER.ERROR_DATE 
		 WHERE (@SearchTerm = '' OR E.User_Name LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%' OR E.Encode_Date LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%') 
		 ORDER BY E.ENCODE_DATE DESC;


        SET @SortDirection =
				CASE 
					WHEN LOWER(@SortDirection) = 'desc' THEN 'DESC'
					ELSE 'ASC'
				END;

			SET @OrderBy =	
				CASE @SortColumn
					WHEN 'User_Name' THEN
					'LEFT(R.User_Name, PATINDEX(''%[0-9]%'', R.User_Name + ''0'') - 1),
					 ISNULL(TRY_CAST(SUBSTRING(R.User_Name,PATINDEX(''%[0-9]%'', R.User_Name + ''0''),LEN(R.User_Name)) AS INT),0)'
					 WHEN 'N8_TO_9' THEN 'R.N8_TO_9'  WHEN 'N8_TO_9_ERR' THEN 'R.N8_TO_9_ERR'
					 WHEN 'N9_TO_10' THEN 'R.N9_TO_10'  WHEN 'N9_TO_10_ERR' THEN 'R.N9_TO_10_ERR'
					 WHEN 'N10_TO_11' THEN 'R.N10_TO_11'  WHEN 'N10_TO_11_ERR' THEN 'R.N10_TO_11_ERR' 
					 WHEN 'N11_TO_12' THEN 'R.N11_TO_12' WHEN 'N11_TO_12_ERR' THEN 'R.N11_TO_12_ERR'
					 WHEN 'N12_TO_1' THEN 'R.N12_TO_1' WHEN 'N12_TO_1_ERR' THEN 'R.N12_TO_1_ERR' 
					 WHEN 'N1_TO_2' THEN 'R.N1_TO_2' WHEN 'N1_TO_2_ERR' THEN 'R.N1_TO_2_ERR'
					 WHEN 'N2_TO_3' THEN 'R.N2_TO_3' WHEN 'N2_TO_3_ERR' THEN 'R.N2_TO_3_ERR' 
					 WHEN 'N3_TO_4' THEN 'R.N3_TO_4' WHEN 'N3_TO_4_ERR' THEN 'R.N3_TO_4_ERR'
					 WHEN 'N4_TO_5' THEN 'R.N4_TO_5' WHEN 'N4_TO_5_ERR' THEN 'R.N4_TO_5_ERR'
					 WHEN 'N5_TO_6' THEN 'R.N5_TO_6' WHEN 'N5_TO_6_ERR' THEN 'R.N5_TO_6_ERR'
					 WHEN 'N6_TO_7' THEN 'R.N6_TO_7' WHEN 'N6_TO_7_ERR' THEN 'R.N6_TO_7_ERR'
					 WHEN 'N7_TO_8' THEN 'R.N7_TO_8'  WHEN 'N7_TO_8_ERR' THEN 'R.N7_TO_8_ERR' 
					 WHEN 'ENCODE_DATE' THEN 'R.ENCODE_DATE' WHEN 'TOTAL_ENCODE_EPC' THEN 'R.TOTAL_ENCODE_EPC' 
					 WHEN 'TOTAL_ERROR_EPC' THEN 'R.TOTAL_ERROR_EPC' WHEN 'ERR_PER' THEN 'R.ERR_PER'
					 ELSE 'R.ENCODE_DATE'
				END
				+ ' ' + @SortDirection
				+ ', R.ENCODE_DATE DESC';
			
           	SET @SQL = N'SELECT *FROM (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY ' + @OrderBy + N') AS RowNumber,* FROM #SHOWWAREHOUSEENCODEDATA R ) T
			WHERE RowNumber BETWEEN @StartRow AND @EndRow
			ORDER BY RowNumber;';

			EXEC sp_executesql	@SQL,N'@StartRow INT, @EndRow INT',
			@StartRow = @StartRow,	@EndRow   = @EndRow;

            SELECT @8TO9 = SUM(N8_TO_9),@8TO9_ERR = SUM(N8_TO_9_ERR),@9TO10=SUM(N9_TO_10),@9TO10_ERR=SUM(N9_TO_10_ERR),
			@10TO11=SUM(N10_TO_11),@10TO11_ERR=SUM(N10_TO_11_ERR),@11TO12=SUM(N11_TO_12),@11TO12_ERR=SUM(N11_TO_12_ERR),
			@12TO13=SUM(N12_TO_1),@12TO13_ERR=SUM(N12_TO_1_ERR),@13TO14=SUM(N1_TO_2),@13TO14_ERR=SUM(N1_TO_2_ERR),
			@14TO15=SUM(N2_TO_3),@14TO15_ERR=SUM(N2_TO_3_ERR),@15TO16=SUM(N3_TO_4),@15TO16_ERR=SUM(N3_TO_4_ERR),
			@16TO17=SUM(N4_TO_5),@16TO17_ERR=SUM(N4_TO_5_ERR),@17TO18=SUM(N5_TO_6),@17TO18_ERR=SUM(N5_TO_6_ERR),
			@18TO19 =SUM(N6_TO_7),@18TO19_ERR =SUM(N6_TO_7_ERR),@19TO20=SUM(N7_TO_8),@19TO20_ERR=SUM(N7_TO_8_ERR),
            @MRGQTY=SUM(N8_TO_9) + SUM(N9_TO_10) + SUM(N10_TO_11) + SUM(N11_TO_12) + SUM(N12_TO_1),
			@EVNQTY=SUM(N1_TO_2)+ SUM(N2_TO_3)+ SUM(N3_TO_4) + SUM(N4_TO_5) + SUM(N5_TO_6)+ SUM(N6_TO_7)+ SUM(N7_TO_8),
			@QTY = SUM(TOTAL_ENCODE_EPC),@ERRQTY=SUM(TOTAL_ERROR_EPC),@TotalCount = COUNT(DISTINCT User_Name),@RecordCount = COUNT(*) FROM #SHOWWAREHOUSEENCODEDATA;

			--SELECT @ENCQTY = COUNT(*),@AVGQTY=CAST(COUNT(*) * 1.0 / DAY(CAST(GETDATE() AS DATE)) AS DECIMAL(18,2))FROM  dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl] E 
			--WHERE E.WH_ID IS NOT NULL AND E.Encode_Date >= DATEADD(MONTH, DATEDIFF(MONTH, 0, CAST(GETDATE()AS DATE)), 0);

			SELECT @ENCQTY = COUNT(*), @AVGQTY = CAST(COUNT(*) * 1.0 / NULLIF(DAY(GETDATE()) - 1, 0) AS DECIMAL(18,2)) FROM dbo.tbl_Encoding_Dtl E
			WHERE E.WH_ID IS NOT NULL AND E.Encode_Date >= DATEADD(MONTH, DATEDIFF(MONTH, 0, GETDATE()), 0) AND E.Encode_Date < CAST(GETDATE() AS DATE);

			--SELECT  @T_ENC_QTY = COUNT(*),@T_ENC_USERS=COUNT(DISTINCT Encode_By) FROM dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl] E WHERE E.WH_ID IS NOT NULL;
			SELECT @T_ENC_QTY = COUNT(*), @T_ENC_USERS = COUNT(DISTINCT User_Name) FROM #SHOWWAREHOUSEENCODEDATA;
		
		
	
			DROP TABLE #SHOWWAREHOUSEENCODEDATA;
    END

	ELSE If(@status='EXPORT_SHOW_WAREHOUSE_ENCODE_DATA')
    BEGIN	
		--SELECT CAST(E.Encode_Date AS date) AS 'ENCODE_DATE' ,E.Encode_By, U.User_Name,
		--SUM(CASE WHEN DATEPART(HOUR,E.Encode_Date)=8  THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS '8 TO 9',
		--SUM(CASE WHEN DATEPART(HOUR,E.Encode_Date)=9  THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS '9 TO 10',
		--SUM(CASE WHEN DATEPART(HOUR,E.Encode_Date)=10 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS '10 TO 11',
		--SUM(CASE WHEN DATEPART(HOUR,E.Encode_Date)=11 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS '11 TO 12',
		--SUM(CASE WHEN DATEPART(HOUR,E.Encode_Date)=12 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS '12 TO 13',
		--SUM(CASE WHEN DATEPART(HOUR,E.Encode_Date)=13 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS '13 TO 14',
		--SUM(CASE WHEN DATEPART(HOUR,E.Encode_Date)=14 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS '14 TO 15',
		--SUM(CASE WHEN DATEPART(HOUR,E.Encode_Date)=15 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS '15 TO 16',
		--SUM(CASE WHEN DATEPART(HOUR,E.Encode_Date)=16 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS '16 TO 17',
		--SUM(CASE WHEN DATEPART(HOUR,E.Encode_Date)=17 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS '17 TO 18',
		--SUM(CASE WHEN DATEPART(HOUR,E.Encode_Date)=18 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS '18 TO 19',
		--SUM(CASE WHEN DATEPART(HOUR,E.Encode_Date)=19 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS '19 TO 20',
		--COUNT(*) AS 'TOTAL_ENCODE_EPC'
		--FROM  dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl] E JOIN [dbo].[User_Registration] U ON E.Encode_By=U.User_ID WHERE E.WH_ID IS NOT NULL
		--AND E.Encode_Date >= @FROMDATE AND E.Encode_Date < DATEADD(DAY,1,@TODATE) AND (@User_ID ='' OR E.Encode_By =@User_ID)
		--GROUP BY E.Encode_By,U.User_Name,CAST(E.Encode_Date AS date) ORDER BY CAST(E.Encode_Date AS date) DESC;

		;WITH ENCODE_DATA AS
        (SELECT E.Encode_By, U.User_Name,
		SUM(CASE WHEN DATEPART(HOUR,E.Encode_Date)=8  THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS 'N8_TO_9',
		SUM(CASE WHEN DATEPART(HOUR,E.Encode_Date)=9  THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS 'N9_TO_10',
		SUM(CASE WHEN DATEPART(HOUR,E.Encode_Date)=10 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS 'N10_TO_11',
		SUM(CASE WHEN DATEPART(HOUR,E.Encode_Date)=11 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS 'N11_TO_12',
		SUM(CASE WHEN DATEPART(HOUR,E.Encode_Date)=12 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS 'N12_TO_1',
		SUM(CASE WHEN DATEPART(HOUR,E.Encode_Date)=13 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS 'N1_TO_2',
		SUM(CASE WHEN DATEPART(HOUR,E.Encode_Date)=14 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS 'N2_TO_3',
		SUM(CASE WHEN DATEPART(HOUR,E.Encode_Date)=15 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS 'N3_TO_4',
		SUM(CASE WHEN DATEPART(HOUR,E.Encode_Date)=16 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS 'N4_TO_5',
		SUM(CASE WHEN DATEPART(HOUR,E.Encode_Date)=17 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS 'N5_TO_6',
		SUM(CASE WHEN DATEPART(HOUR,E.Encode_Date)=18 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS 'N6_TO_7',
		SUM(CASE WHEN DATEPART(HOUR,E.Encode_Date)=19 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS 'N7_TO_8',
		COUNT(*) AS 'TOTAL_ENCODE_EPC',CAST(E.Encode_Date AS date) AS 'ENCODE_DATE' 
		FROM  dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl] E JOIN [dbo].[User_Registration] U ON E.Encode_By=U.User_ID WHERE E.WH_ID IS NOT NULL
		AND E.Encode_Date >= @FROMDATE AND E.Encode_Date < DATEADD(DAY,1,@TODATE) AND (@User_ID ='' OR E.Encode_By =@User_ID)
		GROUP BY E.Encode_By,U.User_Name,CAST(E.Encode_Date AS date)
		),

		ERROR_RN AS (SELECT *,ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY EPC, CAST(Error_Dt AS DATE) ORDER BY Error_Time ASC) AS RN FROM dbo.tbl_Encoding_Error_Log
        WHERE Error_Dt >= @FROMDATE AND Error_Dt < DATEADD(DAY,1,@TODATE) AND (@User_ID = '' OR Encode_By = @User_ID)
		AND NOT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM dbo.tbl_Encoding_Dtl D WHERE D.Encode_EPC = EPC AND CAST(D.Encode_Date AS DATE) = CAST(Error_Dt AS DATE))),

		ERROR_DATA AS
		(SELECT CAST(E.Error_Dt AS DATE) AS ERROR_DATE,E.Encode_By,
		 SUM(CASE WHEN DATEPART(HOUR,E.Error_Time)=8 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)  AS 'N8_TO_9_ERR',
		 SUM(CASE WHEN DATEPART(HOUR,E.Error_Time)=9 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)  AS 'N9_TO_10_ERR',
		 SUM(CASE WHEN DATEPART(HOUR,E.Error_Time)=10 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS 'N10_TO_11_ERR',
		 SUM(CASE WHEN DATEPART(HOUR,E.Error_Time)=11 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS 'N11_TO_12_ERR',
		 SUM(CASE WHEN DATEPART(HOUR,E.Error_Time)=12 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS 'N12_TO_1_ERR',
		 SUM(CASE WHEN DATEPART(HOUR,E.Error_Time)=13 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS 'N1_TO_2_ERR',
		 SUM(CASE WHEN DATEPART(HOUR,E.Error_Time)=14 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS 'N2_TO_3_ERR',
		 SUM(CASE WHEN DATEPART(HOUR,E.Error_Time)=15 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS 'N3_TO_4_ERR',
		 SUM(CASE WHEN DATEPART(HOUR,E.Error_Time)=16 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS 'N4_TO_5_ERR',
		 SUM(CASE WHEN DATEPART(HOUR,E.Error_Time)=17 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS 'N5_TO_6_ERR',
		 SUM(CASE WHEN DATEPART(HOUR,E.Error_Time)=18 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS 'N6_TO_7_ERR',
		 SUM(CASE WHEN DATEPART(HOUR,E.Error_Time)=19 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS 'N7_TO_8_ERR',
		 COUNT(*) AS ERROR_COUNT
		  FROM ERROR_RN E WHERE E.RN = 1 GROUP BY CAST(E.Error_Dt AS DATE), E.Encode_By)

		 SELECT E.ENCODE_DATE,--E.Encode_By,
		 E.User_Name,
		 E.N8_TO_9 AS '8 TO 9 ENC',ISNULL(ER.N8_TO_9_ERR,0) AS '8 TO 9 ERR',
		 E.N9_TO_10 AS '9 TO 10 ENC',ISNULL(ER.N9_TO_10_ERR,0) AS '9 TO 10 ERR',
		 E.N10_TO_11 AS '10 TO 11 ENC',ISNULL(ER.N10_TO_11_ERR,0) AS '10 TO 11 ERR',
		 E.N11_TO_12 AS '11 TO 12 ENC',ISNULL(ER.N11_TO_12_ERR,0) AS '11 TO 12 ERR',
		 E.N12_TO_1 AS '12 TO 13 ENC',ISNULL(ER.N12_TO_1_ERR,0) AS '12 TO 13 ERR',
		 E.N1_TO_2 AS '13 TO 14 ENC',ISNULL(ER.N1_TO_2_ERR,0) AS '13 TO 14 ERR',
		 E.N2_TO_3 AS '14 TO 15 ENC',ISNULL(ER.N2_TO_3_ERR,0) AS '14 TO 15 ERR',
		 E.N3_TO_4 AS '15 TO 16 ENC',ISNULL(ER.N3_TO_4_ERR,0) AS '15 TO 16 ERR',
		 E.N4_TO_5 AS '16 TO 17 ENC',ISNULL(ER.N4_TO_5_ERR,0) AS '16 TO 17 ERR',
		 E.N5_TO_6 AS '17 TO 18 ENC',ISNULL(ER.N5_TO_6_ERR,0) AS '17 TO 18 ERR',
		 E.N6_TO_7 AS '18 TO 19 ENC',ISNULL(ER.N6_TO_7_ERR,0) AS '18 TO 19 ERR',
		 E.N7_TO_8 AS '19 TO 20 ENC',ISNULL(ER.N7_TO_8_ERR,0) AS '19 TO 20 ERR',
		 E.TOTAL_ENCODE_EPC,ISNULL(ER.ERROR_COUNT,0) AS 'TOTAL_ERROR_EPC',
		 CAST(CAST(ISNULL(ER.ERROR_COUNT,0) AS DECIMAL(18,2)) / NULLIF(E.TOTAL_ENCODE_EPC,0) * 100 AS DECIMAL(10,2)) AS 'ERROR_PERCENTAGE'
		 FROM ENCODE_DATA E LEFT JOIN ERROR_DATA ER ON E.Encode_By = ER.Encode_By AND E.ENCODE_DATE = ER.ERROR_DATE ORDER BY E.ENCODE_DATE DESC;


	END

	ELSE If(@status='VIEW_SHOW_WAREHOUSE_ENCODE_DATA')
    BEGIN	
		SELECT U.User_name AS 'USER_NAME','WAREHOUSE' AS 'LOCATION',E.EAN,E.Encode_EPC AS 'ENCODE_EPC',ISNULL(M.Material, 'NA') AS 'ARTICLE',
        ISNULL(M.ART_DESC, 'NA') AS 'ARTICLE_DESC',FORMAT(E.Encode_Date,'yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss') as 'ENCODE_DATE'
		INTO #VIEWSHOWWAREHOUSEENCODEDATA
		FROM dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl] E LEFT JOIN dbo.[tbl_Ean_Mat_Mst] EM ON E.ean = EM.EAN_UPC
	    LEFT JOIN dbo.[tbl_Material_Master] M ON EM.Material = M.Material LEFT JOIN dbo.[User_Registration] U ON E.Encode_By = U.User_ID
	    WHERE E.WH_ID IS NOT NULL AND E.Encode_Date >= @FROMDATE AND E.Encode_Date < DATEADD(DAY,1,@TODATE) AND (@User_ID ='' OR E.Encode_By =@User_ID)
	    AND (@SearchTerm = '' OR U.USER_NAME LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%' OR E.EAN LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%' OR M.Material LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%' 
	    OR M.ART_DESC LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%' OR E.Encode_EPC LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%')
        SET @SortDirection =
				CASE 
					WHEN LOWER(@SortDirection) = 'desc' THEN 'DESC'
					ELSE 'ASC'
				END;

			SET @OrderBy =	
				CASE @SortColumn
					WHEN 'USER_NAME' THEN 'R.USER_NAME'
					WHEN 'EAN' THEN 'R.EAN' 
					WHEN 'ENCODE_EPC' THEN 'R.ENCODE_EPC' 
					WHEN 'ARTICLE' THEN 'R.ARTICLE' 
					WHEN 'ARTICLE_DESC' THEN 'R.ARTICLE_DESC' 
					WHEN 'ENCODE_DATE' THEN 'R.ENCODE_DATE' 
			        ELSE 'R.ENCODE_DATE'
				END
				+ ' ' + @SortDirection
				+ ', R.ENCODE_DATE DESC';
			
           	SET @SQL = N'SELECT *FROM (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY ' + @OrderBy + N') AS RowNumber,* FROM #VIEWSHOWWAREHOUSEENCODEDATA R ) T
			WHERE RowNumber BETWEEN @StartRow AND @EndRow
			ORDER BY RowNumber;';

			EXEC sp_executesql	@SQL,N'@StartRow INT, @EndRow INT',
			@StartRow = @StartRow,	@EndRow   = @EndRow;

            SELECT @RecordCount = COUNT(*) FROM #VIEWSHOWWAREHOUSEENCODEDATA;

	        DROP TABLE #VIEWSHOWWAREHOUSEENCODEDATA;
    END

	ELSE If(@status='EXPORT_VIEW_SHOW_WAREHOUSE_ENCODE_DATA')
    BEGIN	
		SELECT U.User_name AS 'USER_NAME','WAREHOUSE' AS 'LOCATION',E.EAN,E.Encode_EPC AS 'ENCODE_EPC',ISNULL(M.Material, 'NA') AS 'ARTICLE',
        ISNULL(M.ART_DESC, 'NA') AS 'ARTICLE_DESC',FORMAT(E.Encode_Date,'yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss') as 'ENCODE_DATE'
		FROM dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl] E LEFT JOIN dbo.[tbl_Ean_Mat_Mst] EM ON E.ean = EM.EAN_UPC
	    LEFT JOIN dbo.[tbl_Material_Master] M ON EM.Material = M.Material LEFT JOIN dbo.[User_Registration] U ON E.Encode_By = U.User_ID
	    WHERE E.WH_ID IS NOT NULL AND E.Encode_Date >= @FROMDATE AND E.Encode_Date < DATEADD(DAY,1,@TODATE) AND (@User_ID ='' OR E.Encode_By =@User_ID)
	END

	ELSE If(@status='EXPORT_ALL_VIEW_SHOW_WAREHOUSE_ENCODE_DATA')
    BEGIN	
		SELECT U.User_name AS 'USER_NAME','WAREHOUSE' AS 'LOCATION',E.EAN,E.Encode_EPC AS 'ENCODE_EPC',ISNULL(M.Material, 'NA') AS 'ARTICLE',
        ISNULL(M.ART_DESC, 'NA') AS 'ARTICLE_DESC',FORMAT(E.Encode_Date,'yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss') as 'ENCODE_DATE'
		FROM dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl] E LEFT JOIN dbo.[tbl_Ean_Mat_Mst] EM ON E.ean = EM.EAN_UPC
	    LEFT JOIN dbo.[tbl_Material_Master] M ON EM.Material = M.Material LEFT JOIN dbo.[User_Registration] U ON E.Encode_By = U.User_ID
	    WHERE E.WH_ID IS NOT NULL AND E.Encode_Date >= @FROMDATE AND E.Encode_Date < DATEADD(DAY,1,@TODATE) AND (@User_ID ='' OR E.Encode_By =@User_ID)
	END

	ELSE If(@status='SHOW_VALIDATE_HU_DATA')
    BEGIN	
		SELECT S.Reciving_Plant AS 'STORE_NAME',S.HU_No AS 'HU_NUMBER',CAST(SUM(S.Act_Qty) AS INT) as 'ACT_QTY',CAST (SUM(S.Scan_Qty)AS INT) as 'SCAN_QTY',
        CAST (S.Sending_HU_Scan_Dt AS date) AS 'HU_SCAN_DATE'
		INTO #SHOWVALIDATEHUDATA
		FROM dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl] S WHERE S.Is_Complete_Status_Flg='1'AND S.Ref_No IS NOT NULL AND S.Ref_No <>'' 
        AND S.Sending_HU_Scan_Dt >= @FROMDATE AND S.Sending_HU_Scan_Dt < DATEADD(DAY,1,@TODATE) AND (@HU_NO ='' OR S.HU_No =@HU_NO)
		AND(@SearchTerm = '' OR S.Reciving_Plant LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%' OR S.HU_No LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%')
        GROUP BY S.Reciving_Plant,S.HU_No,CAST (S.Sending_HU_Scan_Dt AS date)
		
		SET @SortDirection =
				CASE 
					WHEN LOWER(@SortDirection) = 'desc' THEN 'DESC'
					ELSE 'ASC'
				END;

			SET @OrderBy =	
				CASE @SortColumn
					WHEN 'STORE_NAME' THEN 'R.STORE_NAME' 
					WHEN 'ACT_QTY' THEN 'R.ACT_QTY' 
					WHEN 'SCAN_QTY' THEN 'R.SCAN_QTY'
			        ELSE 'R.HU_SCAN_DATE'
				END
				+ ' ' + @SortDirection
				+ ', R.HU_SCAN_DATE DESC';
			
           	SET @SQL = N'SELECT *FROM (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY ' + @OrderBy + N') AS RowNumber,* FROM #SHOWVALIDATEHUDATA R ) T
			WHERE RowNumber BETWEEN @StartRow AND @EndRow
			ORDER BY RowNumber;';

			EXEC sp_executesql	@SQL,N'@StartRow INT, @EndRow INT',
			@StartRow = @StartRow,	@EndRow   = @EndRow;

            SELECT @ACTUALQTY = SUM(ACT_QTY),@SCANQTY=SUM(SCAN_QTY),@RecordCount = COUNT(*) FROM #SHOWVALIDATEHUDATA;
			

	        DROP TABLE #SHOWVALIDATEHUDATA;
    END

	ELSE If(@status='EXPORT_SHOW_VALIDATE_HU_DATA')
    BEGIN	
		SELECT S.Reciving_Plant AS 'STORE_NAME',S.HU_No AS 'HU_NUMBER',CAST(SUM(S.Act_Qty) AS INT) as 'ARTICLE QTY',CAST (SUM(S.Scan_Qty)AS INT) as 'SCANNED QTY',
        CAST (S.Sending_HU_Scan_Dt AS date) AS 'SCANNED DATE'
        FROM dbo.[tbl_SAP_DC_Outward_Dtl] S WHERE S.Is_Complete_Status_Flg='1'AND S.Ref_No IS NOT NULL AND S.Ref_No <>'' 
        AND S.Sending_HU_Scan_Dt >= @FROMDATE AND S.Sending_HU_Scan_Dt < DATEADD(DAY,1,@TODATE) AND (@HU_NO ='' OR S.HU_No =@HU_NO)
		AND(@SearchTerm = '' OR S.Reciving_Plant LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%' OR S.HU_No LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%')
        GROUP BY S.Reciving_Plant,S.HU_No,CAST (S.Sending_HU_Scan_Dt AS date)
	END

	ELSE If(@status='TAG_CYCLE_COUNT')
    BEGIN	
		--SELECT DISTINCT E.TID AS 'TAG_DETAILS',COUNT(*) AS 'CYCLE_COUNT',CAST(GETDATE() AS DATE) AS 'DATE' 
		--INTO #TAGCYCLECOUNT FROM  dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl] E WHERE E.TID <> '' AND E.TID <> 'NULL' AND E.Is_Status <> 'R' 
		--AND(@SearchTerm = '' OR E.TID LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%') GROUP BY TID;

		SELECT DISTINCT E.TAG_ID AS 'TAG_DETAILS',SUM(RECYCLE_COUNT) AS 'CYCLE_COUNT',CAST(GETDATE() AS DATE) AS 'DATE' 
		INTO #TAGCYCLECOUNT FROM  dbo.[tbl_TAGMANAGEMENT_HISTORY] E WHERE (@SearchTerm = '' OR E.TAG_ID LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%')  GROUP BY TAG_ID;

        SET @SortDirection =
				CASE 
					WHEN LOWER(@SortDirection) = 'desc' THEN 'DESC'
					ELSE 'ASC'
				END;

			SET @OrderBy =	
				CASE @SortColumn
					
					WHEN 'TID' THEN 'R.TID' 
					WHEN 'CYCLE_COUNT' THEN 'R.CYCLE_COUNT'
			        ELSE 'R.CYCLE_COUNT'
				END
				+ ' ' + @SortDirection
				+ ', R.CYCLE_COUNT ASC';
			
           	SET @SQL = N'SELECT *FROM (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY ' + @OrderBy + N') AS RowNumber,* FROM #TAGCYCLECOUNT R ) T
			WHERE RowNumber BETWEEN @StartRow AND @EndRow
			ORDER BY RowNumber;';

			EXEC sp_executesql	@SQL,N'@StartRow INT, @EndRow INT',
			@StartRow = @StartRow,	@EndRow   = @EndRow;

            SELECT @QTY = SUM(CYCLE_COUNT),@RecordCount = COUNT(*) FROM #TAGCYCLECOUNT;

			;WITH EPC_Counts AS
			--(SELECT E.TID,COUNT(*) AS Cycle_Count FROM dbo.tbl_Encoding_Dtl E WHERE E.TID <> '' AND E.TID <> 'NULL' AND E.Is_Status <> 'R' GROUP BY E.TID)
			(SELECT E.TAG_ID AS 'TID',SUM(RECYCLE_COUNT) AS Cycle_Count FROM  dbo.[tbl_TAGMANAGEMENT_HISTORY] E GROUP BY TAG_ID)

            SELECT CASE WHEN Cycle_Count = 1 THEN '1' WHEN Cycle_Count= 2 THEN '2' WHEN Cycle_Count = 3 THEN '3' WHEN Cycle_Count = 4 THEN '4' ELSE '>=5' END AS Count_Range,
			COUNT(*) AS EPC_Count,CASE WHEN Cycle_Count = 1 THEN 1 WHEN Cycle_Count = 2 THEN 2 WHEN Cycle_Count = 3 THEN 3 WHEN Cycle_Count = 4 THEN 4 ELSE 5 END AS SortOrder 
			FROM EPC_Counts GROUP BY CASE WHEN Cycle_Count = 1 THEN '1' WHEN Cycle_Count= 2 THEN '2' WHEN Cycle_Count = 3 THEN '3' WHEN Cycle_Count = 4 THEN '4'  ELSE '>=5' END,
			CASE WHEN Cycle_Count = 1 THEN 1 WHEN Cycle_Count = 2 THEN 2 WHEN Cycle_Count = 3 THEN 3 WHEN Cycle_Count = 4 THEN 4 ELSE 5 END ORDER BY SortOrder;

			DROP TABLE #TAGCYCLECOUNT;
    END

	ELSE If(@status='EXPORT_TAG_CYCLE_COUNT')
    BEGIN	
		SELECT DISTINCT E.TID AS 'TAG_DETAILS',COUNT(*) AS 'CYCLE_COUNT',CAST(GETDATE() AS DATE) AS 'DATE' 
		FROM  dbo.[tbl_Encoding_Dtl] E WHERE E.TID <> '' AND E.TID <> 'NULL' AND E.Is_Status <> 'R' AND(@SearchTerm = '' OR E.TID LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%') 
		GROUP BY TID ORDER BY CYCLE_COUNT DESC;
    END

	
	ELSE If(@status='TAG_MANAGEMENT_LOCATION') 
    BEGIN	
		SELECT DATE, TAG_ID as 'TID', RECYCLE_COUNT 'CYCLE_COUNT',TAG_LOCATION AS LOCATION, ISNULL(NULLIF(LOCATION_NAME,''),'NA') AS LOCATION_NAME, STATUS INTO #TAGMANAGEMENTLOCATION FROM [dbo].[tbl_tagmanagement_history]
		where (@SearchTerm = '' OR TAG_ID LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%' OR RECYCLE_COUNT LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%' OR TAG_LOCATION LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%'  OR LOCATION_NAME LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%' 
		OR STATUS LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%');
 
		SET @SortDirection =
				CASE 
					WHEN LOWER(@SortDirection) = 'desc' THEN 'DESC'
					ELSE 'ASC'
				END;

			SET @OrderBy =	
				CASE @SortColumn
					
					WHEN 'TID' THEN 'R.TID' 
					WHEN 'CYCLE_COUNT' THEN 'R.CYCLE_COUNT' 
					WHEN 'LOCATION' THEN 'R.LOCATION'
					WHEN 'LOCATION_NAME' THEN 'R.LOCATION_NAME'
					WHEN 'STATUS' THEN 'R.STATUS'
			        ELSE 'R.STATUS'
				END
				+ ' ' + @SortDirection
				+ ', R.STATUS';
			
           	SET @SQL = N'SELECT *FROM (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY ' + @OrderBy + N') AS RowNumber,* FROM #TAGMANAGEMENTLOCATION R ) T
			WHERE RowNumber BETWEEN @StartRow AND @EndRow
			ORDER BY RowNumber;';

			EXEC sp_executesql	@SQL,N'@StartRow INT, @EndRow INT',
			@StartRow = @StartRow,	@EndRow   = @EndRow;

            SELECT @RecordCount = COUNT(*), @QTY = SUM(CYCLE_COUNT),@STORECOUNT= SUM(CASE WHEN LOCATION ='Store' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END),@WHCOUNT= SUM(CASE WHEN LOCATION ='Warehouse' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)
		 --   ,@CYCLE_COUNT= SUM(CASE WHEN STATUS ='Cycle Count' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END),@GRC_DONE= SUM(CASE WHEN STATUS ='GRC Done' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END),
			--@GRC_INCOMPLETE= SUM(CASE WHEN STATUS ='GRC Incomplete' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END),@HU_VALIDATION= SUM(CASE WHEN STATUS ='HU Validation' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END),
			--@POS_CHECKOUT= SUM(CASE WHEN STATUS ='POS Checkout' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END),@STOCK_TAKE= SUM(CASE WHEN STATUS ='Stock Take' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END),
			--@STORE_ENCODEING= SUM(CASE WHEN STATUS ='Store Encoding' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END),@WAREHOUSE_ENCODEING= SUM(CASE WHEN STATUS ='Warehouse Encoding' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END),
			--@WAREHOUSE_INWARD= SUM(CASE WHEN STATUS ='Warehouse Inward' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) 
			FROM #TAGMANAGEMENTLOCATION;

            DROP TABLE #TAGMANAGEMENTLOCATION;

			SELECT ISNULL(SM.Store_Name, 'GRC INCOMPLETE') AS'STORE_NAME',COUNT(*) AS 'TAG_COUNT' FROM dbo.tbl_TAGMANAGEMENT_HISTORY TH LEFT JOIN dbo.tbl_Store_Master SM
			ON CASE WHEN TH.LOCATION_NAME LIKE '% - %' THEN LEFT(TH.LOCATION_NAME, CHARINDEX(' - ', TH.LOCATION_NAME) - 1) ELSE TH.LOCATION_NAME END = SM.Store_Code
		    WHERE TH.TAG_LOCATION = 'STORE' GROUP BY ISNULL(SM.Store_Name, 'GRC INCOMPLETE') ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC;
    END

	ELSE If(@status='EXPORT_TAG_MANAGEMENT_LOCATION')
    BEGIN	
		SELECT DATE, TAG_ID as 'TID', RECYCLE_COUNT 'CYCLE_COUNT',TAG_LOCATION AS LOCATION, ISNULL(NULLIF(LOCATION_NAME,''),'NA') AS LOCATION_NAME, STATUS FROM [dbo].[tbl_tagmanagement_history] order by RECYCLE_COUNT DESC;
    END

	ELSE If(@status='TAG_MANAGEMENT_LOCATION_COUNT')
    BEGIN	
		;WITH CTE AS (SELECT TID,MAX(Encode_Date) AS Encode_Date FROM dbo.tbl_Encoding_Dtl WHERE TID IS NOT NULL AND TID <> '' AND TID <> 'null' AND Is_Status <> 'R'
              GROUP BY TID)

        SELECT CAST(GETDATE() AS DATE) AS 'DATE',SUM(CASE WHEN E.WH_ID IS NOT NULL AND E.Store_ID IS NULL THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS 'WAREHOUSE', 
		SUM(CASE WHEN E.Store_ID IS NOT NULL THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS 'STORE' INTO #TAGMANAGEMENTLOCATIONCOUNT
        FROM CTE C JOIN dbo.tbl_Encoding_Dtl E ON E.TID = C.TID AND E.Encode_Date = C.Encode_Date;

		
        SET @SortDirection =
				CASE 
					WHEN LOWER(@SortDirection) = 'desc' THEN 'DESC'
					ELSE 'ASC'
				END;

			SET @OrderBy =	
				CASE @SortColumn
					WHEN 'DATE' THEN 'R.DATE' 
					WHEN 'STORE' THEN 'R.STORE' 
					WHEN 'WAREHOUSE' THEN 'R.WAREHOUSE'
				    ELSE 'R.STORE'
				END
				+ ' ' + @SortDirection
				+ ', R.STORE';
			
           	SET @SQL = N'SELECT *FROM (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY ' + @OrderBy + N') AS RowNumber,* FROM #TAGMANAGEMENTLOCATIONCOUNT R ) T
			WHERE RowNumber BETWEEN @StartRow AND @EndRow
			ORDER BY RowNumber;';

			EXEC sp_executesql	@SQL,N'@StartRow INT, @EndRow INT',
			@StartRow = @StartRow,	@EndRow   = @EndRow;

            SELECT @RecordCount = COUNT(*) FROM #TAGMANAGEMENTLOCATIONCOUNT;
			

	        DROP TABLE #TAGMANAGEMENTLOCATIONCOUNT;

    END

	ELSE If(@status='ERROR_COUNT_REPORT')
    BEGIN	
		SELECT DISTINCT E.Encode_By,UR.User_Name AS 'ERROR_BY',COUNT(*) AS 'ERROR_COUNT' INTO #ERROR_COUNT FROM dbo.tbl_Encoding_Error_Log E LEFT JOIN dbo.User_Registration UR ON  E.ENCODE_BY=UR.User_ID
		WHERE Error_Dt >= @fromdate AND Error_Dt < DATEADD(DAY,1,@todate) 
		AND (@SearchTerm = '' OR User_Name LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%')
		GROUP BY E.Encode_By,UR.User_Name;
		
        SET @SortDirection =
				CASE 
					WHEN LOWER(@SortDirection) = 'desc' THEN 'DESC'
					ELSE 'ASC'
				END;

			SET @OrderBy =	
				CASE @SortColumn
					WHEN 'ERROR_BY' THEN 'R.ERROR_BY' 
					WHEN 'ERROR_COUNT' THEN 'R.ERROR_COUNT' 
					ELSE 'R.ERROR_BY'
				END
				+ ' ' + @SortDirection
				+ ', R.ERROR_BY';
			
           	SET @SQL = N'SELECT *FROM (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY ' + @OrderBy + N') AS RowNumber,* FROM #ERROR_COUNT R ) T
			WHERE RowNumber BETWEEN @StartRow AND @EndRow
			ORDER BY RowNumber;';

			EXEC sp_executesql	@SQL,N'@StartRow INT, @EndRow INT',
			@StartRow = @StartRow,	@EndRow   = @EndRow;

            SELECT @RecordCount = COUNT(*),@TotalCount=SUM(ERROR_COUNT) FROM #ERROR_COUNT;
			

	        DROP TABLE #ERROR_COUNT;

    END

	ELSE If(@status='EXPORT_ERROR_COUNT_REPORT')
    BEGIN	
		SELECT DISTINCT E.Encode_By,UR.User_Name AS 'ERROR_BY',COUNT(*) AS 'ERROR_COUNT' FROM dbo.tbl_Encoding_Error_Log E LEFT JOIN dbo.User_Registration UR ON  E.ENCODE_BY=UR.User_ID
		WHERE Error_Dt >= @fromdate AND Error_Dt < DATEADD(DAY,1,@todate) GROUP BY E.Encode_By,UR.User_Name;
	END

	ELSE If(@status='ERRORMSG_COUNT_REPORT')
    BEGIN	
       ;WITH ErrorData AS (SELECT CAST(Error_Dt AS DATE) AS Error_Date,ENCODE_BY,UR.USER_NAME,
                    CASE WHEN Error_Msg LIKE 'Scan EAN Tag%' THEN 'Already Encoded' ELSE Error_Msg END AS ErrorGroup,
                    ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY EPC, CAST(Error_Dt AS DATE) ORDER BY Error_Dt) AS RN
                    FROM dbo.tbl_Encoding_Error_Log LEFT JOIN dbo.User_Registration UR ON  ENCODE_BY=UR.User_ID
					WHERE Error_Dt >= @FROMDATE AND Error_Dt < DATEADD(DAY,1,@TODATE) AND Encode_By = @User_ID
					AND NOT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM dbo.tbl_Encoding_Dtl D WHERE D.Encode_EPC = EPC AND CAST(D.Encode_Date AS DATE) = CAST(Error_Dt AS DATE)))

        SELECT Error_Date AS 'ERROR_DATE',ENCODE_BY,USER_NAME,ErrorGroup AS ERROR_MSG,COUNT(*) AS ERROR_COUNT INTO #ERRORMSGCOUNTREPORT FROM ErrorData WHERE RN = 1 
		AND (@SearchTerm = '' OR User_Name LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%'OR ErrorGroup LIKE '%')
		GROUP BY Error_Date,ENCODE_BY,User_Name,ErrorGroup;

		
        SET @SortDirection =
				CASE 
					WHEN LOWER(@SortDirection) = 'desc' THEN 'DESC'
					ELSE 'ASC'
				END;

			SET @OrderBy =	
				CASE @SortColumn
					WHEN 'ERROR_DATE' THEN 'R.ERROR_DATE' 
					WHEN 'USER_NAME' THEN 'R.USER_NAME' 
					WHEN 'ERROR_MSG' THEN 'R.ERROR_MSG' 
					WHEN 'ERROR_COUNT' THEN 'R.ERROR_COUNT' 
					ELSE 'R.ERROR_DATE'
				END
				+ ' ' + @SortDirection
				+ ', R.ERROR_DATE';
			
           	SET @SQL = N'SELECT *FROM (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY ' + @OrderBy + N') AS RowNumber,* FROM #ERRORMSGCOUNTREPORT R ) T
			WHERE RowNumber BETWEEN @StartRow AND @EndRow
			ORDER BY RowNumber;';

			EXEC sp_executesql	@SQL,N'@StartRow INT, @EndRow INT',
			@StartRow = @StartRow,	@EndRow   = @EndRow;

            SELECT @RecordCount = COUNT(*),@TotalCount=SUM(ERROR_COUNT) FROM #ERRORMSGCOUNTREPORT;
			

	        DROP TABLE #ERRORMSGCOUNTREPORT;

    END

	ELSE If(@status='EXPORT_ERRORMSG_COUNT_REPORT')
    BEGIN	
       ;WITH ErrorData AS (SELECT CAST(Error_Dt AS DATE) AS Error_Date,ENCODE_BY,UR.USER_NAME,
                    CASE WHEN Error_Msg LIKE 'Scan EAN Tag%' THEN 'Already Encoded' ELSE Error_Msg END AS ErrorGroup,
                    ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY EPC, CAST(Error_Dt AS DATE) ORDER BY Error_Dt) AS RN
                    FROM dbo.tbl_Encoding_Error_Log LEFT JOIN dbo.User_Registration UR ON  ENCODE_BY=UR.User_ID
					WHERE Error_Dt >= @FROMDATE AND Error_Dt < DATEADD(DAY,1,@TODATE) AND Encode_By = @User_ID
					AND NOT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM dbo.tbl_Encoding_Dtl D WHERE D.Encode_EPC = EPC AND CAST(D.Encode_Date AS DATE) = CAST(Error_Dt AS DATE)))


        SELECT Error_Date AS 'ERROR_DATE',ENCODE_BY,USER_NAME,ErrorGroup AS ERROR_MSG,COUNT(*) AS ERROR_COUNT FROM ErrorData WHERE RN = 1 
	    GROUP BY Error_Date,ENCODE_BY,User_Name,ErrorGroup;
	END

    ELSE If(@status='VIEW_ERROR_COUNT_REPORT')
    BEGIN	
	   ;WITH ErrorData AS (SELECT EPC,Error_Msg,Error_Dt,Error_Time,Encode_By,ROW_NUMBER() OVER(PARTITION BY EPC, CAST(Error_Dt AS DATE)ORDER BY Error_Dt
                    ) AS RN FROM dbo.tbl_Encoding_Error_Log WHERE Error_Dt >= @FROMDATE AND Error_Dt < DATEADD(DAY,1,@TODATE) AND Encode_By = @User_ID
					AND NOT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM dbo.tbl_Encoding_Dtl D WHERE D.Encode_EPC = EPC AND CAST(D.Encode_Date AS DATE) = CAST(Error_Dt AS DATE)))

		SELECT EPC,Error_Msg,Error_Dt,Error_Time INTO #VIEWERRORCOUNT FROM ErrorData WHERE RN = 1 AND 
		((@ERROR_MSG = 'Already Encoded' AND Error_Msg LIKE 'Scan EAN Tag%') OR (@ERROR_MSG <> 'Already Encoded' AND Error_Msg = @ERROR_MSG))
		AND (@SearchTerm = '' OR EPC LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%' OR Error_Msg LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%'  OR Error_Dt LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%');
		
        SET @SortDirection =
				CASE 
					WHEN LOWER(@SortDirection) = 'desc' THEN 'DESC'
					ELSE 'ASC'
				END;

			SET @OrderBy =	
				CASE @SortColumn
					WHEN 'EPC' THEN 'R.EPC' 
					WHEN 'Error_Msg' THEN 'R.Error_Msg'
					WHEN 'Error_Dt' THEN 'R.Error_Dt' 
					ELSE 'R.Error_Dt'
				END
				+ ' ' + @SortDirection
				+ ', R.Error_Dt';
			
           	SET @SQL = N'SELECT *FROM (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY ' + @OrderBy + N') AS RowNumber,* FROM #VIEWERRORCOUNT R ) T
			WHERE RowNumber BETWEEN @StartRow AND @EndRow
			ORDER BY RowNumber;';

			EXEC sp_executesql	@SQL,N'@StartRow INT, @EndRow INT',
			@StartRow = @StartRow,	@EndRow   = @EndRow;

            SELECT @RecordCount = COUNT(*) FROM #VIEWERRORCOUNT;
			

	        DROP TABLE #VIEWERRORCOUNT;

    END

	ELSE If(@status='EXPORT_VIEW_ERROR_COUNT_REPORT')
    BEGIN	
	   ;WITH ErrorData AS (SELECT EPC,Error_Msg,Error_Dt,Error_Time,Encode_By,ROW_NUMBER() OVER(PARTITION BY EPC, CAST(Error_Dt AS DATE)ORDER BY Error_Dt
                    ) AS RN FROM dbo.tbl_Encoding_Error_Log WHERE Error_Dt >= @FROMDATE AND Error_Dt < DATEADD(DAY,1,@TODATE) AND Encode_By = @User_ID
					AND NOT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM dbo.tbl_Encoding_Dtl D WHERE D.Encode_EPC = EPC AND CAST(D.Encode_Date AS DATE) = CAST(Error_Dt AS DATE)))

		SELECT EPC,Error_Msg,Error_Dt,Error_Time FROM ErrorData WHERE RN = 1 AND 
		((@ERROR_MSG = 'Already Encoded' AND Error_Msg LIKE 'Scan EAN Tag%') OR (@ERROR_MSG <> 'Already Encoded' AND Error_Msg = @ERROR_MSG));
	END


	ELSE If(@status='PARK_HU_REPORT')
    BEGIN	
		SELECT  CAST (Park_Dt AS date) AS 'DATE',HU_No,Main_Article,Article_Code,Act_Qty,Park_Qty AS 'Scanned_Qty', ISNULL(Park_Qty, 0) - ISNULL(Act_Qty, 0) AS Diff_Qty
		INTO #PARKHUREPORT
        FROM dbo.tbl_WH_Val_Box_HU_Park_Dtl P WHERE P.Act_Qty <> p.Park_Qty AND p.Park_Dt >= @fromdate AND  p.Park_Dt < DATEADD(DAY,1,@todate)
		AND P.Park_No = (SELECT MAX(P2.Park_No) FROM dbo.tbl_WH_Val_Box_HU_Park_Dtl P2 WHERE P2.HU_No = P.HU_No)
		AND (@SearchTerm = '' OR Park_Dt LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%' OR HU_No LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%'  OR Article_Code LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%' 
		OR Act_Qty LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%' OR Park_Qty LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%');
		
        SET @SortDirection =
				CASE 
					WHEN LOWER(@SortDirection) = 'desc' THEN 'DESC'
					ELSE 'ASC'
				END;

			SET @OrderBy =	
				CASE @SortColumn
					WHEN 'DATE' THEN 'R.DATE' 
					WHEN 'HU_No' THEN 'R.HU_No'
					WHEN 'Article_Code' THEN 'R.Article_Code' 
					WHEN 'Act_Qty' THEN 'R.Act_Qty'
					WHEN 'Scanned_Qty' THEN 'R.Scanned_Qty'
					WHEN 'Diff_Qty' THEN 'R.Diff_Qty'
					ELSE 'R.DATE'
				END
				+ ' ' + @SortDirection
				+ ', R.DATE';
			
           	SET @SQL = N'SELECT *FROM (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY ' + @OrderBy + N') AS RowNumber,* FROM #PARKHUREPORT R ) T
			WHERE RowNumber BETWEEN @StartRow AND @EndRow
			ORDER BY RowNumber;';

			EXEC sp_executesql	@SQL,N'@StartRow INT, @EndRow INT',
			@StartRow = @StartRow,	@EndRow   = @EndRow;

            SELECT @RecordCount = COUNT(*),@TotalCount=COUNT(DISTINCT HU_No),@ACTUALQTY=SUM(Act_Qty),@SCANQTY=SUM(Scanned_Qty),
			@Excess_Qty = SUM(CASE WHEN Diff_Qty > 0 THEN Diff_Qty ELSE 0 END), 
			@DIFFQTY = SUM(CASE WHEN Diff_Qty < 0 THEN Diff_Qty ELSE 0 END) FROM #PARKHUREPORT;
			
	        DROP TABLE #PARKHUREPORT;

    END

	ELSE If(@status='VIEW_PARK_HU_VENDOR_REPORT')
    BEGIN	
		SELECT  CAST (Park_Dt AS date) AS 'DATE',HU_No as 'HU_NO', Vendor_Code as 'VENDOR_CODE', Vendor_Name as 'VENDOR_NAME', Main_Article as 'MAIN_ARTICLE', Article_Code as 'ARTICLE_NO',
		Article_Description as 'ARTICLE_DESCRIPTION', Act_Qty as 'ACTUAL_QTY',Park_Qty AS 'SCANNED_QTY', ISNULL(Park_Qty, 0) - ISNULL(Act_Qty, 0) AS 'DIFFERENCE_QTY'
		INTO #PARKHUVENDORREPORT
        FROM dbo.tbl_WH_Val_Box_HU_Park_Dtl P  join dbo.tbl_Vendor_Master VM on p.Article_Code=VM.Article_No 
		WHERE P.Act_Qty <> p.Park_Qty AND p.Park_Dt >= @fromdate AND  p.Park_Dt < DATEADD(DAY,1,@todate) and Vendor_Code=@Vendor_Code
		AND P.Park_No = (SELECT MAX(P2.Park_No) FROM dbo.tbl_WH_Val_Box_HU_Park_Dtl P2 WHERE P2.HU_No = P.HU_No)
		AND (@SearchTerm = '' OR Park_Dt LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%' OR HU_No LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%'  OR Article_Code LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%' OR Vendor_Code LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%' 
		OR Vendor_Name LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%'  OR Act_Qty LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%' OR Park_Qty LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%');
				
        SET @SortDirection =
				CASE 
					WHEN LOWER(@SortDirection) = 'desc' THEN 'DESC'
					ELSE 'ASC'
				END;

			SET @OrderBy =	
				CASE @SortColumn
					WHEN 'DATE' THEN 'R.DATE' 
					WHEN 'HU_NO' THEN 'R.HU_No'
					WHEN 'ARTICLE_NO' THEN 'R.Article_Code' 
					WHEN 'ACTUAL_QTY' THEN 'R.Act_Qty'
					WHEN 'SCANNED_QTY' THEN 'R.Scanned_Qty'
					WHEN 'DIFFERENCE_QTY' THEN 'R.Diff_Qty'
					ELSE 'R.DATE'
				END
				+ ' ' + @SortDirection
				+ ', R.DATE';
			
           	SET @SQL = N'SELECT *FROM (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY ' + @OrderBy + N') AS RowNumber,* FROM #PARKHUVENDORREPORT R ) T
			WHERE RowNumber BETWEEN @StartRow AND @EndRow
			ORDER BY RowNumber;';

			EXEC sp_executesql	@SQL,N'@StartRow INT, @EndRow INT',
			@StartRow = @StartRow,	@EndRow   = @EndRow;

            SELECT @RecordCount = COUNT(*),@TotalCount=COUNT(DISTINCT [HU_NO]),@ACTUALQTY=SUM([ACTUAL_QTY]),@SCANQTY=SUM([SCANNED_QTY]),
			@Excess_Qty = SUM(CASE WHEN [DIFFERENCE_QTY] > 0 THEN [DIFFERENCE_QTY] ELSE 0 END), 
			@DIFFQTY = SUM(CASE WHEN [DIFFERENCE_QTY] < 0 THEN [DIFFERENCE_QTY] ELSE 0 END) FROM #PARKHUVENDORREPORT;
			
	        DROP TABLE #PARKHUVENDORREPORT;

    END


	ELSE If(@status='TAG_CLEANING_REPORT')
    BEGIN	
		select cast(Entry_Dt as date) as 'INWARD_DATE', Store_Name as 'STORE_NAME', count(distinct EPC) as 'TOTAL_COUNT' INTO #TAG_CLEANING from tbl_WH_Checkout_Confirmation_Tag_Dtl WHIn inner join tbl_Encoding_Dtl EncDet on WHIn.EPC=EncDet.Encode_EPC
	inner join tbl_Store_Master SM on EncDet.Store_ID=SM.Store_ID where cast(Entry_Dt as date) >= @fromdate and cast(Entry_Dt as date) < DATEADD(DAY,1,@todate) and WHIn.Is_Status='P' 
	and (@SearchTerm = '' OR Store_Name LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%')
	group by cast(WHIn.Entry_Dt as date), Store_Name;		
		
        SET @SortDirection =
				CASE 
					WHEN LOWER(@SortDirection) = 'desc' THEN 'DESC'
					ELSE 'ASC'
				END;

			SET @OrderBy =	
				CASE @SortColumn
					WHEN 'TOTAL_COUNT' THEN 'R.TOTAL_COUNT' 
					WHEN 'STORE_NAME' THEN 'R.STORE_NAME' 
					ELSE 'R.INWARD_DATE'
				END
				+ ' ' + @SortDirection
				+ ', R.INWARD_DATE, R.STORE_NAME';
			
           	SET @SQL = N'SELECT *FROM (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY ' + @OrderBy + N') AS RowNumber,* FROM #TAG_CLEANING R ) T
			WHERE RowNumber BETWEEN @StartRow AND @EndRow
			ORDER BY RowNumber;';

			EXEC sp_executesql	@SQL,N'@StartRow INT, @EndRow INT',
			@StartRow = @StartRow,	@EndRow   = @EndRow;

            SELECT @RecordCount = COUNT(*),@TotalCount=SUM(TOTAL_COUNT) FROM #TAG_CLEANING;
			

	        DROP TABLE #TAG_CLEANING;

    END

	ELSE If(@status='TAG_CLEANING_REPORT_COUNT')
    BEGIN	
		select count(distinct EPC) as 'TOTAL COUNT' from tbl_WH_Checkout_Confirmation_Tag_Dtl WHIn inner join tbl_Encoding_Dtl EncDet on WHIn.EPC=EncDet.Encode_EPC
	inner join tbl_Store_Master SM on EncDet.Store_ID=SM.Store_ID where cast(Entry_Dt as date) >= @fromdate and cast(Entry_Dt as date) < DATEADD(DAY,1,@todate) and WHIn.Is_Status='P';
	END

	ELSE If(@status='EXPORT_TAG_CLEANING_REPORT')
    BEGIN	
		select ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY CAST(WHIn.Entry_Dt AS DATE) ASC, SM.Store_Name) AS 'SR. NO.', cast(Entry_Dt as date) as 'TAG CLEANED DATE', Store_Name as 'STORE NAME (TAG BELONGS TO)', count(distinct EPC) as 'TOTAL COUNT' from tbl_WH_Checkout_Confirmation_Tag_Dtl WHIn inner join tbl_Encoding_Dtl EncDet on WHIn.EPC=EncDet.Encode_EPC
	inner join tbl_Store_Master SM on EncDet.Store_ID=SM.Store_ID where cast(Entry_Dt as date) >= @fromdate and cast(Entry_Dt as date) < DATEADD(DAY,1,@todate) and WHIn.Is_Status='P' 
	group by cast(WHIn.Entry_Dt as date), Store_Name order by cast(WHIn.Entry_Dt as date), Store_Name;
	END


END
